Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Test-time RL framework (NTRL-Code) uses conservative denoising and AST-based aggregation to self-improve code LLMs on unlabeled noisy prompts, yielding consistent robustness gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world user instructions are noisy and expensive to curate paired clean-noisy data for robustness-oriented fine-tuning; need a method that adapts using only unlabeled noisy data during testing.

Method: Conservative self-denoising to obtain a semantic anchor; generate multiple candidate programs; AST-based structural aggregation to produce a proxy target; optimize the model policy on original noisy prompts via reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward (format validity, code similarity, anti-repetition).

Result: On three benchmarks with character-, word-, and paragraph-level perturbations, NTRL-Code shows consistent robustness improvements and prediction stabilization across several base models; code released on GitHub.

Conclusion: NTRL-Code enables robust self-evolution of code LLMs at test time using only unlabeled noisy prompts, producing more stable and robust program predictions across perturbation types.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across various code-related tasks. However, unlike carefully curated datasets that are typically high-quality and error-free, real-world user instructions are often vague and error-prone, posing significant challenges to the robustness of code LLMs. Furthermore, robustness-oriented fine-tuning relies on paired clean-noisy samples, which are costly to curate and require sophisticated noisy simulation techniques. To address these challenges, we propose the Noisy Test-time Reinforcement Learning framework (NTRL-Code), which enables robust self-evolution of code LLMs using only unlabeled noisy data during the testing stage. Specifically, NTRL-Code uses conservative self-denoising to obtain a cleaner semantic anchor for target estimation, and employs an abstract-syntax-tree (AST)-based structural aggregation mechanism to estimate a proxy target from multiple candidate programs. The policy is then optimized on the original noisy prompts with a hybrid reward that combines format validity, code similarity, and anti-repetition signals. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks, each incorporating character-level, word-level, and paragraph-level perturbations, demonstrate that NTRL-Code yields robust and consistent improvements, stabilizing the predictions of various base models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xikai97/NTRL-Code.

</details>


### [35] [AI Harness: Certification under Proposal-Conditioned Information for Foundation-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32184)
*Hailin Zhong,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Theoretical and experimental characterization of when preserving proposal-history correlations is essential for safety certification of foundation-model agents; gives a precise ‘‘common-action’’ criterion and shows both loss and possible recovery of robustness depending on proposal observability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deployed foundation-model agents emit semantic proposals that serve both as action candidates and as runtime observations; system designers often collapse these into state-only action envelopes for simplicity or certification—this work asks when such a collapse preserves the ability to certify safe operation under robust interventions.

Method: Formal analysis of a finite robust interface model: define viability kernels for history-augmented and collapsed models, prove containment relationships, derive necessary and sufficient ‘‘common-action’’ condition for lossless collapse, provide a dual result about observability of proposals restoring feasibility, extend results temporally using exact finite belief updates and standard safety/reachability fixed points, and validate predictions with controlled model-in-the-loop experiments.

Result: The paper shows (1) collapsing proposals into state-only envelopes can maximally shrink the verified viable set even with small alphabets; (2) collapse is lossless iff every proposal-conditioned collapsed fibre admits a common robust-safe intervention; (3) observing proposals can recover feasibility when they disambiguate latent modes needing different interventions; (4) temporal extension separates indefinite operational viability from finite verified progress; (5) empirical tests reproduce the theoretical obstructions under restricted telemetry or intervention authority.

Conclusion: Proposal–history correlation at the model–tool boundary is necessary for certification in general; collapsing history-conditioned proposals into a state-only action envelope can preserve action coverage but may destroy robust certifiability unless a common robust-safe intervention exists across collapsed fibres.

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are often modeled as policies over an observed state. In deployed systems, however, a runtime may intervene only after the model has emitted a semantic proposal, making the proposal both an action candidate and a decision-time observation generated by a history-conditioned process. We show that collapsing this structure into a state-only proposal envelope can preserve proposal coverage while destroying certifiability. In a finite robust interface, the viability kernel of the collapsed model is contained in the physical projection of the history-augmented kernel, and the collapse is lossless exactly when every proposal-conditioned collapsed fiber retains a common robust-safe intervention. This gap can be maximal even with constant-size proposal and history alphabets. The same common-action condition yields a dual result: observing the current proposal can restore robust feasibility when it separates latent modes requiring incompatible interventions. We extend these one-step results over time using exact finite beliefs and standard safety and reachability fixed points, separating indefinite operational viability from finite worst-case verified progress. Controlled model-in-the-loop tests reproduce the predicted obstructions when telemetry or effect verification is removed or intervention authority is restricted. Thus, our contribution is not a new fixed-point calculus, but a characterization of when proposal--history correlation at the model--tool boundary is necessary for certification.

</details>


### [36] [CoMemBench: Benchmarking Collaborative Memory Boundaries across Multi-Agent Workflow Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32192)
*Sen Zhao,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Xinyu He,Ding Zou,Xu Zhang,Qinghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向工作流拓扑的“协作记忆边界”概念并发布CoMemBench（800个工作流、四领域、可验证移交与隔离挑战），展示了广上下文提高可用性但可能破坏隔离的权衡，系统性能随拓扑与违规模式显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准侧重保留/检索，多代理基准关注协同与端到端完成，但缺乏对由工作流拓扑决定的任务条件记忆边界（即哪些信息应共享、何时失效）的系统化测量，故提出CoMemBench填补该空白。

Method: 从源端依赖图构造800个跨四个领域的复合工作流；为每个节点提供本地规格和可验证的工件移交；设计原生评估器与匹配的隔离挑战；度量包括工作流完成率、节点进度验证、必需移交可靠性、隔离鲁棒性与token成本；通过实验比较多种系统在不同拓扑与违规模式下的表现。

Result: CoMemBench成功构建了大规模、执行落地的测试集并揭示共享与隔离之间的显著权衡；实验证明系统排名受拓扑与工件违规影响，提示需要在设计中权衡可用性与隔离性。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了CoMemBench，一个面向多代理工作流中协作记忆共享与隔离的执行驱动基准，表明存在共享-隔离权衡：更广的上下文提升可用性但削弱隔离，且不同系统在不同拓扑和工件违规条件下排名会变化。

Abstract: Multi-agent workflows require task-relevant information to be shared across agents, while irrelevant, stale, unverified, or incompatible information must remain isolated. We call this task-conditioned scope of information a collaborative memory boundary. Workflow topology determines which intermediate artifacts are applicable to which downstream workers and when they cease to be valid, thereby providing a structural stress dimension for sharing and isolation. Existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate retention and retrieval, whereas multi-agent benchmarks emphasize coordination and end-to-end completion, leaving topology-conditioned memory boundaries largely unmeasured. We introduce CoMemBench, an execution-grounded benchmark for collaborative memory sharing and isolation across multi-agent workflow topologies. It constructs 800 composite workflows across four domains from source-grounded dependency graphs, with node-local specifications, verifiable artifact handoffs, native evaluators, and matched isolation challenges. CoMemBench measures workflow completion, verified node progress, required-handoff reliability, isolation robustness, and token cost. Experiments reveal a sharing-isolation trade-off: broader context improves information availability but can weaken isolation, while system rankings shift across topologies and artifact violations.

</details>


### [37] [Instruct, Not Answer: Using Instruction Privileges in On-Policy Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32201)
*Hantao Yu,Sandy Han,Udaya Ghai,Ferhat Erata,Joe Lilien,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用面向常见错误的短指令作为OPCD的privilege，比实例级gold更能提升OOD泛化且不牺牲域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现实例专属的gold信息会导致训练时过度依赖、削弱OOD泛化；为提高可迁移性和稳健性，探索信息含量更低却更普适的privilege策略。

Method: 在On-Policy Context Distillation框架中，教师模型以privileged信息为条件，训练目标为在学生生成的token上最小化教师与学生输出的KL散度。作者将privilege从实例级gold答案/示例替换为针对训练样本中学生常见错误的通用指令（例如格式化指令），在多数据集（ProverQA、ProofWriter、ProntoQA）和多模型（Qwen3-Thinking、Olmo3-Thinking）上进行对比实验。

Result: 在8组实验中有7组显示matched instruction在OOD准确率上比gold高4–17个百分点，同时在域内表现与gold持平。每条指令仅数句，统一应用于所有训练样本，具有更强的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 在OPCD设置下，统一的、针对学生常见错误设计的简短通用指令（matched instruction）作为privilege，比实例级的gold信息在OOD泛化上更有利，同时在域内性能保持接近。

Abstract: On-Policy Context Distillation (OPCD) has recently emerged as a powerful technique for transferring context to student models and for self-improvement. In OPCD, the teacher is conditioned on privileged information, and the goal is to minimize the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between the privileged teacher and the student, evaluated on student-generated tokens. Many existing studies show that using instance-specific gold answers or gold demonstrations as the default privilege can hurt training performance, especially out-of-distribution (OOD). In this work, we instead design general instructions that target common student mistakes observed on the training samples, and show that such simple instructions can outperform gold as the OPCD privilege. In autoformalization tasks, using a matched formatting instruction as the privilege could outperform gold in OOD accuracy by a large margin. In 7 out of 8 experiments using ProverQA, ProofWriter, and ProntoQA as datasets, and Qwen3-Thinking and Olmo3-Thinking families as models, matched instruction privileges outperform gold in OOD by 4 to 17 points, while remaining on par with gold in-domain. Each instruction is only a few sentences (and thus contains much less information compared to all instance-specific gold) and is applied uniformly to every training sample. These results indicate that a general instruction, which applies equally to source and target domain examples, can be substantially more transferable than instance-specific gold in OPCD while maintaining in-domain performance.

</details>


### [38] [Witness: Discovery, Deciphering, and Epiphany in Interactive Puzzle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32208)
*Guanghan Ning,Ping Liu,Linyi Li,Huangjie Zheng,Arjun Neervannan,Huu Nguyen,Michael Sklar,Deniz Zorlu,Nicolai Ouporov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WITNESS reveals that modern LLMs struggle to infer hidden rules; access to rules or targeted RL helps, but substantial gaps remain, indicating rule learning and execution are key research directions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To diagnose whether and how contemporary language models can discover and exploit hidden environment rules by interaction, and whether reinforcement learning helps on rules held out from training.

Method: Introduce WITNESS, a 2D grid environment with controllable, ground-truth ASCII observations; build an agentic pipeline that generates games and two suites: WitnessGym for RL training and WitnessBench for validation/test (including held-out compositions and unseen primitives); evaluate 18 models under a shared harness, run ablations (e.g., provide ground-truth rules), and apply RL to measure transfer to external discovery benchmarks.

Result: Best model solved only 24% of private test level slots. Providing ground-truth rules dramatically improved Opus-5’s RHAE-L5 (59.9→97.8) but barely helped a 27B open model (+2.1). RL on WitnessGym raised the 27B model’s private test RHAE-L5 from 2.1 to 5.4 and produced a mean +4.1 point gain on four external benchmarks. Performance is sensitive to observation interface and agent setup.

Conclusion: Rule acquisition is the primary bottleneck for current frontier language models on interactive, hidden-rule puzzles; RL training improves execution and transfers modestly, but even large models remain limited without explicit access to rules.

Abstract: Automated science needs agents that can work out the rules of an unfamiliar environment by interacting with it. Interactive rule-discovery puzzles offer a controlled setting for studying this ability: an agent infers hidden rules through experimentation and uses what it has inferred to reach a stated goal. We ask what limits current language models on these puzzles and whether reinforcement learning (RL) improves performance on rules held out from training. To study both, we introduce WITNESS, a 2D grid-based puzzle environment with ground-truth ASCII observations and controlled access to rules. An agentic pipeline generates games for WitnessGym, the RL training suite, and WitnessBench, comprising public validation and private test games. The validation set separately tests new compositions of trained rule primitives and primitives absent from training. Under a shared harness, the best of 18 frontier proprietary and open-weight models solves only 24\% of private test level slots, with scores sensitive to the observation interface and agent configuration. Providing ground-truth rules raises Opus-5's validation RHAE-L5 (relative human action efficiency over the first five levels) from 59.9 to 97.8, whereas a 27B open-weight model gains only 2.1 points and remains limited even with the rules provided. RL on WitnessGym raises the 27B model's private test RHAE-L5 from 2.1 to 5.4 and yields a mean gain of 4.1 points on four external discovery benchmarks. Together, these results point to rule acquisition as a major difficulty for frontier models like Opus-5 while smaller models further struggle on rule-based execution, and indicate that RL on hidden-rule puzzles transfers to broader rules and real-world tasks beyond training. Benchmark is available at: https://witnessbench.ai

</details>


### [39] [Fracast-0: Fractal Weight Sharing for a Time Series Foundation Model with Only 85K Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32209)
*Tianxiang Zhan,Huanyao Zhang,Yuanpeng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fracast-0 用参数无关的季节检测与在几何膨胀阶梯上复用模块的设计，实现了仅85k参数的概率性多尺度时间序列基础模型，在多域无微调评估中取得了良好的参数—精度折中，支持跨尺度权重重用作为模型压缩的实际途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型需要兼顾多域广度、概率预测与多时间尺度表示，但为每个尺度设定独立表示会导致参数数目线性增长，因此探索跨尺度权重重用以压缩模型。

Method: 提出 Fracast-0：①参数无关的季节性检测器提取显著周期结构；②编码器在几何膨胀(dilation)阶梯上复用同一局部块并加入尺度条件；③解码器将上下文状态与显式的季节性未来状态融合，并在另一阶梯上复用第二个块，最终预测九个分位数；跨六个语料库进行预训练，模型仅有85,001参数。

Result: 在97个GIFT-Eval配置上（无针对数据集的微调），Fracast-0是28个检查点中参数最少的，在参数-准确率聚合平面上处于非支配位置（MASE=0.808，WQL=0.564）；相比 TinyCast，参数减少42.0%，而MASE和WQL分别仅劣4.2%和3.3%。

Conclusion: 跨尺度权重重用能在保持多域、概率输出与多时间尺度能力的同时显著压缩参数量；Fracast-0 展示了这一方向的可行性和效率。

Abstract: Time series foundation models must preserve multi-domain breadth, probabilistic output, and multiple temporal scales, but parameter count grows when each scale receives a separate representation. We introduce Fracast-0, a probabilistic forecasting foundation model that exploits temporal self-similarity to reuse one operator across scales. A parameter-free detector extracts significant seasonal structure. The encoder applies a shared local block along a geometric dilation ladder with scale conditioning, while the decoder combines context-gathered states with an explicit seasonal future state and reuses a second block along another ladder before emitting nine quantiles. Pretraining across six corpora preserves multi-domain breadth within 85,001 parameters. On 97 GIFT-Eval configurations without per-dataset fine-tuning, Fracast-0 is the smallest of 28 evaluated checkpoints and remains non-dominated in the aggregate parameter-accuracy plane with MASE 0.808 and WQL 0.564. It uses 42.0% fewer parameters than TinyCast, whose MASE and WQL are 4.2% and 3.3% lower. These results support cross-scale weight reuse as a practical route to further time series foundation model compression.

</details>


### [40] [A bilingual AI audiologist built through rubric-guided playbook induction outperforms human audiologists in a blinded evaluation of simulated cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32220)
*Linkai Li,Changgeng Mo,Hanlin Yu,Congxi Lu,Shangqiguo Wang,Matthew B Fitzgerald,Shan X Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过rubric引导的playbook与检索增强的LLM结合，在低数据条件下可构建出在模拟评估中显著优于临床听力师的双语AI听力咨询系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床听力咨询需要结构化病史采集、听力图解读与以患者为中心的沟通，但真实病例材料稀缺。目标是在低数据医疗领域实践可行的专科咨询代理，减少对大规模标注或微调的需求。

Method: 使用未微调的通用大语言模型，结合21项评分量表指导的playbook诱导、对听力图的多模态解析及检索增强的基底信息检索。基于73个训练病例（43英文、30中文）和AI患者模拟器归纳出19条咨询策略规则；在58个独立模拟病例（30中文、28英文）上，与17名执业听力师进行来源盲检比较并做成分消融分析。

Result: AI在所有58例中均优于人类（58/58）；在5分制综合评分上平均配对差值+1.35，Cohen's d=1.84，P=4.5×10^-20；在21项量表中赢得20项，并在中英文双语表现良好。消融分析显示playbook对性能贡献最大。

Conclusion: 该论文表明，在有限的训练病例下，通过将通用大语言模型与基于评分量表的“playbook”引导、多模态听力图解析和检索增强的证据支持结合，能够构建出一个双语AI听力师，并在模拟病例的盲测比较中显著优于17名临床听力师。

Abstract: Audiology consultation requires structured history-taking, audiometric interpretation and patient-centred communication, yet real-world case material is scarce. We present a bilingual AI audiologist pairing a general-purpose large language model with rubric-guided playbook induction, multimodal audiogram interpretation and retrieval-augmented grounding, without fine-tuning the language-model backbone. Using a 21-item rubric and an AI patient simulator, we induced a 19-rule consultation policy from 73 training cases (43 English, 30 Chinese) and evaluated the system on 58 independent simulated cases (30 Chinese, 28 English) in a pre-specified, source-blinded comparison with 17 practising audiologists. The AI audiologist outperformed human audiologists on every case (58/58; mean paired $Δ$ = +1.35 on a 5-point composite, Cohen's d = 1.84, $P = 4.5 \times 10^{-20}$), on 20 of 21 rubric items and in both languages. Component ablation identified the playbook as the largest contributor, offering a practical route to specialist consultation agents in low-data medical domains.

</details>


### [41] [RAO-Nav: Probing Omni-Language Models for Zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32224)
*Qilang Ye,Meng Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RAO-Nav与测试时LNR模块，展示了在零样本下利用OML实现有竞争力的语义视听导航，并引入新的评测场景；但需要更多细节（例如消融、计算开销、基线比较与泛化测试）来验证结论的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前专用、多任务训练的模型仍难以实现真正的通用多模态导航，研究者希望直接利用日益强大的Omni-Language Models的隐式多模态知识，实现无需任务特定训练的零样本语义视听导航（SAVN）。

Method: 提出RAO-Nav部署流水线，利用现有OML的隐式音视频知识让智能体在环境中执行‘听-看-推理-行动’循环；在模型解码阶段插入测试时Latent Navigation Reasoning（LNR）模块，促使模型检索更相关的观测并输出更有效的导航决策。

Result: 在公开SAVN基准上，RAO-Nav在零样本设置下超过了现有SOTA基线，并提出了新的Global Navigation Instruction评测设置以检验OMLs作为具身导航代理的能力。论文同时公开了代码仓库。

Conclusion: RAO-Nav显示OMLs可以在零样本条件下以推理式部署实现有竞争力的语义视听导航，提出的测试时潜在导航推理（LNR）模块能在解码空间中提升目标相关观测的检索并改进导航决策。

Abstract: We explore whether Omni-Language Models (OLMs) can be directly applied to zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation (SAVN). Recent work demonstrates that even state-of-the-art specialized models still struggle to achieve generalist multimodal navigation, despite extensive task-specific training. In this paper, we introduce RAO-Nav, short for Reasoning All-in-One OLM, a deployment pipeline for zero-shot SAVN. By leveraging the rich implicit audio-visual knowledge encoded in OLMs, the embodied agent is enabled to ``hear'', ``see'', ``reason'', and ``act'' in the environment. To further elicit the built-in thinking ability of OLMs, we propose a test-time Latent Navigation Reasoning (LNR) module that can be seamlessly integrated into the decoding space. LNR encourages the model to retrieve more target-relevant observations and make effective navigation decisions. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that our framework surpasses existing state-of-the-art baselines on public SAVN benchmarks without using any training data. Moreover, we introduce a new \emph{Global Navigation Instruction} setting to further evaluate the ability of OLMs to serve as embodied navigation agents. Code: https://github.com/rikeilong/OmniAV\_Nav.

</details>


### [42] [Certifying Interventional Agreement Among Observationally Equivalent Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32247)
*Sourena Khanzadeh,Daniel Platnick,Marjan Alirezaie,Hossein Rahnama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ISS 是一种通过在真实系统上选择能区分候选模型的许可干预并淘汰不符模型来生成可检验介入一致性证书的算法；用MILP处理连续变量的无限版本空间；在彩色MNIST因果抽象审计中显示出可行性与局限性（平均13.6次干预得到证书，但存在被绕过单元导致的虚假证书风险）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察等价的因果模型在外推到未观测的干预时可能行为不同，观测数据永远无法保证模型在干预情形下的正确性；需要一种能用有限代价通过干预对模型的介入行为做可检验认证的方法。

Method: 提出了一种交互式筛选算法——Interventional Separation Selection (ISS)：重复选取对当前候选集合有分歧的“许可干预”去询问真实系统，丢弃与观测结果矛盾的候选模型，直到不存在在代价上界内可分离幸存者的干预为止。对连续变量的无限版本空间，用混合整数线性规划(MILP)在容差范围内精确判定停止条件。实验在三位彩色MNIST因果抽象任务中验证。

Result: 理论上：若真实系统在候选集中，ISS 停止时可证实幸存模型在所有许可干预（代价约束内）上与真实系统一致；该停止条件仅取决于候选集合可检验。实证上：在三位彩色MNIST任务上，普通卷积网络对训练集达到零误差，但在单个数字编辑上的形状标签与网络预测有26%的不一致；ISS 平均每张图像用13.6次干预给出证书；当网络绕过某一候选普遍依赖的单元时，覆盖该单元的证书可能被静默作废，随机的20次验证干预能推翻其中69%的证书。

Conclusion: ISS 能在有限干预预算下为候选因果模型集合提供可检验的介入一致性证书：如果真实系统在候选集中，则幸存模型在所有许可的介入（在代价界限内）上与真实系统一致；该证书的停止条件仅依赖幸存者并可在无须知晓真实系统的情况下检查。

Abstract: Observationally equivalent causal models can still disagree about what happens under intervention, because interventions create inputs that never occur in observational data. We introduce Interventional Separation Selection (ISS), which repeatedly queries the true system with an admissible intervention on which the surviving candidate models disagree, discards the candidates the outcome contradicts, and stops once no intervention within a cost bound separates the survivors. If the true system is among the candidates, this stopping condition certifies that every survivor agrees with it on every admissible intervention within the bound, a guarantee that no observational learner can give, however much data it sees. The stopping condition depends only on the survivors, so it can be checked without knowing the truth. For continuous variables the candidates form an infinite version space, and mixed-integer linear programs decide the stopping condition exactly over all of it, with agreement holding up to a tolerance. On a three-digit colored MNIST causal abstraction task in which ink hue tracks digit size, plain convolutional networks trained on examples reach zero held-out error, yet disagree with shape-based labels on 26% of single-digit edits, as often as hue-based labels do. Auditing the causal abstractions of networks observed only on such images, ISS certifies what each network perceives with 13.6 interventions per image on average, and each certificate, checked against every admissible intervention, holds whenever the network's true abstraction is among the candidates. When a network bypasses a unit that every candidate abstraction relies on, certificates covering interventions on that unit can be silently void, and twenty random validation interventions refute 69% of them.

</details>


### [43] [Why Directly Learning Periodic Trajectories Can Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32254)
*Kaixin Zheng,Anita Layton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 问题：周期解的相位随参数剧烈变化导致学习困难。贡献：分析误差机理，提出“对齐波形+分离周期”表示并证明其规整性，实验证明在 ODE/PDE 中均能改善泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算子学习中如何记录和表示周期解：保守地长时间积分并记录至少一个周期的物理时间窗口会产生难学的数据，即使周期轨线随参数规整变化，未对齐或固定物理窗表示导致泛化差。

Method: 理论分析相位差随频率差累积导致的误差下界（包括总体 MSE 下界和固定窗口下的每样本下界）；提出简单表示：先对齐并归一化波形，再单独回归周期；证明在 ODE 假设下该分解的正则性；用数值实验（ODE 与 PDE）验证方法性能。

Result: 发现未对齐轨迹在训练分布内仍泛化差；相位对齐可改善但固定物理时间窗口仍在训练区间外对不同周期产生大误差；通过分解表示能避免两类失败，理论与实验均支持该结论。

Conclusion: 将周期轨迹表示为相位对齐并归一化的波形与其周期分开学习，可以避免因相位随参数快速变化导致的误差下界（population MSE floor），从而显著改善算子学习的泛化能力。作者在常微分方程假设下证明了分解目标的规整性，并在 ODE 和一个 PDE 实验中证实了方法有效性。

Abstract: Operator learning of periodic solutions requires deciding how simulation data should be recorded and represented. A natural choice is to integrate long enough for transients to decay and record a window wide enough to contain at least one full period of all trajectories. We find that these conservative choices can make the resulting trajectories difficult to learn, even when the underlying periodic orbits vary regularly with system parameters. Unaligned trajectories generalize poorly even within the training distribution. Phase alignment substantially improves in-distribution generalization, but models trained on a fixed physical-time window still have large errors on trajectories with periods outside the training range. We explain both failures through a common mechanism: frequency differences accumulate over time, so the target phase varies rapidly with the parameters. Predictors that cannot track this variation incur a population MSE floor in both settings; for fixed window prediction, we also derive a per-sample lower bound. We then study one of the simplest representations that escape these floors: learning an aligned, normalized waveform and its period separately. We establish regularity of the decoupled targets under ODE assumptions and show experimentally that this approach avoids both failures in ODE systems and a PDE case study.

</details>


### [44] [Clarify the User or Verify the World? Uncertainty Routing for Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32255)
*Zhaofeng Li,Xuan Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出通过分解不确定性并按源路由查询（CLARIFY/VERIFY）来改进主动LLM代理决策的PROUR，能更高效地获取信息并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动查询方法通常專注于用户澄清或环境验证中的一类，缺乏显式的源选择机制；实际代理需要既判断是否查询又判断向哪个来源查询以高效解决不确定性。

Method: 将动作不确定性分解为两类信号：不同可能用户意图间的分歧（指示用户侧模糊性）和每种意图内部剩余的熵（指示世界侧证据缺失）。基于此路由决策，训练一个条件模式的查询生成器，使用以信息增益为目标的奖励：在CLARIFY模式下以识别用户目标为目标，在VERIFY模式下以识别下一步行动为目标。

Result: 在τ-bench（零售与航空）上实现28.17%的平均成功率，较最强先行方法高4.57个百分点，同时交互步骤平均减少2.17步；所学策略在无需重训练下迁移到更强任务代理与τ^3-bench事务域，显示出源对齐不确定性解析的实用价值。

Conclusion: 提出了PROUR框架，通过源对齐的不确定性路由在ACT、CLARIFY、VERIFY三种动作之间选择信息获取路径，从而提升工具使用型LLM代理的主动决策能力，实验证明在标准基准上优于既有方法并具备一定的泛化性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents must decide not only whether additional information is needed, but also which source can resolve the uncertainty. Existing proactive approaches often specialize in either user clarification or environment verification, without explicitly determining the appropriate information source for each decision. We formulate this problem as uncertainty routing among ACT, CLARIFY, and VERIFY, and propose PROUR, a proactive uncertainty routing framework. PROUR decomposes action uncertainty into disagreement across plausible user-goal interpretations, which signals user-side ambiguity, and the entropy remaining within each interpretation, which signals missing world-side evidence. To acquire information from the routed source, a query generator is trained with a mode-conditioned information-gain reward, targeting user-goal identification under CLARIFY and next-action identification under VERIFY. On $τ$-bench, PROUR achieves 28.17% average success rate across retail and airline, outperforming the strongest prior method by 4.57% while using 2.17 fewer interaction steps. The learned policy further generalizes to stronger task agents and transactional domains of $τ^3$-bench without retraining, demonstrating the benefit of source-aligned uncertainty resolution for proactive agents.

</details>


### [45] [LAM: Efficient Lossy Agent Memory Framework With A Retrieval-Score Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32256)
*Baixi Sun,Le Chen,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Xiaolong Ma,Chih-Hsuan Yang,Mingze Xia,Syed Zawad,Sheng Di,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Huihuo Zheng,Rajeev Thakur,Venkatram Vishwanath,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAM 用确定性去重+前缀保留的并行压缩+部署前性能建模，实现了小比例 token 删除下几乎无损的关键证据保留，并通过在 prefill 前移除记录而获得数十倍的端到端加速；限制造成的是只给出分数扰动界限而非排名证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理在读取输入、推理和调用工具时记忆线性增长，导致上下文窗口被耗尽并增加推理成本。基于 LLM 的摘要会带来额外延迟且无法给出信息丢失的显式界限，因而需要一种可控、有界信息损失且能带来实际加速的记忆压缩方案。

Method: 三个组成部分：1) 确定性去重规则，提供对检索分数扰动的替代界限（substitution bound），但不保证排名不变；2) 记忆管理器保留缓存前缀并将压缩与推理重叠，以减少延迟；3) 性能模型在部署前估算不同压缩策略的代价并预测加速效果。评估基于 600 条代理轨迹。

Result: LAM 删除了 22.47% 的观测 token，同时保留了 99.984% 测量到的 gold-patch 证据。在固定删除集合下，性能模型预测在 prefill 前移除记录而不是从已填充上下文中删除，会带来 71.4x–91.6x 的端到端加速。该加速来源于调度而非规则，对于任何保前缀测试均适用。

Conclusion: LAM 提出了一种可控的有损代理记忆系统，通过确定性去重规则（带对检索分数的替代界限）、保留缓存前缀并与推理并行压缩的记忆管理器，以及部署前估算压缩代价的性能模型，实现了在大幅提高运行效率的同时对关键信息的高留存率。调度（在 prefill 前移除记录）而非去重规则本身带来了主要的端到端加速。

Abstract: Agent memory grows as agents read inputs, reason, and call tools. Longer histories increase inference cost and eventually exceed the context window. LLM-based summarization reduces this history but adds latency and provides no explicit bound on information loss. We propose LAM, a Lossy Agent Memory system with three components: a deterministic deduplication rule with a substitution bound on retrieval scores - a bound on score perturbation, not a certificate of unchanged ranking; a memory manager that preserves the cached prefix and overlaps compaction with inference; and a performance model that estimates compaction costs before deployment. On 600 agent trajectories, LAM removes 22.47% of observation tokens while retaining 99.984% of the measured gold-patch evidence. At a fixed deletion set, the performance model predicts a 71.4x-91.6x end-to-end speedup from removing records before prefill instead of deleting them from a prefilled context. That benefit comes from the schedule rather than the rule and applies to any prefix-preserving test.

</details>


### [46] [VPEvolve: A Self-Evolving Virtual Process Engineer for Computational Lithography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32473)
*Tianyi Li,Wenxuan Dong,Donger Luo,Nan Wang,Yanpeng Chen,Jiaqi Liu,Xinyun Zhang,Hao Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A frozen-LLM-driven Virtual Process Engineer plus an evidence-linked Skill Bank iteratively edits OPC recipes and, when tested on a FreePDK45 benchmark, substantially lowers maximum edge-placement error while satisfying quality constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPC recipes become large and brittle as engineers add local repair rules; interactions between corrections are complex and commercial-tool trial lessons are scattered across logs and code. The system aims to centralize measured engineering experience, enable closed-loop, evidence-backed recipe edits, and automate iterative improvement without retraining LLM weights.

Method: A Virtual Process Engineer harness wraps a frozen LLM with access to process manuals, layout analysis, recipe editing, and interfaces to commercial-tool evaluations. An actor proposes global-parameter changes, local rule edits, or diagnostic trials. After each measured evaluation, an LLM reflector and curator convert the measured responses into evidence-linked judgments that the actor retrieves for subsequent trials. Feasible edits update the retained recipe while all measured trials populate the Skill Bank that guides future edits; model weights remain fixed throughout.

Result: On a FreePDK45-derived benchmark with ten commercial-tool evaluations per case, the system reduced mean per-case maximum edge placement error from 18.294 nm to 5.361 nm on Poly and from 22.052 nm to 15.692 nm on Metal1. Every final recipe satisfied predefined quality constraints and improved maximum error by at least 0.1 nm. The evaluation used ten evaluations per case and retained frozen model weights.

Conclusion: \system can automatically improve OPC recipes by iteratively proposing edits, running measured evaluations with commercial tools, and ingesting those outcomes into a Skill Bank; with frozen LLM weights it produces feasible recipe updates that reduce edge-placement errors and meet predefined quality constraints.

Abstract: Optical proximity correction (OPC) recipes grow as engineers add local rules to repair newly discovered lithography hotspots. Each correction can interact with existing rules, while lessons from commercial-tool trials remain scattered across code and logs. \system combines a Virtual Process Engineer (VPE) harness with a Skill Bank of measured engineering experience. The harness equips a frozen language model with process manuals, layout analysis, recipe editing, and commercial-tool evaluation. The actor proposes changes to the global parameters, local targeted rules, or diagnostic trials. After each evaluation, an LLM reflector and curator turn the measured response into evidence-linked judgments. The actor retrieves them before its next trial. Feasible improvements update the retained recipe; every measured trial informs the Skill Bank that guides the next edit. The model weights remain fixed. On a FreePDK45-derived benchmark with ten commercial-tool evaluations per case, \system reduces the mean per-case maximum edge placement error from 18.294 to 5.361 nm on Poly and from 22.052 to 15.692 nm on Metal1. Every final recipe satisfies the predefined quality constraints and improves the maximum error by at least 0.1 nm.

</details>


### [47] [Prefill-Free Cross-Family KV Cache Transfer for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32259)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Haneul Yoo,Sungjoo Yoo,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Murali Annavaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeteroFold maps and calibrates KV caches between frozen heterogeneous models to enable fast, accurate, prefill-free cross-family KV reuse.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In multi-agent LLM systems, text-based context sharing forces receivers to re-prefill already-processed context; reusing KV caches avoids redundancy but cross-family transfers are hard due to architectural and tokenization differences.

Method: Aligns model structures, maps sender KV cache into the receiver latent space, and applies calibration to preserve the receiver's behavior—handling tokenization, depth, and KV representation mismatches without fine-tuning receiver weights.

Result: Across six transfer directions, HeteroFold attains top cache-transfer performance on four long-context benchmarks and most short-context settings; on a 32K context transfer (Llama-3.1-8B→Ministral-3-14B) it is 10.7× faster than native prefill and 1.18–1.47× faster than state-of-the-art prefill-free baselines (Dense Latent, KV Ridge), while matching multi-agent text-based communication performance.

Conclusion: HeteroFold enables efficient prefill-free transfer of KV caches across heterogeneous model families while keeping both sender and receiver frozen, achieving best or near-best transfer quality on long- and short-context benchmarks and matching text-based communication in multi-agent settings.

Abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly combine heterogeneous models for specialized agent roles. However, text-based communication requires each receiver to prefill shared context already processed by the sender. Reusing the sender's key-value (KV) cache avoids this redundancy, but prefill-free transfer across model families must handle differences in tokenization, model depth, and KV representations. To address these issues, we propose \textit{HeteroFold}, a prefill-free cross-family KV cache transfer method that keeps both the sender and receiver frozen. HeteroFold aligns model structures, maps the sender cache into the receiver space, and calibrates it to preserve receiver behavior. Across six transfer directions, HeteroFold achieves the best cache-transfer performance on all four long-context benchmarks and most short-context settings. It also matches text-based communication on the multi-agent benchmark. At 32K context length, Llama-3.1-8B$\rightarrow$Ministral-3-14B transfer is $10.7\times$ faster than Native Prefill and $1.18$--$1.47\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art prefill-free baselines, Dense Latent and KV Ridge. These results show that HeteroFold enables efficient cross-family KV reuse without receiver prefill.

</details>


### [48] [CUA-SWE: When Computer-Use Agents Meet Visual Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32600)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Chenhao Liang,Zelong Xu,Aojie Yuan,Xiaolin Zhou,Haiyue Zhang,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUA-SWE：一个强调基于视觉反馈与交互的可验证软件工程基准，展示了将运行时界面观测整合进代码修复闭环的必要性与当前代理的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实的软件开发超越仅编辑代码，开发者需要运行软件、交互界面、基于视觉观察诊断并修复错误。现有研究多将代码层代理与桌面/应用使用代理孤立研究，缺乏统一标准去评估代理在包含运行时视觉反馈的真实开发闭环中的能力。

Method: 提出 CUA-SWE：包含基准、交互环境与评测流水线。任务横跨四个软件工程领域，要求代理在同一任务中修改源码与配置、执行命令、与运行中软件进行图形界面交互并基于屏幕截图/视觉反馈判断与验证修改。每项任务提供确定性的任务专属测试，用于验证结果的正确性并保证特定行为被保留。对比并分析前沿代理如何结合源码级执行与应用截图/交互来生成经验证的软件修改。

Result: 构建了一个涵盖多领域的可执行基准，刻画了代理在不同领域与信息可用性条件下的表现，并分析成功修复时的开发行为特征。实验展示了代理如何利用屏幕截图与交互信息来定位与验证修复，同时揭示了现阶段存在的薄弱环节与改进方向。

Conclusion: 视觉反馈与交互在软件工程自动化中至关重要；CUA-SWE 提供了统一的基准与评测管线，使得研究者可系统地研究代理如何把运行时界面观测映射到源码并验证修复。当前前沿代理在部分任务上能成功，但整体仍受限，需改进 GUI 理解与执行-编辑闭环能力。

Abstract: Software development requires more than editing code: developers repeatedly run software, interact with its interfaces, visually inspect its behavior, and use these observations to decide what to change next and whether a change works. Existing coding agents and computer-use agents are largely studied in isolation, leaving this integrated development process underexplored. Diagnosing a runtime interaction failure requires agents to connect visual observations with the responsible code, then use the application again to verify the repair. We introduce CUA-SWE, a benchmark, environment, and evaluation pipeline for software engineering with computer use. Beyond studying how GUI feedback supports diagnosis and repair, we ask whether agents can complete software engineering tasks when required specification or operational information is available only through the running application's visual interface. CUA-SWE spans four software engineering domains and requires agents to modify code and configuration, execute commands, interact with running software, and inspect visual feedback within the same task. Each task includes deterministic, task-specific tests that verify whether the resulting software satisfies the requirements and preserves specified behavior. Our evaluation characterizes how frontier agents combine source-level execution with application screenshots and graphical interaction to produce verified software changes. We examine performance across domains and task information requirements, alongside the development behaviors associated with successful repairs. CUA-SWE provides a unified testbed for studying how agents use visual feedback and interaction to guide software engineering, with executable correctness criteria for the resulting software.

</details>


### [49] [When Does a Skill Add Value? Task-Conditional Gain Prediction for Selective Skill Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32274)
*Anjie Xu,Zhiyu Zhang,Ruiqing Ding,Fengli Xu,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillDelta 用配对执行史学习任务条件化的技能收益并在新任务上做局部迁移；在多项基准上能显著改善技能分配并提升成功率，但依赖历史覆盖与表征匹配，组内挑选价值有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然技能通常被期望提升代理性能，但实际中技能有时无益甚至有害且带来额外代价。目的是在代理实际调用技能前，预测该技能在给定任务上的净增益，从而做出更经济且高效的激活决策。

Method: 提出 SkillDelta：构造“局部预测器”把历史的任务条件化技能增益（paired gains）迁移到新任务，基于显式的迁移假设分析预测误差与决策遗憾受支持覆盖、表征错配和执行噪声的影响；在五个基准、三种代理上用配对历史改进技能分配并评估成功率与排序质量。

Result: 在 15 个设置中，配对历史提升了观测增益排序（相较于仅用技能辅助结果的排序）在 12 个设置上表现更好；在匹配期望技能使用率下，SkillDelta 在全部 15 个设置上优于随机激活，平均绝对收益 4.3%。主要收益来源于按任务组分配技能，组内选择价值在 ToolQA 上最明显，其他数据集较弱。

Conclusion: SkillDelta 在多数评估设置中能可靠地用历史的配对执行记录（有技能 vs 无技能）预测并分配技能，从而在相同技能使用率下普遍优于随机激活，平均绝对成功率提升约 4.3%。然而其效果依赖于配对历史的覆盖度、表征匹配与执行噪声，且对某些任务（组内选择价值）提升有限。

Abstract: Agent skills are expected to improve task performance. Yet we find that they often provide no benefit, and can even hurt performance while incurring additional token costs. Can we predict whether a skill will help before the agent acts? We introduce SkillDelta, a framework for predicting task-conditional skill gains from paired executions of the same agent with and without the skill. A local predictor transfers these historical gains to new tasks without retraining the agent. Under explicit transfer assumptions, our analysis links support coverage, representation mismatch, and execution noise to prediction error and decision regret. Across five benchmarks and three target agents, paired history improves observed-gain ranking over skill-assisted outcomes alone in 12 of 15 settings. At matched expected skill-use rates, SkillDelta improves success over random activation in all 15 settings, with an average absolute gain of 4.3%. Most of this advantage comes from allocation across task groups. Evidence for additional within-group selection value is strongest on ToolQA and weaker elsewhere. Code is available at https://github.com/TankTechnology/skilldelta.

</details>


### [50] [SWE-MILE: Asynchronous Potential-Induced Milestone Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32631)
*Chaoqun Cui,Hao Zhou,Meiqi Chen,Fandong Meng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于导航/验证势的里程碑信用分配与异步影子探测，从运行时自动产生中间监督，显著改善长时序软件工程RL代理的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以终端结果作为监督难以区分有益动作与冗余探索或功能回退，导致稀疏、噪声高的学习信号；需要不依赖额外奖励模型或外部评估器的细粒度、可验证的过程监督来提升长时序SWE智能体的样本效率与稳定性。

Method: 定义两类势函数：导航势（基于任务相关文件的暴露度）与验证势（基于测试/状态对齐度），用势函数差分把里程碑进展或回退归因到具体动作；将这些差分量经折扣向后传播到先前步骤以获得中间过程奖励；为减少验证延迟，引入异步影子探测，在隔离沙箱中重放改动并并行运行验证，从而获得中间验证状态并隐藏验证开销；最终将得到的过程信用与终端优势结合用于强化学习更新。

Result: 在两项代表性的长时序SWE任务上，SWE-MILE 显著提升了代理性能（包括任务成功率与学习效率），表明工作流运行时信号是实用的过程监督来源；异步影子探测有效降低了验证带来的延迟影响。

Conclusion: SWE-MILE 能从工作流运行时提取细粒度的过程监督（里程碑前进和回退），结合折扣向后传播与并行化的影子探测，提升了基于终端结果监督的长时序软件工程智能体的学习效果与任务成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon software engineering (SWE) agents trained with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically receive only terminal outcome supervision, making it difficult to distinguish productive actions from redundant exploration or functional regressions. We propose SWE-MILE, an asynchronous potential-induced milestone credit assignment framework that derives fine-grained process supervision from workflow runtime, without auxiliary reward models or external evaluators. SWE-MILE quantifies task-relevant file exposure and test-state alignment as navigation and verification potentials, respectively. Differences in these potentials attribute milestone progress and regressions to individual actions, while discounted backward credit propagates supervision to preceding steps. To efficiently acquire intermediate verification states, SWE-MILE further introduces asynchronous shadow probing, which replays repository-changing actions in an isolated sandbox and runs verification in parallel with the agent's primary interaction, largely hiding verification latency. The resulting process credit augments terminal outcome advantages and provides informative learning signals. Experiments on two representative long-horizon SWE tasks demonstrate substantial improvements in agent performance, highlighting workflow runtime signals as a practical source of process supervision for long-horizon SWE agents.

</details>


### [51] [GLIDE: Generalized Layer-wise Intrinsic Distributional Evaluation for Heterogeneous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32295)
*Wei Zhu,Yiming Wang,Rui Wang,Lixing Yu,Kun Yue,Zhiwen Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIDE利用层级残差一致性与基于近期分数分布的悲观校准，给出跨智能体可比的内在步级评价与不确定性估计，从而在多类任务中提高性能和资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量步级评价困难：外部验证器增加推理开销；智能体自评或置信度常常失校准，尤其当候选来自异构智能体时，难以进行跨分支比较与计算资源分配。

Method: 从模型每层的残差更新中度量局部更新是否一致支持候选步骤引起的全局残差变化（称为层级残差一致性），将该内在证据按生成智能体近期输出分数分布进行校准并转为悲观化的奖励分数；把该奖励作为跨智能体的价值信号用于MCTS分支选择，同时用归一化的预测不确定性来指导自适应分支扩展。

Result: 在多跳推理、序贯决策和符号逻辑等任务上，GLIDE在不使用外部核验器或任务特定监督的情况下，显著提升了任务成功率、步级排序质量以及计算效率。

Conclusion: GLIDE通过基于层级残差一致性的内在证据与对生成器最近分数分布的校准，提供一种不依赖外部核验器的轻量级、跨智能体可比较的步级评价信号，从而改善MCTS分支选择与自适应扩展，提升任务性能与计算效率。

Abstract: LLM agents require reliable step-level evaluation to compare candidate branches and allocate computation effectively. However, lightweight evaluation remains challenging. External verifiers introduce additional inference cost, while agent-produced confidence or self-evaluation scores can be miscalibrated, especially when candidates are generated by heterogeneous agents. We propose \textbf{G}eneralized \textbf{L}ayer-wise \textbf{I}ntrinsic \textbf{D}istributional \textbf{E}valuation (\textbf{GLIDE}) for LLM agents. \textsc{GLIDE} derives intrinsic step evidence from layer-wise residual coherence, which measures whether local residual updates consistently support the global residual change induced by a candidate step. It calibrates this evidence against the recent score distribution of the generating agent and converts it into a pessimistic reward that jointly accounts for absolute residual evidence and agent-relative standing. The reward provides a cross-agent value signal for MCTS branch selection, while normalized predictive uncertainty guides adaptive branching. Experiments on multi-hop reasoning, sequential decision making, and symbolic logic show that \textsc{GLIDE} improves task performance, step-level ranking quality, and computational efficiency without external verifiers or task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [52] [The Decomposition Tax: LLM Pipelines Lose Up to 40 Accuracy Points at Their Own Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32825)
*Tianqi Bu,YuXuan Peng,Junteng Tu,Henghui Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多阶段LLM流水线会因阶段间丢失原始问题信息而支付显著的"分解税"，通过在丢失接口之后对后续阶段进行重新接地并在需要列出数值时明确要求保留它们之间的关系，可以有效修复性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被逐步构建为多阶段流水线，阶段间接口的设计可能造成性能损失；需要量化这种损失（分解税）并寻找可行的修复方法，从而指导流水线构建者更好地设计阶段接口。

Method: 在保持模型、问题、阶段数量、阶段提示和完成预算不变的条件下，仅改变每个阶段是否能看到原始问题，比较有无原始问题可见时的准确率差值，定义为分解税；在21个开源或开放权重模型上、GSM-Hard和MATH-500两个基准上进行配对实验（每单元n=200），并用Benjamini-Hochberg和Holm校正多重检验；通过替换阶段指令、引入“重接地”以及添加明确保留关系的指令来测试修复策略。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上检测到高达40.5个准确率点的分解税（例如gemma-3-12B在MATH-500上，Holm校正p=1.66e-19）；大量检验在多重比对校正后仍显著；重写阶段指令和在列量阶段加入“保留关系”能显著降低税额；重接地位置很关键，应放在丢信息接口之后；该修复在新模型上（如gemma-4-12B）也有效；已注册的更强预测规则被封闭的测试集否定。

Conclusion: 分解税（decomposition tax）是影响多阶段LLM流水线性能的主要因素；通过在丢失信息的接口之后对阶段进行重新接地（re-grounding）以及在列出数值时要求保留它们之间的关系，可以显著降低或修复性能损失。

Abstract: A four-stage LLM pipeline gives up as much as 40.5 accuracy points at its own interfaces (gemma-3-12B on MATH-500, Holm-corrected p = 1.66e-19; the largest tax in the primary family). We hold model, problem, stages, stage prompts and completion budget fixed, vary only whether each stage can still see the original problem, and call the accuracy difference the decomposition tax. Across 21 open-weight models from nine organisations, on GSM-Hard and MATH-500 at n = 200 paired items per cell, 70 of 118 primary-family tests survive Benjamini-Hochberg correction and 54 survive Holm. On GSM-Hard, a placebo recovers nothing: it carries at least 60% of the extra tokens and at most one word of the problem. Builders design a pipeline one stage at a time, and its bill arrives at the interfaces between stages. Rewriting one stage's instruction moves gemma-3-12B's tax from 4.5 to 36.5 points, and adding "every relationship stated between them" to a stage that lists the numerical quantities lowers the tax on 9 of 9 models on MATH-500. Re-grounding, which shows a stage the original problem again, belongs after the loss. With one lossy interface, re-grounding the stage after it beats re-grounding the stage before it on 7 of 7 models on both benchmarks; on MATH-500 the earlier repair is worse than none on 7 of 7. Newer models still pay: gemma-4-12B gives up 37.0 points, and the repair holds on all three of the newest models we test. A sealed held-out test refuted a stronger rule we registered, which predicted the paying stage from the interface and receiver types, so we locate the tax by measuring one stage at a time. The prescription has two parts: re-ground the stage after the lossy interface, and if a stage must list the quantities, tell it to keep the relationships.

</details>


### [53] [Agentsensus: Consensus-Compressed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32297)
*Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过事件合并与语义链接的统一长期记忆，Agentsensus显著减少重复存储并实现真实共享与高覆盖的关联，合并机制对效果至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统代理叙事/故事世界中，常为每个角色维护独立的记忆流；但同一共享事件会被为每个目击者重复存储，导致存储冗余、共享知识难以体现以及语义连贯性不足。提出统一记忆以减少冗余并显式建立共享与关联。

Method: 提出一个统一的长期记忆结构：相同事件的多条目合并为一条由所有目击者共同拥有的记录，并将语义相关的记录相互连接。实现上应包含事件对齐/聚类与语义链接模块。在四个世界（两部古典中文小说、《哈姆雷特》、真实冲突时间线）中，按相同粒度协议运行40–80轮，和三种按角色私有记忆设计的基线比较，另有关闭合并的消融实验和不同轮次（10/20/40）重复实验。

Result: Agentsensus在写入量上比最优基线节省22–44%；实现共享记录占比14–28%且部分记录被多达10人共有；语义链接率94–99%；关闭合并时存储量增加3.1倍并彻底丧失共享；在延展轮数（10→20→40）中共享率随着轮数增长而上升（示例从6%→9%→14%）。模拟质量经判定与基线相当或更好。

Conclusion: Agentsensus通过将同一事件的多位目击者记录合并为单一共享条目，并把语义相关的记忆条目建立连接，从根本上减少了多余存储并建立了共享/关联的长期记忆。实验表明在四个故事世界中，它比最接近的基线少写入22–44%的条目，14–28%的记录被多名角色共享（有些被多达10人共享），94–99%的记录被链接，且模拟质量与基线可判别为不差甚至更好。消融实验表明合并机制是关键：关闭合并会使存储膨胀3.1倍，共享量降为零。

Abstract: A agentic story world is a dynamic system simulating who learned what, when, and from whom -- yet the standard design gives each character a private memory stream. A shared event is therefore stored once per witness, large duplication will be incurred in terms of storage. We present Agentsensus, a story-world simulation framework in which there is an unified long-term memory. Records of the same event merge into one owned by all its witnesses, and semantically relevant memory records are linked. We evaluate on four worlds -- two classical Chinese novels, Hamlet, and a real-world conflict timeline -- run for 40 to 80 rounds against three per-character memory designs under an equal-granularity protocol. Agentsensus writes 22-44% fewer entries than the closest baseline and is the only design whose memory becomes shared (14-28% of records held by more than one character, some by 10) and linked (94-99%), at judged simulation quality indistinguishable or even better than the baselines. An ablation attributes this to the merge itself: disabling it multiplies the store by 3.1x and takes sharing to exactly zero. Sharing also compounds with the horizon rather than saturating early, rising 6% to 9% to 14% as one world is re-run at 10, 20 and 40 rounds.

</details>


### [54] [Relic: From Multi-Agent Collaboration to Persistent Organizational Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32965)
*Hongyi Du,Tianyi Zhang,Weijia Zhang,Yi Yang,Haofei Yu,Kunlun Zhu,Tianxiang Dai,Shang Jiang,Zhelun Gao,Jiaxin Pei,Shang Zhu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Relic 通过让团队把反复错误提炼成可执行、可治理的协议，显著提高了协作正确性与交付率，并在多个基准与迁移场景下优于文本或无协议的对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际组织中多智能体会发生冲突，口头或临时的对话无法保证教训随人员更替而持续生效，因而需要一种能把协作经验固化为可执行、可治理的组织级状态的机制。

Method: 作者设计并实现了 Relic：成员反思可见工作、提议规则并治理其采纳；采纳的协议将触发条件、责任、所需证据与执行后果绑定到运行时，同时允许修订和退役。评估包括：360 次受控运行（10 个软件工作负载、3 个模型）、新成员迁移实验（无协议、文本协议、可执行绑定三种条件对比）、以及在 CooperBench 基准上的大规模评测。

Result: Relic 将“完整合同交付率”从 14.06% 提升到 19.76%（+5.71 个百分点）；在新成员迁移场景，行为正确率分别为：无继承协议 25.4%、可读文本 34.6%、可执行绑定 41.2%（比文本高 6.5 个点）；在 CooperBench（排除损坏对）上达成 367/477（76.9%），为同类对等结构系统中最好；在固定的 48 对同模型子集上也超越了 Solo 基线（29/48 vs. 26/48），并在所有模型层次上改善了全部四个验证的生产端点。

Conclusion: Relic 将反复出现的协作失败转化为组织拥有的、可执行的协议，从而使经验能够跨成员持续生效并改善协作结果。

Abstract: Multiple agents may often conflict in an organization: for example, one coding agent changes an interface in a repository, but another continues to develop on the old version where existing tests become stale. A conversation can resolve the episode, but when the participants change, what makes the lesson continue to govern the team? We introduce Relic, which turns recurring collaboration failures into organization-owned, executable protocols. Members reflect on visible work, propose rules, and govern their adoption. Adopted protocols bind triggers, responsibilities, required evidence, and execution consequences to the runtime, while remaining open to revision and retirement. In one traced case, repeated integration friction produces an interface-review rule that governs later pull requests and is revised as work continues. Across 360 controlled runs over ten software workloads and three models, Relic raises complete-contract delivery from 14.06% to 19.76% (+5.71 percentage points) over a matched structured team without the protocol lifecycle, improving all four verified production endpoints in every model stratum. Under fresh-member transfer, behavioral correctness is 25.4% with no inherited protocol, 34.6% with the same rules provided as readable text, and 41.2% with executable bindings, a +6.5-point advantage over text alone. On the full CooperBench benchmark, after excluding broken benchmark pairs, Relic achieves 367/477 (76.9%), establishing the best reported result among peer-structured systems. On the fixed 48-pair same-model subset, Relic also exceeds Solo (29/48 vs. 26/48), reversing the coordination loss exhibited by the official peer baseline. Together, these results show how collaboration experience can become persistent organizational state that remains useful beyond the members who created it.

</details>


### [55] [Train4Merge: A Controlled Single-Teacher Study of RL vs. SFT Teachers for OPD-Based Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32303)
*Jingyuan Huang,Zuming Huang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Xiaoming Zhai,Wei Chu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 受控OPD实验表明：从相同基点训练出的RL专家比SFT专家作为教师更易教学，关键原因可能是RL模型与共享初始化的参数距离更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察构造领域专家时常见的上游选择——监督微调（SFT）或强化学习（RL）——在其作为OPD教师时的有效性差异。即便教师在标准任务上表现相近，它们是否同样“好教”学生？

Method: 开展受控的单教师OPD实验：从Qwen3.5-9B出发在Agentic、Reasoning、Perception三类任务上分别训练性能可比的SFT与RL专家；以相同初始化构造学生，采用on-policy蒸馏（教师在学生轨迹上监督）训练学生；对比学生最终表现与教师与基线的差距，并分析教师参数空间与共享初始化的距离。

Result: 在最优检查点下，RL指导的学生比SFT指导的学生在Agentic、Reasoning、Perception上分别高出4.27、1.50、0.86个百分点；RL学生也能更多地恢复教师相对于基线的性能增益（Agentic：SFT学生恢复44.44%，RL学生恢复115.00%，RL学生甚至超过其教师）。分析显示RL教师在参数空间上更接近共享初始化，因而学生更容易跟随其方向更新。

Conclusion: 在基于共享初始化的on-policy蒸馏（OPD）场景中，等性能的RL专家比SFT专家更能作为有效教师，因而更利于学生模型恢复并超越教师的性能增益。

Abstract: Domain experts trained from a shared checkpoint can be merged into one model through on-policy distillation (OPD), where they act as teachers supervising a student on its own trajectories. One upstream choice is rarely examined: whether to build each expert with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning (RL). Yet equally strong teachers need not be equally good teachers. We probe this choice through controlled single-teacher OPD, a building block of multi-teacher OPD: in Agentic, Reasoning, and Perception, comparably performing SFT and RL teachers are trained from Qwen3.5-9B, each guiding a student initialized from it. At their best checkpoints, RL-guided students outperform SFT-guided students by 4.27, 1.50, and 0.86 percentage points in Agentic, Reasoning, and Perception, respectively, and recover more of their teachers' performance gains over the base model. The contrast is clearest in Agentic, where the best SFT-guided student recovers only 44.44% of its teacher's gain, whereas the best RL-guided student recovers 115.00%, surpassing its teacher. Our analysis points to an explanation: RL teachers stay much closer to the shared initialization in parameter space than SFT teachers and are therefore easier for their students to follow.

</details>


### [56] [SRE-Marathon: A Continuous, Change-Driven Benchmark for Autonomous Site Reliability Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33023)
*Yifang Tian,Yingjian Bai,Yifeng He,Zichun Chong,Yuanchen Gao,Yiran Li,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRE-Marathon构建了长期连续SRE评测环境，暴露了现有方法在持续运维修复能力上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SRE基准多为短期单次注入情形，但真实生产是持续的、噪声多、故障重叠并经常与代码/配置变更相关，需要一个更贴近生产的长期评估框架。

Method: 设计一个以固定节奏调用的代理接口：每次提供累积报警历史和持久工作区；在一个实时两区Kubernetes部署上，故障编排器按生产校准的带种子安排注入重叠故障；通过同一构建流水线部署经策划的代码和配置变更；每次运行封装记录并离线评分。Marathon-Score按故障对“关联→定位→修复”有序进展计分，所有指标基于记录的系统证据确定性计算。

Result: 在三个应用、每次约60个故障的设置下，10种方法中最优者仅得分41.3/100。代理常能完成相关性与定位步骤，但几乎不会在故障仍然存在时完成修复。

Conclusion: 本文提出了SRE-Marathon，一个针对长期、连续性站点可靠性工程任务的基准；结论是现有自动化方法在这种更接近生产的连续场景下表现薄弱——能做出相关性判断和定位，但几乎不能在故障持续存在时完成修复。

Abstract: Benchmarks for site reliability engineering (SRE) agents are typically episodic: one fault is injected, the agent receives an incident task, and its response is scored. Production operation is not. Incidents surface through noisy alerts, overlap in time, and often originate from code or configuration changes. We present SRE-Marathon, a benchmark for long-horizon, continuous SRE operation. An agent is invoked at a fixed cadence with cumulative alert history and a persistent workspace while operating a live two-zone Kubernetes deployment as a fault orchestrator injects overlapping faults according to a seeded, production-calibrated schedule. Curated code and configuration changes deployed through the same build pipeline available for repair. Each run is recorded into a sealed bundle and scored offline: Marathon-Score credits each injected fault for ordered progress through correlation, localization, and repair, with all metrics computed deterministically from recorded system evidence. Across three applications and about sixty faults per run, the best of 10 methods reaches only 41.3 out of 100. Agents often correlate and localize faults, but almost never complete repairs while the faults remain active.

</details>


### [57] [PhiFold: Towards Dynamic Protein Design with Physics-Structured Covariance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32309)
*Yutian Liu,Mujie Lin,LanqianZhang,Meng Fan,Chang Liu,ZhiweiNie,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhiFold统一生成蛋白骨架与其平衡二阶动力学，通过将协方差分解为三部分并重构为正定矩阵，提供了一个紧凑且可控的动力学感知蛋白质生成框架，有助于设计“会动”的蛋白质而不仅是“长得像”的蛋白质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常把动力学视为结构生成之后通过模拟或预测的下游属性，而以MD轨迹作为生成目标又会因路径相关性而过度指定平衡态。需要一种紧凑且物理一致的平衡动力学表征以用于功能导向的蛋白质设计。

Method: 通过将全协方差分解为三部分：局部柔性（local flexibility）、低秩集体运动基底（low-rank collective-motion representation）和残基级集体参与度（residue-wise collective participation），并将三者组装为具有精确边缘一致性的正定协方差矩阵，PhiFold以紧凑且物理受限的方式表征平衡动力学，联合生成骨架与协方差。

Result: 在生成的蛋白上，PhiFold在恢复局部波动和远程残基耦合方面优于基线，并在主导集体运动子空间上保持竞争力；同时支持双向调控残基柔性且不破坏骨架可设计性。

Conclusion: PhiFold实现了在生成蛋白质骨架的同时显式生成其二阶动力学（残基位移协方差），从而将结构生成扩展为包含平衡动力学信息的功能感知设计方法。

Abstract: Protein design is moving beyond structural correctness toward function-aware design, yet existing generative models typically treat dynamics as a downstream property estimated through simulation or prediction after structure generation. Using MD trajectories as a generative target is also undesirable because stochastic, path-dependent trajectories over-specify the underlying equilibrium ensemble. We introduce PhiFold, a framework for jointly generating protein backbones and their second-order dynamics, represented by residue-displacement covariance. Rather than predicting the quadratically sized full covariance, PhiFold decomposes dynamics into three interpretable components: local flexibility, a low-rank collective-motion representation, and residue-wise collective participation. These components are assembled into a positive-definite covariance matrix with exact marginal consistency, yielding a compact and physically constrained representation of equilibrium dynamics. Across generated proteins, PhiFold improves recovery of local fluctuations and long-range residue coupling while remaining competitive on dominant collective-motion subspaces. It further enables bidirectional control of residue flexibility while preserving backbone designability. By unifying structure generation with an explicit representation of equilibrium dynamics, PhiFold lays a foundation for designing proteins not only by how they look, but also by how they move.

</details>


### [58] [Compositional Safety Failures in Harness Evolution: Identification and Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33123)
*Zhixiang Zhang,Zesen Liu,Wai Ip Lai,Hongxu chen,Dongdong She*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce typed hypergraph to model and efficiently monitor cross-component safety interactions in self-evolving agent harnesses, empirically reducing compositional failures while balancing utility and cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-evolving agents continually update persistent components (memory, prompts, skills, tools), causing potential cross-component interactions that can produce emergent unsafe behaviors even when individual updates are safe. Prior work validated isolated updates but neglected cross-component compositional risks.

Method: Systematic discovery of pairwise and 3-way compositional failures across three safety benchmarks; model component states as nodes and safety-relevant higher-order interactions as hyperedges in a typed hypergraph; update only neighborhood of changed states on harness evolution; implement hypergraph-guided runtime monitoring to check interaction neighborhoods instead of full combinatorial composition space.

Result: Empirical identification of 43 pairwise and 18 irreducible 3-way failures; experiments show the hypergraph method mitigates compositional safety risks, preserves utility, reduces interaction-checking cost, and reveals an empirical trade-off between safety, utility, and computational cost across mechanisms.

Conclusion: The paper identifies compositional safety failures arising during harness evolution and proposes a typed hypergraph representation plus hypergraph-guided runtime monitoring to efficiently detect and mitigate cross-component unsafe interactions while preserving task utility and reducing checking cost.

Abstract: Self-evolving agent harnesses continually update persistent components such as memory, prompts, skills, and tools. We call this process harness evolution. However, such evolution could introduce unexpected safety risks. Existing work studies harness misevolution and validates candidate harnesses or attributed individual component updates, leaving safety analysis of cross-component update interactions largely unexamined. To address this gap, we study compositional safety failures in harness evolution, where interactions among individually safe and utility-preserving component updates can produce undesirable or unsafe agent behavior, revealing a safety risk intrinsic to harness evolution. Across three safety-related benchmarks, we identify 43 pairwise and 18 irreducible 3-way compositional safety failures. Conventional solution incurs combinatorial complexity in validating cross-component interactions, leaving the safety checking impractical as the harness evolves. To solve this, we introduced a typed hypergraph that represents component states as nodes and safety-relevant higher-order interactions as hyperedges. When the harness changes, the hypergraph updates only the interaction neighborhood of the changed states rather than reconstructing the global composition space. Building on that, we develop a hypergraph-guided runtime monitoring mechanism. Experiments show that our method effectively mitigates compositional safety risks while preserving task utility and reducing interaction-checking costs, and further reveal an empirical safety-utility-cost trade-off across different safety mechanisms.

</details>


### [59] [Delayed Supervision for Test-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32312)
*Jinha Kim,Taksh Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在长期无关事件后监督一次性问答，能改善基于测试时快速权重/增量状态的模型的记忆可用性，且在多种架构和评估上表现出可观提升，但机制仍需通过消融进一步澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的按标记预测训练未强制事实在多次记忆更新后仍然可访问；需要一种直接监督测试时内存长期保留的训练信号。

Method: 在后训练阶段，向模型插入‘模拟器-有监督’的问答：在长时间无关事件之后提问，答案在一次性分支上评估（不回写到主事件流），与常规下一标记预测并行训练。对LaCT-760M、DeltaNet-1.3B（并单独报告RWKV-7）使用TextWorld轨迹训练，评估采用BABILong和RULER面板。

Result: 相比仅事件训练，延迟QA使LaCT在BABILong上提升5.48个百分点、在RULER(single-needle)提升1.45点；DeltaNet分别提升1.32点和3.27点；RWKV-7在其面板上分别报告4.60和7.00点的提升。作者同时指出尚未明确多少收益来自‘延迟’本身而非一般问答或答案终止监督。

Conclusion: 延迟问答作为一种外层训练目标，可以显著改善测试时的可用短期/中期记忆保留，使得LaCT、DeltaNet等在保留任务上优于仅事件预测的训练。

Abstract: Test-time language models adapt a compact memory while processing the input sequence. This
  perspective encompasses nonlinear fast-weight learning in LaCT, associative delta-rule
  updates in DeltaNet, and generalized delta-rule state updates in RWKV-7. Training these
  models to predict the next token does not explicitly require a fact to remain accessible
  after many subsequent memory updates. We study delayed supervision for this test-time memory:
  during post-training, ask a simulator-grounded question only after a long interval of
  unrelated events, and supervise its answer alongside ordinary next-token prediction.
  Questions are evaluated on disposable branches, so their answers never enter the continuing
  event stream. The construction distinguishes retention from revision: a retained fact must
  remain valid throughout the delay, whereas a revised fact must be answered with its latest
  value. We evaluate this approach on LaCT-760M and plain DeltaNet-1.3B using TextWorld
  training trajectories and shared BABILong and RULER evaluation panels, and include a
  separately reported RWKV-7 comparison. Relative to event-only training, delayed QA improves
  BABILong by 5.48 percentage points for LaCT and 1.32 points for DeltaNet, and single-needle
  RULER by 1.45 and 3.27 points, respectively. The RWKV-7 comparison reports gains of 4.60 and
  7.00 points on its own panels. These results support delayed semantic supervision as a
  practical outer training objective for usable test-time memory, while leaving open how much
  of the benefit derives specifically from delay rather than general question-answering and
  answer-termination supervision.

</details>


### [60] [On Device Agentic Operation Caches -- Classifier-Centric NL-to-Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33141)
*Moghis Fereidouni,Anthony Arnold,Sumit Gulwani,Mark Marron,A. B. Siddique*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把NL-to-Action问题改为分类并使用在设备操作缓存，论文在Excel公式场景下实现了显著的成本和延迟改善，适用于高频可枚举操作的在设备执行场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘化代理化AI常依赖大型云端LLM，带来网络延迟、隐私和成本问题。针对高频可枚举操作，将问题转为分类并本地缓存，可以改善响应时间、隐私保护与成本效率。

Method: 提出在设备操作缓存（operation caches），并用轻量分类器将输入映射到预定义的操作/操作类；对未命中缓存的请求回退到云端大模型生成并将高频结果写入缓存。以Excel公式生成为案例，评估缓存命中与云路由策略的对比。

Result: 在Excel公式生成任务上，相较于纯云端模型路由策略，总推理成本降低56%；在缓存命中时，响应延迟缩短约5倍。

Conclusion: 将自然语言到操作（NL-to-Action）问题从生成式转为以分类为中心的在设备操作缓存机制，能显著降低对云端大模型推理的依赖，从而减少延迟、提升隐私并降低运行成本。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly being embedded in software applications to provide natural language interfaces to features and functionality. In most cases these agents are powered by enterprise (100+ billion parameter) or frontier class large language models that require substantial computational resources run and depend on cloud hosted inference to handle the task of transforming natural language inputs into actionable software operations. This reliance on cloud-hosted inference introduces substantial network latency on top of LLM inference times, creates data privacy concerns, and, given the costs of running these models, can rapidly escalate expenses associated with supporting agentic features.
  This paper introduces a novel means of converting the NL-to-Action problem from a generative one into a classification-centric formulation via on-device operation caches. These caches allow an agentic system to handle frequently occurring classes of actions completely on-device -- reducing latency, enhancing privacy, and lowering operational costs. We show that for a classic NL-to-Formula task, generating Excel Formula in response to user requests, this approach reduces total inference cost by 56% when compared to cloud-only model-routing based inference and, on cache hits, reduces the latency to response latency by 5x.

</details>


### [61] [RLHarness: Co-evolving Procedural Skills with Reinforcement Learning for Long-horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32326)
*Ziqiao Shang,Zian Xu,Ji-Chen Yan,Weiming Wu,Ziyi Jia,Jie Meng,Tao Huang,Shan Huang,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RLHARNESS通过版本化、可重构的技能与示例库，交替演化外部程序与策略，解决强化学习在多模态长链推理中的程序冷启动并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅靠终端验证信号的强化学习中，策略知道答案是否正确却不知道如何产生它，导致必须同时发现并学会可复用的推理子程序，产生程序冷启动问题。外部技能能外化成功程序并减少重复探索，但固定技能库会与不断演化的策略失配，单独更新技能会导致触发条件与示例不一致。需要一个同步演化的机制。

Method: 提出一个统一、版本化的Harness，包含Skills、选择与执行协议、少样本示例和任务合同。流程为：先用Exploration-Distillation Harness构建初始技能集与经验证的轨迹（用于SFT与DAPO I），在首次RL后用Post-RL Reconstruction Harness从新的成功/失败滚动（rollouts）重建技能、协议与示例，然后用DAPO II适配策略。方法核心是交替更新Harness与策略，而非固定技能库。

Result: 在四个多模态推理任务上获得大幅提升：MetroMap/TravelMap准确率从16.25%/27.50%提升到62.00%/50.00%，Fee-VL/Cancel-VL F1从37.13%/45.50%提升到65.81%/65.51%。所有任务在重构与DAPO II之后达到最佳，证明演化的Harness能补强RL。

Conclusion: 引入RLHARNESS能通过版本化的Harness在策略学习与外部技能程序间交替演化，从而解决强化学习在多模态推理中的程序冷启动问题，并显著提升多个任务的准确率与F1。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires models to preserve visual evidence through long decision chains while selecting appropriate procedures across diverse scenarios and rules. When learning is guided only by terminal verifiers, reinforcement learning (RL) reveals whether a final answer is correct but not how it should be produced. The policy must therefore discover reusable reasoning procedures while learning to execute them, creating a program cold-start problem. Skills can externalize successful procedures, reduce repeated exploration, and provide inspectable guidance. However, a fixed Skill Bank assumes that this guidance remains compatible with an evolving policy, while updating Skills alone can leave their triggers, execution protocols, and demonstrations stale or mutually inconsistent. We introduce RLHARNESS, which organizes Skills, selection and execution protocols, few-shot demonstrations, and task contracts into a unified, versioned Harness and alternates Harness evolution with policy learning. An Exploration-Distillation Harness builds the initial Harness and version-aligned verified traces for SFT and DAPO I. After the first RL block, a Post-RL Reconstruction Harness rebuilds Skills, protocols, and demonstrations from fresh success-failure rollouts, and DAPO II adapts the policy to the reconstructed program. RLHARNESS improves Accuracy from 16.25%/27.50% to 62.00%/50.00% on MetroMap/TravelMap and raises F1 score from 37.13%/45.50% to 65.81%/65.51% on Fee-VL/Cancel-VL. All four tasks achieve their best results only after reconstruction and DAPO II, showing that an evolving Harness complements RL by continually updating the external program that the policy learns to execute.

</details>


### [62] [MetaBench-Harness: Unlocking End-to-End Optimization of Benchmark Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33411)
*Xuanjun Chen,Hua-Hsuan Chen,Wei-Chung Lu,Yinghao Ma,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaBench-Harness——一个双回路的端到端基准生成与元优化框架，自动演化出对前沿LLM更具挑战性和判别性的基准，并在CodeContests与AIME-2024上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM进展速度快导致静态基准迅速饱和，现有自动进化方法依赖硬编码规则、受限于孤立任务的扰动，需要一个能端到端优化基准生成流程、自动发现更难更具判别性的题目的方法。

Method: 提出双回路搜索框架：内回路用可编排的benchmark harness在每轮生成新基准；外回路为meta-harness，对历史演化轨迹进行搜索与迭代，自动改进harness实现。将该框架应用于CodeContests和AIME-2024两个竞赛级数据集，使用轨迹分析、质量指标和案例研究评估演化效果。

Result: 演化出的基准在能力上更具挑战性且可区分前沿模型；伴随轮次演化，评估显示演化合理性、基准胜任力和评估器鲁棒性均稳步提升；案例显示框架能灵活利用多种难度杠杆重构题目并提升所需能力维度。

Conclusion: MetaBench-Harness通过端到端优化基准生成流程，超越了对单个任务的僵硬扰动策略，能够生成对前沿大模型更具挑战性和判别性的测试集，缓解静态基准饱和问题。

Abstract: Rapid progress in Large Language Models (LLMs) is saturating static benchmarks faster than they can be designed. While existing automated evolution frameworks attempt to generate harder questions by perturbing individual tasks, they remain constrained by rigid, hard-coded generation rules. Moving beyond the evolution of isolated tasks, we propose to optimize the benchmark generation workflow itself end to end with MetaBench-Harness, a dual-loop search framework. Specifically, the inner loop utilizes a benchmark harness to generate a new benchmark in each round, while the outer meta-harness orchestration layer iteratively refines and searches over harness implementations based on historical evolution trajectories. By applying MetaBench-Harness to the competitive programming CodeContests and Olympiad mathematics AIME-2024 datasets, we demonstrate that the evolved benchmarks are challenging and discriminative for frontier models. Trajectory and quality analyses verify that MetaBench-Harness enables multi-dimensional evolution, steadily improving evolution reasonableness, benchmark competency, and evaluator robustness across successive rounds. Furthermore, case studies reveal its effective utilization of diverse difficulty levers to reframe problems and elevate required capabilities. Ultimately, this work provides a solution to the pressing challenge of benchmark saturation.

</details>


### [63] [HyperReCo: Retrieving and Connecting Evidence with Hypergraph Neural Networks for LLM Multi-hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32327)
*Zicheng Zhao,Linhao Luo,Junnan Dong,Haoran Luo,Xiaoli Li,Shirui Pan,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于HyperGNN的检索与显式超路径解码方法，改善多跳QA的证据检索与连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图检索依赖固定扩散/结构，忽略查询相关的交互且未显式呈现证据间连接，增加LLM推理负担。

Method: 把文档作为包含实体的超边构建超图，使用超图消息传递并联合监督文档与实体以学习查询依赖的交互；通过梯度归因将学习到的交互解码为显式超路径（GGHD），便于LLM推理。

Result: 在六个基准上，特别是三个多跳QA数据集，HyperReCo在检索指标上优于对比方法，并带来强下游QA表现；案例分析显示解码的超路径有助于连接证据。

Conclusion: HyperReCo通过HyperGNN和Gradient-Guided Hyper-Path Decoding，有效检索并显式连接多跳QA所需证据，显著提升检索和下游QA性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities, with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) supporting complex multi-hop reasoning by retrieving evidence distributed across documents. Graph-based approaches exploit connections among evidence, and hypergraph-based retrieval further preserves higher-order entity associations within documents and connects documents through shared entities. However, existing hypergraph retrievers often rely on predefined structural expansion or diffusion, which may miss query-dependent interactions needed to identify relevant evidence. They also leave connections among retrieved evidence implicit, requiring LLMs to reconstruct these connections before reasoning. Therefore, we propose HyperReCo, a framework for retrieving and connecting evidence with a hypergraph neural network (HyperGNN). We represent each document as a hyperedge over its extracted entities, with shared entities connecting the hyperedges. Through hypergraph message passing with joint supervision over documents and entities, the HyperGNN learns query-dependent interactions to retrieve complementary evidence. We further introduce Gradient-Guided Hyper-Path Decoding (GGHD), which uses gradient attribution to interpret the learned interactions and translate them into explicit hyper-paths that help LLMs combine complementary facts for multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on six benchmarks show that HyperReCo achieves the best retrieval performance among the compared methods on all three multi-hop QA datasets, together with strong downstream QA performance. Case studies and further analyses demonstrate the utility of decoded hyper-paths for connecting retrieved evidence.

</details>


### [64] [SpecRead: A Benchmark for Measuring Whether Language Models Understand Hardware Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33699)
*Feilian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecRead，一个自动可评分且可再生的硬件规范理解基准，分离规范理解与代码生成；初步基线显示模型在检索/定位上较好，但在矛盾判定与一致性判断上易错且误报高，提示对规范理解的评估仍有明显挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件设计LLM基准通过下游产物（RTL/测试）评价，若模型失败难以判定是读不懂规范还是会写代码，故需一个单独度量“规范阅读/理解”的基准。

Method: 构建SpecRead v2.1：从10个公开OpenTitan IP提取规范片段，设计4类题型（精确检索、跨节推理、变异后规范矛盾检测、规范-RTL一致性检查）并加入82个控制项；对Type-4使用实际RTL变异并用Icarus Verilog模拟确认可观测行为改变；提供带/不带规范的消融测试；使用自动确定性判分，保守地将灰区计为错误；用Ministral-3B作为初始基线并测试不同提示（含“规则表”）。

Result: SpecRead v2.1包含385题+82控制项；无规范时t1/t2子集准确率为3/20，说明题目需要给定片段而非单纯记忆。Ministral-3B总体33.2%（128/385，宏均39.0%）：t1检索55.2%，t2跨节推理51.7%；矛盾题（按“判定+定位”）t3=48.0%、t4=63.3%，但对干扰项虚报51.2%、对一致性控制项虚报100%；分层评分显示定位准确率高（78.9–81.6%）但矛盾类别判定低（43.9–49.7%）；结构化“规则表”提示普遍降低准确率（除t2持平）。基准可通过注入变异再生type-3题，全部基于公开来源。

Conclusion: SpecRead有效地把“理解规范”与“生成代码”能力分离，提供可自动评分的、可再生的规范理解基准；初步评测显示小型模型在检索与跨节推理上尚可，但在矛盾检测与一致性判断上表现薄弱且存在高误报率。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) in hardware design evaluate downstream artifacts such as generated RTL, assertions, or testbenches. When a model fails such a benchmark, the failure is ambiguous: it may have misread the specification, or it may have understood the specification and failed to write the code. We present SpecRead, a benchmark that isolates specification comprehension from generation ability. SpecRead v2.1 contains 385 questions over 10 open-source OpenTitan IP blocks: exact retrieval, cross-section reasoning, contradiction detection in mutated specifications, and spec-RTL consistency checking, plus 82 controls (41 distractor, 41 consistent-RTL). Type-4 items are built from real RTL mutations; we retain only mutations that Icarus Verilog simulation shows to change observable behavior. A with-spec vs. without-spec ablation suggests the questions require the excerpt, not training recall alone (without-spec accuracy 3/20 on the t1/t2 subset), though memorization of the source text may still help spot mutations. As an initial characterization with a small model, Ministral-3B scores 33.2% overall (128/385; macro average 39.0%): 55.2% on retrieval, 51.7% on cross-section reasoning. On the two contradiction-focused types, the verdict-plus-location measure gives 48.0% (t3) and 63.3% (t4), with a 51.2% false-positive rate on distractors and 100% on consistent-RTL controls. Layered scoring shows the model locates contradictions well (78.9-81.6% location accuracy) but scores lower on their category (43.9-49.7%). A structured "rule-table" prompting intervention lowers accuracy on every question type except t2 (tied). SpecRead is automatically scorable by deterministic checks, with gray-zone cases counted wrong under the conservative main scoring. The benchmark is regenerable for type-3 items via mutation injection, and built exclusively from public sources.

</details>


### [65] [Enabling Timely Guidance before Skill Retrieval: Retaining Helpful Warm Tips in Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32339)
*Feng Liang,Yupeng Li,Runhao Zeng,Francis C. M. Lau,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TipsWarm维护一个预算化的技能要点池，结合事件触发的抽取与每轮廉价筛选，把精炼指导按需注入对话，能在不显著增加维护成本的前提下提高LLM代理在多项任务上的成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理解决复杂任务时需要在承诺执行前获得有效指导，但现有技能机制通常仅暴露元数据并按需加载完整内容，导致有用建议在检索前不可用；通用记忆方法维护成本高昂，而将指导长期放在上下文中又易反复暴露无关或有害建议。

Method: 设计一个补充现有技能机制的模块：定期（事件触发时）由大型模型评估并抽取技能要点，维护一个有预算约束的warm tips池；每轮通过廉价的筛选机制从该池中选取少量要点注入对话上下文，实现按需提供指导而非每次载入完整技能内容。

Result: 在三组编码和迭代任务基准测试中，TipsWarm在任务成功率上优于近期的技能机制和通用记忆基线，同时保持时间效率（运行时间低）。

Conclusion: TipsWarm通过在每一轮对话中注入来自技能的预算化关键信息（warm tips），在保证维护成本可控的同时，为LLM代理提供事前、可转移的操作指导，从而明显提升复杂任务的成功率和时间效率。

Abstract: Reusable skills help LLM-based agents solve complex tasks, but the agent must receive guidance before it commits to an ineffective approach. Existing skill mechanisms often expose only metadata and load full content on demand, leaving useful guidance unavailable until the agent decides to retrieve it.
