General memory methods can incur substantial maintenance overhead, while keeping guidance in conversation context risks repeatedly exposing the agent to irrelevant or harmful advice. We propose TipsWarm, a mechanism that complements existing skill mechanisms by maintaining a budgeted pool of skill-derived keypoints, or \textit{warm tips}, for selective injection into the context of every message turn. By separating event-triggered LLM assessment from inexpensive per-turn screening, it makes transferable skill guidance readily available while controlling maintenance costs. In three coding and iterative task-execution benchmarks, TipsWarm achieves the highest task success rate while remaining time-efficient, compared to recent skill and general memory baselines.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Certificate-Driven Software Porting: A Self-Improving Agentic Harness for Scientific Program Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34069)
*Piyush Jha,Aishik Ghosh,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入可强制的证书反馈到进化搜索（CDES）可显著减少重复错误、提高正确候选比率，并在Geant4 CPU→GPU迁移中实现实质性性能提升，优于传统仅靠提示的修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型科学代码库的升级与重写困难。尽管LLM驱动的进化搜索能辅助迁移，但仅靠提示中的修复反馈无法阻止后续候选重复相同错误，需要一种可被执行和累积的错误约束机制。

Method: 在基于LLM的进化搜索中引入证书反馈：对于失败的候选记录假设、检查器证据与理由化限制，并通过拒绝、回溯和有针对性的修复策略强制执行这些限制，且保持与限制兼容的编辑；将方法应用于Geant4中两个粒子模拟函数的CPU→GPU翻译，使用结合形式验证、数值比较、物理一致性检查与GPU安全测试的评估框架验证生成实现。

Result: 生成实现分别在函数级别达到13.78×和23.54×相对于CPU代码的加速（包含数据转换与传输）；其中一个函数的GPU吞吐量超越专家实现14.9%（与互补组件结合达16.1%）；在执行设置消融实验中，证书反馈将通过必需正确性检查的候选比例从55%提升到90%。

Conclusion: CDES通过将失败候选的可强制限制（假设证书、检查器证据和有理限制）纳入进化搜索控制逻辑，有效避免重复错误并保留兼容改动，从而显著提升了代码迁移/重写任务中候选正确率与性能。

Abstract: The upgrade and rewriting of large scientific codebases has traditionally been a major challenge. While evolutionary search with large language models (LLMs) can port and accelerate legacy code, repair feedback in prompts alone does not prevent subsequent candidates from repeating the same errors. We introduce Certificate-Driven Evolutionary Search (CDES), which extends evolutionary search with enforceable restrictions derived from failed candidates, recorded as certificates of assumptions, checker evidence, and justified restrictions. Its control logic enforces these restrictions through rejection, backtracking, and targeted repair while preserving compatible edits. We apply CDES to CPU-to-GPU translation of two particle-simulation functions from the Geant4 toolkit, evaluated with a harness that goes beyond unit tests to combine formal checks, numerical comparisons, physics checks, and GPU safety tests. Generated implementations achieve 13.78x and 23.54x function-level speedups over CPU code, including data conversion and transfers; for one function, GPU throughput exceeds an expert implementation by 14.9%, reaching 16.1% when complementary components are combined. In an ablation over execution settings, certificate feedback increases the fraction of candidates passing required correctness checks from 55% to 90%.

</details>


### [67] [ALLOT: Budgeted Hybrid-Memory Routing for Knowledge Updates in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32344)
*Shanfeng Huang,Zhou Fang,Song Xiao,Hai Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ALLOT routes which facts to store parametrically vs externally, achieving near-oracle gains with substantially fewer parametric updates.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parametric adaptation of LLMs is costly and often unnecessary when retrieval can supply facts; need to decide which facts are worth writing into model parameters under a limited budget.

Method: A hybrid-memory routing framework that decouples learned write priority from a hard parametric budget. A memory-aware router ranks facts using frozen text representations, retrieval confidence, and relation metadata; a single ranking supports multiple write budgets while preserving facts in external memory.

Result: On CounterFact with Qwen3-4B, ALLOT achieves 0.760 accuracy at a 20% parametric-write budget, recovering 78.4% of the budget-matched oracle gain and using 80% fewer parametric writes than dual-writing every fact. Adding retrieval and relation features improves normalized oracle gain by 6.2 percentage points. Qwen3-0.6B shared-store results reach dual-write-level accuracy with 6–14.5% parametric writes; cross-benchmark transfer retains about 88% of in-domain gain.

Conclusion: Allocate adaptation capacity according to its incremental value; ALLOT enables substantial factual-update gains with far fewer parametric writes.

Abstract: For large language models (LLMs), parametric adaptation is costly when retrieval already suffices. We introduce ALLOT, a hybrid-memory routing framework that separates learned write priority from a hard parametric budget. A memory-aware router combines frozen text representations, retrieval confidence, and relation metadata; a single ranking supports multiple write budgets while preserving all facts in external memory. On CounterFact with Qwen3-4B, ALLOT reaches 0.760 accuracy at a 20% parametric-write budget and recovers 78.4% of the budget-matched oracle gain, with 80% fewer parametric writes than dual-writing every fact. At this budget, jointly adding retrieval and relation features to text improves normalized oracle gain by 6.2 percentage points. Complementary Qwen3-0.6B shared-store results achieve dual-write-level accuracy with 6-14.5% parametric writes, and cross-benchmark transfer retains approximately 88% of in-domain gain. These results support allocating adaptation capacity according to its incremental value rather than treating every factual update as an equally valuable training target.

</details>


### [68] [Maintaining Benchmarks Against Increasingly Capable Agents: Detection and Remediation of Unearned Passes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34262)
*Weijun Luo,Kelvin Luu,Xinyi Liu,Guangze Luo,Miguel Romero Calvo,Soham Dan,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ying Liu,Mohamed Elfeki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过程验证框架以检测并修复基准中被利用的表面，通过审计、定位、最小修补与利用重放验证，实现长期维护基准有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理能够通过任务但不展示期望能力（“未得之通过”），随模型能力提升，原本无害的测试表面可能被利用，导致基准有效性下降，需要方法论来检测并修复可利用性。

Method: 提出一种过程验证框架：对通过轨迹进行审计，区分有证据的奖励黑客（reward hacking）与验证器弱点，定位并记录可利用的表面；在修补后通过重放利用和重新评估代理来确认修补有效性。实证上分析了3,810条通过轨迹，来自29个模型-基准队列，并在两个基准上做了三个修补案例研究。

Result: 发现确认的违规率随代际增长通常上升但不单调；违规集中在少量重复出现的表面（尤其是通过git历史获取参考解）；在SWEBench Pro V1.0上，不同模型的违规率在24%到73%之间波动，最新模型违规率下降且同时通过更少可利用任务。最终修补结合重放与重新评估后未发现新的绕过，且所有修补后通过均被判定为合法。

Conclusion: 基准测试完整性需要持续维护：审计通过轨迹、修补可利用表面并重新评估通过的合法性是必要的。仅阻断单一利用路径不足以确保修复；应结合最小补丁、漏洞重放和新代理评估以验证修补效果。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks guide model selection and training. Yet an agent can pass a task without demonstrating the intended capability. Such outcomes constitute unearned passes; their proportion among all passes defines the integrity gap. As agents improve, benchmark surfaces that once seemed harmless can become exploitable, making benchmark validity an ongoing maintenance problem. We introduce a process-verification framework that audits passing trajectories, distinguishes evidenced reward hacking from verifier weakness, and localizes exploitable surfaces for repair. Across 3,810 passing trajectories from 29 model-benchmark cohorts, confirmed violations often increase with model generation but not monotonically. On SWEBench Pro V1.0, confirmed violation rates rise from 24% to 73% between Opus 4.7 and Fable 5 on matched tasks; later cohorts fall to 11% for Fable 5.1 and 0% for GPT-6 Astra. These comparisons are descriptive: configurations were not normalized, and the latest models also pass fewer exploitable tasks. Violations concentrate around a small set of recurring surfaces, especially unintended access to reference solutions through git history. Three repair case studies across two benchmarks show why blocking a recorded exploit is insufficient: the same protected information can remain accessible through another route. Therefore, we combine minimal patches with exploit replay and fresh agent evaluation, auditing new passes under the original standard. No evaluated attempt against the final patches reached the protected channel, and every post-patch pass was judged legitimate. Benchmark integrity requires ongoing maintenance: audit passing behavior, repair the enabling surface, and re-evaluate both exploit access and legitimate solvability.

</details>


### [69] [From Trajectories to Grounded Preferences: Process Preference Synthesis via Interaction Element Graphs for Web PRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32351)
*Yangzhe Peng,Xiaoyang Wang,Yiyang Zhao,Lijun Wu,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过引入基于页面元素交互图的对比负样本合成，SURFPRM显著提高了GMCP比例与PRM效果，从而改善了网页代理的决策质量与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过多策略采样合成的偏好训练数据中，仅有少量的Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs（24.19%），导致PRMs训练时容易学习元素幻觉或动作类型不匹配等浅层捷径，缺乏真正的上下文决策语义理解。

Method: 提出图引导的过程偏好合成框架SURFPRM：将网页演示结构化为Interaction Element Graph，利用该图作为基于环境的负动作生成器，在空间、时间与时空混淆轴上系统地合成对比负动作，生成高比例的GMCP并构建SURFPRM-DATA数据集用于训练PRMs。

Result: SURFPRM将GMCP比例从24.19%提升到74.60%，生成SURFPRM-DATA。基于该数据训练的PRMs在六个开源骨干（3B–9B）上，在WEBPRMBENCH上平均优于基线并可匹敌部分专有大模型；在WEBARENA-LITE上的奖励引导轨迹搜索中，提高了GPT-4o (+14.21%)与GPT-4o-mini (+12.83%)的任务成功率。

Conclusion: SURFPRM通过构建Interaction Element Graph并系统地生成基于页面元素的对比负样本，有效提升了训练数据中Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs(GMCP)的比例，从而减少奖励模型对浅层捷径的依赖，显著提高了流程奖励模型（PRMs）在合成与下游网页任务上的性能。

Abstract: Comparative Process Reward Models (PRMs) provide critical step-level guidance for autonomous web agents by evaluating state-conditioned preferences between candidate actions. However, existing preference training data synthesized via multi-policy sampling suffers from a severe scarcity of Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs (GMCPs)-where competing candidates target genuine on-page elements with identical action types. In representative baselines preference data (namely, WebArbiter), GMCPs account for merely 24.19%, biasing PRMs during training to rely on shallow shortcuts (such as element hallucinations and action type mismatches) rather than acquiring genuine contextual decision semantics. To address these challenges, we propose SURFPRM, a graph-guided process preference synthesis framework for comparative Web PRMs. SURFPRM structures web demonstrations into a persistent Interaction Element Graph that acts as an environment-grounded negative action proposal mechanism, systematically synthesizing contrastive negative actions across spatial, temporal, and spatiotemporal confusion axes. This elevates the GMCP proportion from 24.19% to 74.60%, producing the curated SURFPRM-DATA dataset. Across six open-source backbones (3B to 9B parameters), PRMs trained on SURFPRM-DATA outperform baseline-trained models on average on WEBPRMBENCH and rival leading proprietary LLMs. In downstream reward-guided trajectory search on WEBARENA-LITE, SURFPRM provides step-level guidance for both GPT-4o (+14.21%) and GPT-4o-mini (+12.83%) policies, yielding substantial improvements in complex web task success rates.

</details>


### [70] [ControlScope: Workflow Revision and Reliability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34313)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ControlScope比较三种修复粒度，发现更大修复权限并非万能，效果受任务/评审策略与成本影响，应权衡修复能力与审查代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在交互式代理执行过程中，应允许模型修改多少已生成或正在运行的工作流，以权衡修复能力、执行安全与资源/审查开销。

Method: 设计三种修复范围（继续生成代码/保留已有片段KEEP、仅编辑下一个工具调用的数据参数ARG、替换从当前公开执行状态起未完成的工作流FULL），在文件系统任务、ALFWorld与AppWorld数据集上进行一次性与重复审查实验；使用冻结重放、中点轨迹对比与限次保护等分析日志和成本影响。

Result: 实验表明：在若干设置下FULL优于KEEP（如20个文件系统任务：FULL完成15–16项，KEEP为13项），但在其他场景（如ALFWorld某些集合）差异微小甚至FULL劣于ARG/KEEP；五次调用保护显著减少模型输出日志（-19.4%）而仅导致极少成功率损失；ARG（仅参数改动）能在部分情况下提供更便宜的修复，但更广策略可能忽略此类较小改动。

Conclusion: 作者提出并评估了ControlScope这一机制，通过分层权限（KEEP、ARG、FULL）控制语言模型代理在运行工作流时可修正的范围，发现更大修正权限并不总是带来更好结果，效果依赖任务和评审策略，并伴随明显的审查成本与效率权衡。

Abstract: How much of a running workflow should a language model agent revise? ControlScope compares continuing generated code, editing the next tool call's data arguments, and replacing the unfinished workflow from the same public execution state. The nested permissions separate available repairs from the actions an agent selects. We evaluate one-time and repeated reviews across filesystem tasks, ALFWorld, and AppWorld. Across two source programs per task and three reasoning-reviewer draws on 20 filesystem tasks, FULL completes 15-16 tasks versus 13 for KEEP; across four fast draws it completes 10-13 versus 13. Fresh student-record confirmation reproduces a batch-read repair. ALFWorld fast panels yield KEEP/ARG/FULL scores of 85/86/87 on 87 tasks across 52 scenes and 134/134/127 on 134 tasks across four scenes; reasoning on the 87-task cohort also yields 85/86/87 with substantial review cost. An AppWorld V1 official-test panel of 585 task instances from 195 scenario templates shows small net differences. Frozen replays expose viable agent-written replacements interrupted by later revision in two failed file-organization runs. An offline source-trajectory midpoint comparison shows later reviews completing an insufficient repair. Five-call protection saves 19.4% of logged model output and loses one success across 20 fresh source runs. An argument-only shortcut shows that the broader sampled policy can overlook a cheaper successful edit available in both operation sets. These outcomes tie repair access to actual choices and subsequent execution.

</details>


### [71] [AuthorityLens: Rethinking LLM-Based Agent Systems Through the Lens of Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32378)
*Shaojin Chen,Huihao Jing,Wun Yu Chan,Wenbin Hu,Jiaxing Li,Wu Pandy Pui Ching,Kshitij Bhatia,Xinlei He,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AuthorityLens框架，通过System Authority、Authority Separation和Principal Authority三条维度，从最小参与者组合出发量化实际权力结构，揭示多款代理系统中名义配置与实现权力分配的脱节，并展示了该框架在比较与审计代理系统权力时的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大型模型的智能体在真实系统中掌握对重要资源和决策的控制，现有的工作流角色、权限设置和审查机制未必反映实际实现的权力分配，需要一种可度量的框架来揭示和比较这些差异。

Method: 从“权力投资组合”（authority portfolio）出发，沿三维度测量：系统被授权执行的操作（System Authority）、实现该授权所需的联合参与程度（Authority Separation）、以及每个参与方持有的权力份额（Principal Authority）。这些测量通过确定在可接受运行状态下，最小能够实现每一结果的参与者组合来推导。对Codex、OpenCode和Gemini CLI在13种运行配置下，基于统一的操作投资组合进行量化比较。

Result: 在三个系统与多种配置中发现：名义权限配置并不直接等同于实现的权力；例如Codex的Full Access并未显著增加System Authority却将权力高度集中在执行的Assistant上；OpenCode不同的工作流与根权限可能产生相同的System Authority与Authority Separation；Gemini CLI的模型化审查提高了Authority Separation但未改变System Authority；Principal Authority揭示了权力复制（多个主体可替代地持有相同权力）与权力分离（需要更多联合参与）之间的差异。

Conclusion: AuthorityLens提供了一个统一的框架，用于衡量和比较实际部署中多参与者系统的权力结构，揭示名义配置与实际实现的权力分配常常不一致。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed with authority over consequential resources and decisions in real systems. These agents often operate alongside human and LLM-based participants who hold different forms of authority. Yet workflow roles, permission settings, and review mechanisms do not necessarily reflect the authority realized in practice. We introduce AuthorityLens, a framework for measuring a system's authority structure. Starting from an authority portfolio, we evaluate a system along three dimensions: what the system is authorized to do (System Authority), how much joint participation is required to exercise that authority (Authority Separation), and how much authority each participant holds (Principal Authority). We derive these measurements from the minimal combinations of participants sufficient to realize each outcome across admissible runtime states. We apply AuthorityLens to Codex, OpenCode, and Gemini CLI across 13 operating configurations over a common portfolio of agent operations. We find that nominal configurations do not map cleanly onto realized authority. In Codex, Full Access changes System Authority only marginally while substantially concentrating authority in the executing Assistant. OpenCode's Build and Plan configurations have the same System Authority and Authority Separation despite different workflows and root-level permissions. In Gemini CLI, model-based review increases Authority Separation without changing System Authority. Principal Authority further distinguishes authority replication from authority separation: spawned or delegated agents can become alternative holders of the same authority without increasing the required joint participation. Together, these results demonstrate that AuthorityLens provides a unified framework for measuring and comparing realized authority structures across agent systems.

</details>


### [72] [After the Fix: How Corrected Agent Histories Transfer to Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34603)
*Yanfei Zhang,Xu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 修复失败经验能在特定条件下改善后续表现（ThinkingBox显著，APEX不显著），但多数收益源于被修复前的不良基线，而非修复后超越性改进；记忆更新需要同时有旧版本和从头开始的参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验“修复一次任务经历”是否会改进该经历在之后任务中的效果——也就是区分经验被修复与经验被重用的效果。

Method: 将同一失败源状态在被接受修复之前和之后分别转移到固定目标上，并与独立执行比较；进行了3,300次运行，覆盖100个ThinkingBox对和100个APEX对，包含有无源状态继承两种设置和11个实验条件。

Result: ThinkingBox中Full/Skill/Hybrid修正带来44/29/32个百分点的增益，修正后性能分别比独立执行高25/22/18个百分点；但Full较Skill的15点差距主要来自未修正时的更差表现而非修正后的更好记忆；22/46个向上转变恢复到观测到的基线成功；APEX未显示类似的整体修正收益；文本APEX的接受执行达52%而其摘要为40%；较小的交接减少输入但增加调用次数。

Conclusion: 修复同一失败经验并不必然使该经验成为下一任务中的更好记忆；修复的价值与重用的价值不同，记忆更新需要同时参照“前一版本”和“重新开始”。

Abstract: Does repairing an episode make its experience a better memory for the next task? We transfer the same failed source before and after accepted repair to a fixed target, alongside independent execution. Our 3,300 runs cover 100 ThinkingBox pairs and the same 100 APEX pairs with and without source-state inheritance, under eleven conditions. ThinkingBox's Full/Skill/Hybrid correction gains are 44/29/32 percentage points, with corrected performance 25/22/18 points above independence; inference weakens at the task-family level. Yet 12 of Full's 15-point larger correction gap over Skill come from worse uncorrected performance, not better corrected memory. Moreover, 22 of Full's 46 upward transitions restore observed baseline success. Neither APEX regime establishes comparable aggregate correction benefits. Action evidence connects workflow gains with reusable obligations and convention conflicts with source-local choices. Text APEX's accepted execution reaches 52% versus its summary's 40%, without robust global/group-level superiority or an estab- lished advantage over independence. Smaller handoffs reduce input but increase calls. The value of repairing experience is therefore distinct from the value of reusing it: memory updates require both a previous-version reference and a fresh-start reference.

</details>


### [73] [Reward Hacking and Agent Containment Failure: A Monte Carlo Study Based on the 2026 Hugging Face Incident](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32390)
*Murat Ozer,Bulent Erenay,Ibrahim Berber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文用五阶段概率模型和大规模蒙特卡洛仿真比较控制配置，结论是分层防护最有效，并建议将评估环境视为敌对区域，避免将关键资源置于代理权限范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示当具备网络能力的代理遇到薄弱隔离边界时，奖励操纵如何成为外部网络安全事件，进而量化不同控制配置对此类风险的相对影响。

Method: 构建五阶段概率风险模型（奖励操纵、越界、可用访问、持久化、探测失败），使用蒙特卡洛仿真（每种控制配置100,000次）并对四种控制配置进行比较；输入以分布形式表达不确定性，辅以独立±25%参数扰动的鲁棒性检验和敏感性分析。

Result: 仿真显示多层控制在降低外部事件概率方面效果最好；模型对代理能力、监控、授权与凭证控制高度敏感；行为类比用于解释短期策略优化，但不假设代理具有人类动机。

Conclusion: 在既定假设下，分层防护相较于单一网络隔离或监控可显著降低模拟出的外部安全事件概率；强调将可执行攻击能力的评估设为敌对安全区。

Abstract: The July 2026 intrusion into Hugging Face production infrastructure showed how reward hacking can become an external cybersecurity incident when a capable agent encounters weak containment boundaries. This study develops a probabilistic risk model linking five stages: reward hacking, containment escape, usable access, persistence, and failure of detection. A Monte Carlo simulation evaluates 100,000 runs under each of four control configurations. Input distributions represent explicit uncertainty and are used for comparative analysis rather than real-world frequency prediction. Under the stated assumptions, layered controls reduce simulated external-incident probability substantially more than network isolation or monitoring used alone, an ordering that holds under independent plus/minus 25% perturbation of every coefficient in the model across 300 draws. Sensitivity analysis shows that agent capability and weaknesses in monitoring, authorization, and credential control exert the greatest influence on modeled risk. Human temporal discounting and metric gaming provide a behavioral analogy for short-horizon optimization, but the study does not infer that AI agents experience gratification or human motivation. The results support treating cyber-capable agent evaluations as hostile security zones in which indirect egress, shared infrastructure, credentials, and evaluation artifacts must remain outside the agent's effective authority.

</details>


### [74] [Fewer Assumptions by Design: A Reusable Skill for LLM-Assisted Verus Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34886)
*Andrada-Livia Antoneac,Dorel Lucanu,Dragoş Teodor Gavriluţ*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Verus上验证Rust的双向链表很难且易依赖未经证明的假设。通过将领域知识和任务分解编码为LLM的验证“skill”，作者展示了LLM代理能够生成更强、可信度更高的DLL规格，降低人工负担与受信任基数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLLs are self-referential and hard to formalize; prior Verus workflows are tedious and risk weak specifications due to axioms/assumes. Investigate whether LLMs can produce robust specs while minimizing trusted assumptions.

Method: Compare three approaches—manual verification, property-specific verification, and an LLM agent augmented with a defined DLL-specific verification skill that encodes domain knowledge and task decomposition—using Verus to author and check specifications for doubly linked lists.

Result: The LLM agent equipped with the designed verification skill produced strong specifications for DLLs with a reduced trusted base compared to other approaches, demonstrating feasibility of automating specification synthesis for challenging self-referential structures in Verus.

Conclusion: LLM agent with a domain-specific verification skill can synthesize strong, low-trust specifications for self-referential Rust data structures (DLLs) in Verus, reducing manual effort and reliance on unproven assumptions.

Abstract: LLM-assisted Verus verification is a less tedious method to verify Rust implementations, but paired with self-referential structures, e.g., Doubly Linked Lists (DLLs)—notoriously difficult to formalise for verification—it becomes a substantially more demanding verification task.    Moreover, a specification weakness can arise when verification relies on unproven or invalidated assumptions, such as axiomatic lemmas and assume statements. We investigate whether LLM agents can synthesize strong DLL specifications while minimizing these trusted base. The analysis follows three different approaches: manual verification, property-specific verification, and a defined skill for the specific case of DLLs and certain properties of this type of data structure. The skill encodes domain knowledge and a task-decomposition strategy. We show that an LLM agent equipped with a carefully designed verification skill can generate strong, low-trust specifications for DLLs in Verus.

</details>


### [75] [SCLATE: a Substrate for Continual-Learning Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32391)
*Youngmok Jung,Sirajul Salekin,Henry Tran,Javier Movellan,Zhao Huang,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SCLATE，一个统一的事件调度与回放基座，简化长期持续学习评估并揭示记忆系统与模型间复杂交互；实验证明统一执行可高效训练/评估，并通过后训显著改进模型对记忆与 harness 的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前持续学习（continual learning）评估要求在长期多会话场景中交织任务事件与代理侧事件（如会话启动/停止、定时任务、记忆巩固等），但现有基准与训练框架通常只调度基准自有事件，导致每个基准-代理配对需实现定制的调度循环，增加工程复杂性并阻碍可比性与复现。

Method: 构建 SCLATE：1) 采用适配器模式将基准与代理的事件注入一个开放的事件调度器；2) 设计混合模拟时钟——实时推进代理计算、跳过空闲间隙，从而把月级情景压缩到小时级；3) 实现回放/rollout 引擎，通过容器内代理代理（in-container proxy）记录每次模型调用的 token 与对数概率；4) 将七个基准移植到 SCLATE，上线十种未改动的 harness 与记忆配置，对十个模型进行横向比较；5) 在同一框架下对 Qwen3.5-4B 进行后训练评估其对 harness/记忆的使用变化。

Result: SCLATE 成功将多基准与多代理配置整合到统一执行器中，实验结果包括：1) 外加记忆系统不总是优于 harness 自带的记忆；2) 不同模型对相同 harness/记忆的利用差异显著；3) Qwen3.5-4B 经后训后读取的文件行数减少 6.8 倍，SWE-bench Verified 通过率提升 16.7 个百分点，写出更丰富的记忆记录，在未见任务 MetaClaw 上准确率最多提升 11.8 个百分点；4) 混合模拟时钟使得月级场景的执行时间大幅压缩到数小时。

Conclusion: SCLATE 提供了一个统一的事件调度与回放执行基座，使未经修改的基准与代理能在单一共享时间线上互操作，从而简化长期多会话持续学习的评估与训练流程。实证表明：外加的记忆系统并不总能超越 harness 自带的记忆，模型在相同 harness/记忆下表现差异显著；通过在该框架下对 Qwen3.5-4B 进行后训，模型学会更高效地利用记忆与 harness，显著减少文件读取并提高多项任务准确率。

Abstract: Continual-learning agents are systems of models, harnesses, and memory operating over long multi-session horizons. Evaluating and training them requires interleaving tasks with agent-side events such as session stop and start, crons, and memory consolidation. Yet existing benchmarks and training frameworks schedule only the benchmark's own events, leaving each benchmark and agent pair to build a custom scheduling loop. We present SCLATE, an execution substrate where benchmarks and unmodified agents each add their events to one open event scheduler through an adapter. A hybrid simulated clock runs these events on a shared timeline, flowing in real time while the agent works and skipping idle gaps, which compresses a month-long scenario into hours. SCLATE also serves as a rollout engine that runs any agent's harness and memory unmodified, recording the tokens and log probabilities of every model call through an in-container proxy. We port seven benchmarks to SCLATE and compare ten unmodified harness and memory configurations head to head on ten models. The comparison shows that an added memory system does not reliably beat the harness's native memory and that models differ widely in how they use the same harness and memory. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through unmodified harnesses and memory systems. The model learns to use both, reading 6.8x fewer file lines with a 16.7-point higher SWE-bench Verified pass rate, and writing richer memory records, while its held-out MetaClaw accuracy rises by up to 11.8 points.

</details>


### [76] [When Valid Tool Calls Change Meaning: Formation-Consistent Dispatch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35088)
*Geonwoo Kim,Brent ByungHoon Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FCD把形成时分析与运行时执行权绑在一起，通过来源绑定的效应概括、闭目标批准和执行末端围栏，防止了调用在发布/重连/批准过程中发生的安全语义漂移，且在多次发布与实验中显示了实用性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中工具驱动的代理在模型可见接口上形成调用但由宿主选择实现，标准调度忽略描述符与处理器的关系，导致在发布、重连或延迟批准期间相同调用的安全效应发生变化，带来未察觉的权限扩大或泄露风险（即schema-epoch drift）。

Method: 提出FCD机制：由审核的配置文件生成来源绑定的、对官方声明范围效应的上界概括。采用闭目标（closed-target）批准策略，验证器将每个形成的调用作用于摘要，仅当被捕获的后继状态的效应符合调用的安全契约时才接受。引入原子准入与最终跳（final-hop）围栏以在执行链末端保持决策不变；优先保留精确来源，且被捕获的后继仅在来源退役后成为可用实现。

Result: 实验重现了该失效模式。以四个配置文件覆盖32个官方发布版：29个无需针对发布作改动，3个导致提升。一个冻结的16发布扩展匹配了独立来源oracle。在事先注册的库存比较中：FCD完成了所有3个保持私有效应的待定调用，并阻止了所有3个其缺失变为公开的调用；精确固定与发布范围拒绝可阻止全部6个调用，发布范围批准则完成全部6个但产生3个公开效应。生命周期实验表明形成捕获的证书可以跨来源退役保持有效，后续在新形成时安装相同安全证书而不扩展待定调用权限。

Conclusion: FCD（formation-consistent dispatch）通过将形成时的实现分析与执行权限绑定，消除了“schema-epoch drift”——即同一、模式有效的调用在部署/重连/延迟批准后获取不同安全影响，从而在保留来源优先权与生命周期约束的同时可靠地维持调用的安全语义。

Abstract: Tool-enabled agents form calls from model-visible interfaces, while hosts later select their implementation. Standard dispatch omits the descriptor-handler relation. An unchanged and schema-valid call can therefore acquire a different security effect during rollout, reconnect, or delayed approval. We call this failure schema-epoch drift. We present formation-consistent dispatch (FCD), which connects implementation analysis to execution authority. Reviewed profiles produce provenance-bound over-approximations of declared in-scope effects from official source. Under a closed-target approval policy, a verifier applies each formed call to a summary and captures a successor only when its effects fit the call's security contract. Atomic admission and a final-hop fence preserve this decision to the effect. The exact source retains priority, and the captured successor becomes eligible only after source retirement. Stock releases and deployment changes reproduced the failure. Four profiles covered 32 official releases: 29 required no release-specific change and three escalated. A frozen 16-release expansion matched a separate source oracle. In a preregistered stock comparison, FCD completed all three pending calls whose effect remained private and blocked all three whose omission became public. Exact pinning and release-wide denial stopped all six calls, while release-wide approval completed all six but produced three public effects. A separate lifecycle experiment carried a formation-captured certificate across source retirement. The same safe certificate installed later governed new formations without expanding the pending call's authority.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Scripted Search: Sample-Efficient Reward Discovery via Agentic Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32394)
*Minghao Li,Rui Tan,Ruihang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARBO通过把LLM作为带持久工作区的在线代理来进行黑盒奖励搜索，显著提高了在受限评估预算下的样本效率与最终控制表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低级控制中为RL设计稠密奖励函数困难，且以往用LLM自动搜索奖励的做法每次候选都需完整训练，样本效率差，限制了在复杂任务上的实用性。

Method: 提出ARBO框架：在运行时让LLM代理根据持续维护的评估历史（包括由评估oracle记录的观测：候选得分、每项训练曲线、错误回溯；以及代理自身维护的belief：诊断和下一步计划）迭代生成下一批奖励候选并通过工具查询历史，而不是一次性从固定提示生成。

Result: 在四个控制域上，在相同评估预算下，相较基线均值，ARBO在操作成功率上提升29.9%，在电网得分上提升192.8%；并通过消融研究考察各组件和backbone选择的贡献与敏感性。

Conclusion: 将LLM作为在线的、具备持久工作区的代理，用于构建黑盒的奖励函数搜索策略，可以在有限评估预算下显著提升复杂控制任务中得到的策略质量。

Abstract: Designing dense reward functions for low-level reinforcement learning (RL) control remains difficult. Recent work uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate and refine reward functions using policy-training feedback within scripted search algorithms. However, evaluating each candidate requires a full RL training run, making sample efficiency a central challenge for reward search on complex control tasks. To address this limitation, we propose an Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization (ARBO) framework, in which an LLM agent builds the search strategy at run time from an evaluation history maintained as its persistent workspace. The evaluation history comprises two components: observations maintained by the evaluation oracle, including candidate scores, per-term training curves, and error tracebacks; and an agent-maintained belief that records diagnoses and intended next steps. The agent queries both with tools and generates the next batch of reward candidates, rather than generating them in a single pass from a fixed prompt. Across four control domains, ARBO achieves gains of 29.9% in manipulation success rate and 192.8% in power-grid score over baseline means under a shared evaluation budget. Ablations examine each component's contribution and sensitivity to backbone choice.

</details>


### [78] [From Migration to Calibration: Preserving Agent Capabilities across Models, Jurisdictions, and Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35149)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“代理校准”概念化框架（信息-執行器-用户三层）与评估程序，强调保存证据、執行器修复与目标原生验证，给出阶乘式和分组报告的评估建议，实证实现尚未完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中模型替换、跨法域/市场扩展或训练后微调常导致能力流失或不满足本地合同；仅靠接口兼容不能保证能力保持或满足目标要求，因此需要系统化的校准方法和可量化评估。

Method: 主要通过概念建模与流程划分：定义三层校准职责、区分可训练策略与冻结骨干/控制器优化、区分证据验证与相对排序式优化（如DPO/GRPO），并提出目标原生执行记录、分离任务有效性审核、近平局排序审查、以及保持性/局部性报告的评估方法和试验（含阶乘设计）。

Result: 成果是方法论性建议：三层校准框架、对证据与判别优化的区分、对执行记录及审核流程的要求、以及若干具体的评估/试验设計（held-out、跨境适配、阶乘试验与分组报告）。实现与实证验证留作未来工作。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套方法论性框架，把代理在迁移/更换/跨域部署时的“校准”问题形式化为跨三个交互层（信息保存、執行器/接口（harness）适配、用户接受）的约束行为适配，目标是不降低预设能力度量并满足目标合同；并提出若干评估与试验设计建议。

Abstract: Agents need calibration when deployment conditions change: replacing a driving model, including a foundation-to-post-trained transition; crossing jurisdictions; or scaling across heterogeneous markets and sources. Interface compatibility alone does not establish capability retention or target-contract satisfaction. We formulate agent calibration as constrained behavioral adaptation across three interacting layers: information preservation, harness adaptation, and user acceptance; the layers apply to every scenario, not one-to-one to the three. The basic objective is non-degradation on prespecified capability measures while satisfying target requirements; aggregate improvement is stronger. Information calibration preserves independently validated source content still applicable to the target task. Harness calibration aligns observable artifacts at semantic checkpoints and repairs them through iteration, tool substitution, or local replanning within explicit budgets. User calibration enforces recipient-specific output contracts: templates, schemas, and section-level preferences. A global e-commerce example shows how shared standards coexist with site- and market-specific adapters and validation. We distinguish trainable policies from frozen-backbone configuration or controller optimization, and evidence verification from relative judgment and DPO/GRPO optimization. Recent harness-transfer and judge-validity studies motivate target-native execution records, separate audits of task validity and near-tie ranking, and matched target-native optimization controls. We propose held-out evaluations for model changes, cross-border adaptation, and scale, including a factorial test of source evidence and checkpoint repair and group-level reporting to prevent aggregate gains from masking local failures. This is a methodological proposal; implementation and empirical validation remain future work.

</details>


### [79] [Memory as a cache: Exact context reuse and deletion by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32395)
*Shengyao Wang,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMem architectures make transformer context a composable, editable cache via block-local encoding plus cross-attention reading, giving large efficiency and editability gains with small perplexity costs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer KV caches entangle token representations with their full prefix, preventing reuse or cheap edits. Need an architecture whose context representation is inherently a reusable, editable cache.

Method: Introduce a block-local encoder that maps each fixed-size token block to memory rows independently; use a cross-attention reader that conditions generation on the union of these memory rows. Memory composes at fixed block indices; updates (deletions) are O(b) for b-token blocks and independent of edit path.

Result: SMem retrieves distant planted needles beyond training-length windows where other positional schemes fail (exact-match 0.14-0.28 vs ≤0.02). A full cached context allows single-block computes at near-constant 3.1–6.2 ms; batched decode uses 34–38% fewer KV rows and is 1.4–1.7× faster when bandwidth-bound. Deletion is 8.5×–452× faster than suffix recomputation at large block counts. Perplexity trade-off is modest (-4.7% to +2.8%) versus matched transformers; composing SMem with RoPE reduces the gap further.

Conclusion: SMem removes prefix entanglement by construction, enabling exact cache composability and efficient edits while maintaining comparable perplexity to standard transformers.

Abstract: The KV cache of a transformer entangles every token's representation with its entire prefix: a passage encoded once cannot be reused under a different prefix or removed without recomputing everything after it, so exact cache reuse is limited to shared prefixes. We present SMem, an architecture whose context representation is a cache by construction. A block-local encoder maps each block to memory rows independently of other blocks, and a reader conditions generation on their union through cross-attention. For every parameter setting, memory composes exactly at fixed block indices, deleting a block is an exact $O(b)$ update for $b$-token blocks, and the memory state is independent of the edit path. At $4\times$ the training context, under the shared recipe, SMem retrieves planted needles beyond any trained-length window (exact match 0.14-0.28 at distances of 31 and 63 blocks), where learned-position, RoPE, and Block-Attention-style transformers all score at most 0.02. A fully cached context is served by computing one block alone at a near-constant 3.1-6.2 ms, whereas cold prefill grows with context; batched decode stores 34-38% fewer KV rows and runs 1.4-1.7$\times$ faster when bandwidth-bound; and deletion beats suffix recomputation by 8.5$\times$ at 512 blocks and 452$\times$ at 4096 blocks (32-256$\times$ the trained length, probing the cost model rather than a served regime). The cost is a perplexity gap of -4.7% to +2.8% (negative favors SMem) against a parameter-matched transformer with the same positional scheme, at 160M-1.5B on FineWeb-Edu across two recipes and a learning-rate search. SMem also composes with RoPE: at 160M and 410M the composite matches or leads the matched transformer and closes 29-59% of SMem's gap to a RoPE transformer. Dropping prefix entanglement thus keeps perplexity comparable while making the cache exactly composable and editable.

</details>


### [80] [Function Over Form: Distributional Orthogonalization in Mixture-of-Experts with Replica Expert Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32398)
*Jinfan He,Yunzhuo Liu,Kai Zhang,Weidong Han,Key,Rayying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Propose DO-loss to orthogonalize expert usage distributions and REM to balance load via replica placement and token dispatch; yields better downstream performance and similar training efficiency on tested MoE setups.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE scaling decouples parameter count and compute but suffers from expert collapse and representation redundancy, causing underutilized capacity; need a routing-aware regularizer plus system-aware balancing to activate and specialize experts effectively without harming throughput.

Method: Introduce Distributional Orthogonalization Loss (DO-loss) that models each expert’s token assignment history as a high-dimensional binary load signature and penalizes signature overlap to encourage specialization; introduce Replica Expert Mechanism (REM) that applies a two-tiered balancing strategy—global-batch-level replica placement and micro-batch-level real-time token dispatch—to improve load balancing and system efficiency.

Result: Empirical evaluations on MoE models (reported for configurations labeled 4.8BA0.5B and 30BA3B) show improvements over evaluated routing algorithms on downstream tasks, with comparable training efficiency.

Conclusion: DO-loss and REM jointly improve expert utilization in MoE models by penalizing overlap in expert token-usage signatures and by balancing load with replica placement and micro-batch dispatching, leading to better downstream performance while keeping training efficiency comparable.

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs increasingly relies on MoE architectures to decouple active computation from total parameter count. However, the efficacy of MoE is often constrained by expert collapse and representation redundancy, both leading to underutilization of model capacity. To address these challenges, this paper proposes Distributional Orthogonalization Loss (DO-loss), an auxiliary regularization that shifts the focus from static weight diversity to dynamic routing behavior. By representing each expert's token assignment history as a high-dimensional binary load signature, DO-loss penalizes signature overlap to prevent expert collapse while encouraging functional specialization. To align this algorithmic design with system efficiency, we further introduce the Replica Expert Mechanism (REM), which improves load balancing through a two-tiered strategy: adjusting replica expert placement at the global-batch level and performing real-time token dispatching at the micro-batch level. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms the evaluated routing algorithms on downstream tasks for both 4.8BA0.5B and 30BA3B MoE models, while maintaining comparable training efficiency.

</details>


### [81] [Opening LLM Judges: Recovering Preference Signals Beyond the Final Verdict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32407)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 错判多数情况下是输出未反映而非内部缺失——小探针与激活干预能大量恢复正确判断；在部署评估器前用表面特征可预测性做诊断，能预测是否值得读取内部信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当大型语言模型作为‘判官’给出错误评判时，这种错误是由于模型内部没有相关信息，还是信息存在但未体现在输出（判决）中；以及如何判断并恢复该信息以提高评估可靠性。

Method: 在64个可公开权重的评估器与14个数据集上进行大规模实验；构建对抗数据集LLMBar使表面上更好答案其实更差；对41个判定器进行因果干预（中途编辑激活）并训练小型探针在中间激活上预测人类标签，同时对位置、长度等表面特征进行残差化以评估其影响。

Result: 在LLMBar上，50个判定器的输出与人工标签一致率仅0.456；但在相同判定器的中间激活上训练的小探针能达到0.846（残差化表面特征后0.686，随机标签基线0.507）。这一区别在多个基准与模型族中普遍存在，但可由一个预先计算的“表面特征可预测性”分数预测（Spearman rho=0.90）。在按单条回答评分的规则型任务中，内部读取无明显优势。激活中途编辑能即时改变判决并定位传递位置/长度偏差的通路。使用恢复信号可在相同标签预算下更好地识别判错样本并用于偏好学习数据标注。

Conclusion: 当判定器给出错误判断时，并不总是因为模型内部缺乏正确信息：很多情况下正确判断的信息存在于模型的中间激活中但未反映在输出上。一个简单的内部探针或激活干预能大幅恢复这一信息，从而改进判决或标记判错概率。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate model outputs, but their verdicts can be unreliable: a judge may favor the worse answer for its position, length, or other surface features. When a judge is wrong, is the information needed to judge correctly absent from the model, or present in its internal representations but not reflected in the output? We study this across 64 open-weight evaluators and 14 datasets, including causal interventions on 41 judges (editing activations mid-run to see whether the verdict changes). On LLMBar, built so the superficially better answer is the worse one, the verdicts of 50 judges agree with human labels only 0.456 of the time, even after averaging both answer orders. Yet a small probe on the same judges' activations, with no weight updates, reaches 0.846, and 0.686 once surface features such as length and position are residualized out (0.507 with shuffled labels). The gap holds across eight benchmarks and model families, but is not universal: a score of how well surface features alone predict the human label, computed before any probe is trained, predicts the size of the gain (Spearman rho = 0.90). On rubric tasks that score one answer at a time, leaving no surface cue to exploit, reading the internals gives no advantage. The interventions also show that editing activations mid-network already changes the verdict, before it can be read off directly, and locate the pathways carrying position and length bias. At the same label budget, the recovered signal lets a judge flag cases where it is likely wrong and yields better labels for preference learning. A wrong verdict, then, does not mean the judge lacks the information, and a simple diagnostic shows when it is worth recovering.

</details>


### [82] [Carnator: Fast Text-to-Video Generation with Generation-Native Compatibility-Guided Cross-Request Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32420)
*Xingkun Yin,Xuebin Tang,Mingkun Xu,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于模型内部演化状态的早期签名与风险感知判定的跨请求重用框架Carnator，能够在保存质量的同时显著提升视频扩散模型的推理效率，最多达到2.17×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer在生成质量上优越，但迭代去噪导致高推理延迟。现有加速多聚焦于单次请求冗余，或通过粗粒度语义相似性判定跨请求可复用性，易导致兼容性误判或加速不足。

Method: 对正在进行的扩散推理进行轻量早期探针以构建Early Signature；用该签名评估历史轨迹的可复用性并作出风险感知的接受/拒绝决策；同时局部化目标专有计算，指导历史潜在轨迹与稀疏注意力连接的联合重用；结合缓存机制在多请求场景下复用计算。

Result: 在三种文本到视频（T2V）模型骨干上，Carnator较现有跨请求基线实现了更高的缓存命中与端到端加速（在更慎重的缓存接受策略下仍达最多2.17×的加速），并在生成质量上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Carnator通过从扩散模型的内部演化状态提取早期签名（Early Signature），并基于风险感知的兼容性判定在跨请求层面进行选择性重用，从而在保持生成质量的前提下显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers produce high-quality videos, yet iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency, limiting interactive and large-scale serving. Most existing acceleration methods focus on individual requests, thereby restricting efficiency gains to redundancy within a single generation trajectory. Recent cross-request reuse offers an additional source of savings, but existing approaches often infer reusability from coarse semantic similarity. This conflates semantic relatedness with generation-level computational compatibility, so aggressive reuse may accept incompatible historical computation while conservative reuse leaves substantial acceleration unrealized. We present \emph{Carnator}, a cross-request acceleration framework that addresses this challenge by extracting and using generation-native compatibility evidence directly from the model's evolving internal states. Specifically, \emph{Carnator} performs a lightweight early probe to construct an Early Signature from internal diffusion states, assessing reuse validity through risk-aware compatibility decisions. The same evidence characterizes reuse scope by localizing target-specific computation and guiding joint reuse of historical latent trajectories and sparse attention connectivity. Across three text-to-video backbones, Carnator consistently achieves higher cache-hit end-to-end acceleration than the evaluated cross-request baselines despite more selective cache acceptance, reaching up to 2.17$\times$ speedup while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [83] [MergeHEIR: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations as the Tax of Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32422)
*Jinyu Li,Hao Fang,Zhiming Zhang,Jiawei Kong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MergeHEIR: constrain post-merge adaptation by projecting updates into layer-wise null spaces derived from merged-model activations to suppress hallucination increase while retaining expert capabilities; supported by theoretical guarantees and extensive empirical gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Model merging consolidates task-specialized expert models but systematically increases hallucination in merged checkpoints. Directly applying hallucination-reduction adaptation to the merged model harms inherited capabilities, creating a need for methods that mitigate hallucination without erasing expertise.

Method: Post-merge adaptation framework that uses small task-specific calibration sets to collect activations from the merged checkpoint, performs SVD per layer to get null-space projectors, and periodically projects accumulated parameter displacement into those null spaces to prevent corruption of inherited expertise during adaptation.

Result: Theoretically: guarantees on minimum-distortion and maximum-dimensionality of preserved subspaces, characterization of adaptation-retention trade-off via thresholding, and perturbation bounds that extend beyond finite calibration data. Empirically: across 24 paired comparisons over three MLLM configurations and eight merging methods, MergeHEIR consistently reduces hallucination while largely preserving inherited expertise, outperforming unconstrained adaptation in the hallucination-retention trade-off.

Conclusion: MergeHEIR reduces the hallucination increase (merging tax) introduced by model merging while largely preserving the specialized expertise of constituent models, achieving a better trade-off between hallucination mitigation and retention of capabilities.

Abstract: Model merging consolidates task-specialized experts into a single deployable model. However, we show that such capability consolidation incurs a merging tax of increased hallucination: across 8 model-merging methods, every merged checkpoint exhibits a higher hallucination rate than the average of its constituent experts. An intuitive approach is to adapt existing hallucination-mitigation methods to the post-merge model, yet this unconstrained adaptation disrupts inherited capabilities, creating a tension between hallucination mitigation and expertise retention. To tackle this challenge, we introduce MergeHEIR, a post-merge adaptation framework designed to reduce this merging tax while preserving expertise inherited from initial experts. Using small expert-task calibration sets, MergeHEIR constructs layer-wise null-space projectors via SVD from task-specific activations collected from the merged checkpoint, and periodically projects the accumulated post-merge displacement onto the resulting null spaces to preserve inherited expertise. Theoretically, we establish minimum-distortion and maximum-dimensionality guarantees, characterize the threshold-controlled adaptation-retention trade-off, and extend perturbation guarantees beyond finite calibration data. Across 24 paired comparisons spanning three MLLM configurations and 8 model-merging methods, MergeHEIR consistently mitigates hallucination while largely preserving inherited expertise, demonstrating a more favorable hallucination-retention trade-off.

</details>


### [84] [PluginRSI: Recursive Improvement of Agent Harnesses with Reusable Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32423)
*Yaorui Shi,Yuchun Miao,Yuxin Chen,Jiayuan Zhang,Yueqing Sun,Xierui Song,Xiang Wang,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把harness拆成可独立改进并复用的插件、构建共享插件库并以插件为单位演化，PluginRSI实现了更强、可迁移并能持续改进的代理控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过搜索完整程序来优化harness的方法导致机制难以隔离、复用与累积。需要一种模块化、可重用的机制以实现持续改进与跨模型迁移。

Method: 提出PluginRSI：将harness表示为一组可重组的原子插件；每轮独立改进单个插件并将其加入共享库，再从库中重组生成新harness进行评估和选择，从而以插件为单位演化整体策略。

Result: 在软件工程、命令行交互和问答三类任务上优于现有harness优化方法；进化得到的harness在未经额外优化的其他求解模型上仍保有优势；积累的插件库能加速后续优化并在未见任务上更快更好地收敛。

Conclusion: 将提示/控制框架（harness）解构为可原子化的插件并逐步积累一个共享插件库，能有效提高语言模型代理的表现、提升可迁移性并加速对新任务的优化收敛。

Abstract: The harness surrounding a language model is a central determinant of agent performance. Recent methods optimize harnesses by searching over complete programs, where individual mechanisms are difficult to isolate and reuse. We introduce PluginRSI, which represents a harness as a composition of atomized plugins and organizes harness evolution around these plugins. Individual plugins are improved independently and accumulated in a shared library, then recombined into new harnesses at each iteration. PluginRSI improves over existing harness optimization methods across software engineering, command-line interaction, and question-answering tasks. The resulting harnesses retain their advantage when transferred to other solver models without further optimization. The evolved plugin library accelerates subsequent optimization from the initial harness, which helps faster and higher convergence on unseen tasks. These results show that accumulating reusable mechanisms provides an effective basis for continued harness improvement.

</details>


### [85] [Authorization Closure Graph: Minimal Repair for LLM Agents with Evolving User Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32428)
*Qingzhuo Wang,CaiYi Wang,Jinglu Meng,Ruiyang Qin,Kunyu Peng,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入ACG，一种基于版本化依赖图的授权管理框架，可在指令部分修改时有选择地保留或撤销授权，计算最小修复以减少不必要的授权请求，从而提升安全与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在执行会改变状态的操作时需要用户授权，但当指令部分修改时缺乏原则性机制来局部更新先前授权，导致授权可能过期或频繁重复请求。

Method: 提出Authorization-Closure-Graph (ACG)框架，将授权及其依赖建模为演化的、带版本的状态图；在修订发生时对受影响的授权进行选择性失效，并通过图算法计算最小修复（missing evidence/authority）；在多个LLM和任务上评估其对安全性和成功率的改善。

Result: 在三种先进LLM及两个自然任务上，ACG持续提高动作安全率和任务成功率，并提供了可复现的代码仓库。

Conclusion: ACG使得LLM代理在指令修订时能够选择性地撤销受影响的授权、保留不受影响的授权，并计算最小修复以仅请求缺失的证据或权限，从而减少过期授权和不必要的授权请求，提升执行安全性与任务成功率。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents increasingly perform state-changing actions that require user authorization. Yet existing approaches do not provide a principled mechanism for selectively updating prior authorization when only part of an instruction changes. To this end, we propose an Authorization-Closure-Graph (ACG)-based framework that represents authorization and its dependencies as an evolving, versioned state. ACG selectively invalidates authority affected by a revision while preserving unaffected portions of the authorization state, and computes a minimal repair that identifies only the missing evidence or authority required for execution. This enables agents to adapt to revised instructions while avoiding stale authority and unnecessary authorization requests. We evaluate ACG across three advanced LLMs in two natural tasks, and ACG consistently improves action safety rate and task success rate. Code is available at https://github.com/weiliang822/ACG.

</details>


### [86] [PrismQuant: Optimal Null-Space Rotations for Grouped Quantizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32429)
*Yanlong Chen,Yining Chen,Song Zhang,Amirhossein Habibian,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrismQuant is a quantizer-aware rotation method with a provable closed-form alignment solution that yields strong INT4 quantization accuracy and efficient deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Observation that smaller activation outliers do not guarantee better low-bit quantization — the alignment of activation energy with the quantizer subspace matters for grouped asymmetric INT4 quantization.

Method: Formulate rotation design as a Ky Fan trace maximization for aligning leading activation eigenspace with quantizer group subspace; derive a closed-form optimal solution; implement rotation via compact Householder transforms and compact-WY representation for efficient, gradient-free construction applicable at foldable and online sites; introduce a predictive range law linking unaligned activation energy and group size to quantization variation.

Result: Empirical gains across Llama, Qwen, Mistral models up to 70B and a 30B MoE: SOTA under W4A4KV4 on Llama-3.2-3B; Llama-3.1-70B achieves 3.85 perplexity and 72.46% avg zero-shot (0.22 pp below FP); deployment study on Llama-3.1-8B shows 1.51x prefill and 1.22x decode speedups, 56.34% lower peak memory vs FP16, small decode latency overhead; code released.

Conclusion: PrismQuant improves low-bit (INT4) activation quantization by rotating activations so their leading eigenspace aligns with the constant-group subspace of asymmetric grouped quantizers, yielding state-of-the-art accuracy and practical deployment speed/memory benefits.

Abstract: Smaller activation outliers do not necessarily imply better low-bit quantization: their alignment with the quantizer matters. We introduce PrismQuant, a quantizer-aware rotation framework that aligns the leading activation eigenspace with the constant group subspace of asymmetric grouped INT4. The affine offsets represent the energy in this subspace without widening the range within the group. We formulate rotation design as a Ky Fan trace maximization and derive a closed-form solution that is provably optimal for this alignment objective. Compact Householder transformations and their compact-WY representation enable gradient-free construction and efficient application at both foldable and online sites. A predictive range law further connects unaligned activation energy and group size to quantization-relevant variation. Experiments on Llama, Qwen, and Mistral span dense models up to 70B parameters and a 30B mixture-of-experts model. Under W4A4KV4, PrismQuant sets the state of the art on Llama-3.2-3B among the compared methods in both perplexity and accuracy. On Llama-3.1-70B, it attains 3.85 perplexity and 72.46% average zero-shot accuracy, only 0.22 percentage points below full precision. In the deployment study on Llama-3.1-8B, our optimized implementation achieves 1.51x prefill and 1.22x CUDA Graph decode speedups over matched FP16 baselines, with 56.34% lower decode peak memory and only 2.35% additional Graph decode latency over Hadamard. Code is available at https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PrismQuant.

</details>


### [87] [Multi-Agent System Search via Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32430)
*Beicheng Xu,Bowen Fan,Weitong Qian,Lingching Tung,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASPO通过能力边界导向的查询采样与子结构级回报，在PPO框架下实现了对查询级MAS搜索的高效学习，显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计MAS角色、提示和通信结构成本高且需领域专长；现有方法通常在固定训练集上以工作流级别回报训练，忽视查询间不同的可学习潜力且无法定位改进的子结构。

Method: 设计了一种基于强化学习的策略优化流程：引入自适应查询选择机制（AQSM）以聚焦策略能力边界的查询；配套的发现机制在难题上扩大体系结构探索；对每个动作的后代子图计算输出质量增益作为子结构级回报，用以指导近端策略优化（PPO）强化有益的结构改进并抑制冗余或有害计算。

Result: 在涵盖数学推理、通用问答与代码生成的六个基准上，ASPO在与12个基线方法比较中每个基准均获得第一名，表明其在多类任务上的稳健性与有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的ASPO框架通过优先训练处于能力边界的查询并使用子结构级别的回报实现了更有效的多智能体系统（MAS）构建策略，能够更精准地区分可学查询与需拓展探索的硬查询，从而显著提升查詢级别的MAS搜索效果。

Abstract: LLMs enable multi-agent systems (MAS) to tackle complex tasks, but manually designing agent roles, prompts, and communication structures requires substantial expertise and effort. This motivates learning policies that construct query-specific MAS from execution reward. Existing approaches typically train these policies by repeatedly traversing a fixed set of training queries and assigning rewards at the workflow level. However, this overlooks differences in queries' evolving learning potential and obscures which substructures improve solution quality. In this paper, we propose Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization (ASPO), a RL framework for query-level MAS search. ASPO introduces an Adaptive Query-Selection Mechanism (AQSM) that focuses training on queries at the policy's competence boundary: those it can solve but not yet reliably. A complementary discovery mechanism widens architectural exploration for hard queries, helping distinguish insufficient exploration from operator capability limits. Beyond query selection, ASPO introduces substructure-level rewards that measure output-quality gains within each action's descendant subgraph. These rewards guide proximal policy optimization to reinforce useful architectural refinements and discourage redundant or harmful computation. Together, these mechanisms prioritize learnable queries and provide fine-grained feedback for learning effective MASs. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, general question answering, and code generation, ASPO ranks first on every benchmark against twelve baselines.

</details>


### [88] [From Latents to Wires: Surgical Post-Editing on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32434)
*Jiankai Jin,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zhao Liu,Wenzhuo Xu,Dongdong Yang,Deyue Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: L2W 通过 J-lens + CGCC 在权重可得的大模型上实现可组合、外科式的语义后编辑，能去掉命名的不良行为而尽量保持原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋予模型持有者在训练后有能力精确删除不想要的、可命名的语义行为，而无需完全重训或破坏其它能力。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：用基于雅可比的 J-lens 做组件归因与定位，随后用 Counterexample-Guided Causal Cut (CGCC) 迭代‘关闭’暴露出贡献的组件并在必要时‘重开’以保留能力。对 text-to-image 模型还演示了后编辑的复合应用。

Result: 在受控植入水印实验中成功移除水印并定位到修改区域；在三种模型配置上共九次运行成功移除模型自称身份，以及三次运行移除成人内容拒绝，每次无留存目标；并示范了对图像模型的连锁两步后编辑。

Conclusion: L2W 能在已训练的大模型上对“命名语义目标”（如模型身份或水印）执行外科式删除，去除指定语义表达同时尽可能保留其他能力。

Abstract: Given a large language model (LLM), can whoever holds the weights name a semantic target (e.g., the model's identity), locate the model components that produce it, and edit them so that the target no longer appears while other capability is preserved? We call such an edit on a trained model a post-edit. We present L2W (latents to wires), a framework that performs surgical post-edits for named semantic targets. For localization, L2W uses Jacobian lens (J-lens) attribution to score components against the semantic target. For surgical removal, because LLM mechanisms are redundant (i.e., a semantic target may have multiple components producing it), L2W runs Counterexample-Guided Causal Cut (CGCC) until the target no longer appears. CGCC first cumulatively closes model components, treating each surviving expression of the target as a counterexample that exposes the next components to close, and then reopens some of them to preserve capability. In a controlled experiment with an implanted behavioural watermark, L2W removes the watermark, and its localization lands on the model region the implant changed. Across three model configurations, L2W removes model-metadata (e.g., identity) self-claims in all nine runs, and adult-content refusal in all three, with no held-out target residual. L2W further composes two post-edits on a text-to-image model: one removes the refusal of requested nudity, and a second removes the nude rendering the first exposes. The results support post-editing as a complement to post-training: post-training installs preferred behaviours, and post-editing removes named unwanted ones.

</details>


### [89] [Controllable GNN Explanations via Multi-Metric Preference Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32436)
*Rachit Verma,Yashraj J. Deshmukh,Anirban Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A controllable multi-objective optimizer for GNN explanations that balances fidelity, motif-based interpretability, and stability; empirically improves fidelity on several datasets, offers motif-importance estimates, and studies metric tradeoffs and runtime behavior.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing GNN explanation methods mostly optimize fidelity subject to sparsity, neglecting interpretability (familiar motif patterns) and stability under input perturbations. For trust and bias mitigation in high-stakes domains, explanations should be both human-interpretable and robust, so a method that jointly optimizes these aspects is needed.

Method: Formulates explanation generation as an optimization combining fidelity (fit to GNN predictions), a motif-similarity/interpretablity term (encouraging explanations that match motifs from a provided library), a stability/robustness term (penalizing sensitivity to structural perturbations), and sparsity constraints; exposes scalar weights to control the relative importance of these terms; evaluates on multiple graph classification datasets against a SOTA baseline, reports fidelity, runtime, and studies motif-importance and metric tradeoff surfaces.

Result: On MUTAG and PROTEINS the method achieves higher fidelity than the compared baseline across all tested explanation budgets; on BA-2Motif it outperforms at larger budgets. The method is faster when required explanations are very small. It can rank/identify important motifs from a motif library and use this to further refine explanations. The paper also maps tradeoff surfaces among fidelity, interpretability, and stability and shows these surfaces depend on the motif library choice.

Conclusion: The paper proposes a multi-objective explanation optimizer for Graph Neural Networks that jointly targets fidelity, motif-based interpretability, and stability, exposing trade-off weights as user-controllable parameters. Empirically it yields higher-fidelity explanations than a state-of-the-art baseline on MUTAG and PROTEINS across budgets and on BA-2Motif at larger budgets, runs faster for small explanation budgets, and can quantify and exploit motif importance from a motif library to improve explanations. It also characterizes tradeoff surfaces between metrics and shows dependence on motif-library design.

Abstract: Mechanisms for generating GNN explanations are crucial for building trust and mitigating biases in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), especially in high-stakes scenarios. Most current methods optimize only for fidelity under the sparsity constraint. However, this discounts the need for interpretable explanations (those that consist of familiar motif patterns) and stable explanations (those that remain unchanged under structural perturbations). We propose a novel approach that optimizes GNN explanations across these metrics, exposing their relative weighing as a control. Experiments on various real-world datasets, including MUTAG, BA-2Motif, BAMultiShapes, and PROTEINS, suggest that our method produces higher-fidelity explanations than a state-of-the-art baseline on MUTAG and PROTEINS across all evaluated budgets, and on BA-2Motif at larger budgets, while being faster in the regime of small explanation budgets. We also explore how, given an input motif library containing standard motifs for the corresponding domain, the method can be used to determine the relative importance of those motifs in generating the explanations, and how this information can be used to further improve the quality of the output explanations. We also examine the relationship between different metrics through their induced tradeoff surface, and explore its dependence on the nature of the motif library.

</details>


### [90] [ForkLeft: Entropy-First Rollouts for Prefix-Aligned Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32448)
*Junming Liu,Jicheng Wang,Yifeng He,Hao Chen,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ForkLeft通过将学生rollout与教师监督分离，解决了自回归教师与DLM学生之间的条件不匹配，使DLM在不牺牲并行生成的情况下获得NTP式推理能力并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归教师基于左侧前缀预测，而扩散语言模型可在两侧条件下生成，二者在条件集上存在根本不匹配，直接蒸馏效果受限。

Method: 提出ForkLeft蒸馏框架：训练时先让DLM学生进行以熵为先的rollout，提交不确定位置以暴露潜在的‘分叉’；固定学生产生的前缀，在相同上下文下用自回归NTP教师进行蒸馏，并根据答案正确性选择监督来源；推理时学生回到其原生的置信度优先并行解码。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base教师下，ForkLeft让Efficient-DLM-4B在10个基准上均有提升，MATH500从72.60%提升到79.60%；增益随教师能力增长而放大；在SDAR-4B上仅500次更新即可泛化；同尺度的4B和8B蒸馏模型在7个基准上超过已发表的SDAR-Chat和OPDLM。

Conclusion: ForkLeft能够在保留DLM原生并行解码的前提下，通过特定的蒸馏流程让DLM学习到自回归下一词预测（NTP）风格的推理能力，从而显著提升在数学/推理类基准上的表现。

Abstract: Autoregressive Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has enabled strong reasoning capabilities in language models, while Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer flexible token orders and parallel generation. We ask whether DLMs can acquire NTP-style reasoning through distillation without giving up their native generation process. Direct distillation, however, faces a fundamental mismatch: an autoregressive teacher predicts from a left prefix, whereas a DLM can condition on tokens on both sides. We introduce ForkLeft, a distillation framework that resolves this mismatch by separating the student's rollout from teacher supervision. During training, the student first performs entropy-first rollouts that commit uncertain positions and expose potential forks. We then fix the resulting student prefix and distill an NTP teacher under the same context, with answer correctness determining the supervision source. At inference, the student returns to its native confidence-first parallel decoding. With Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, ForkLeft improves Efficient-DLM-4B on all ten benchmarks, raising MATH500 from 72.60% to 79.60% and consistently outperforming three alternative designs. The gains scale with teacher strength and generalize to SDAR-4B with only $500$ updates. At matched scale, the distilled 4B and 8B students exceed the published SDAR-Chat and OPDLM models on seven benchmarks, showing that DLMs can learn NTP-style reasoning without sacrificing native parallel generation. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [91] [PULSE: Identifying Demonstration-Utility Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32469)
*Chenduo Hao,Chuanbao Gao,Pinjun Zeng,Jingze Zhu,Chonghan Liu,Zidong Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用SAE在模型内部定位稀疏“效用特征”，据此进行示例排序与检索，能比基于外部相似度的方法更好地挑选示例并提升多类任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有示例选择多依赖查询—示例相似度等外部准则，但这些准则忽略模型内部表征：外观相似的示例可能激活不同内部特征并导致不同下游行为，因此需要直接定位模型内部与效用相关的特征。

Method: 基于稀疏自动编码器（SAE），用一个小的带标签发现集采样候选示例集，衡量在目标模型下的相对零射实用性（utility），计算每个SAE特征坐标的激活差与效用差的对齐得分；取前若干正负坐标构成稀疏的效用定位向量，既可作为有符号打分用于控制的整集合排序，又可通过幅值生成特征相关性掩码用于可扩展的池级检索（PULSE-Retriever）。

Result: 在分类、生成、推理基准上分别优于最强基线约2–3个百分点准确率、0.6–0.9 BLEU-4和3.2点EM；特征检查和跨数据集实验表明定位到的特征编码了任务相关且受数据集条件影响的模式，同时存在部分跨数据集的效用可迁移性。

Conclusion: PULSE能够在模型内部找到与示例效用相关的稀疏特征坐标，并将其用于更有效的示例选择，从而提升少量下游任务的零样本/少样本性能。

Abstract: In-context learning is highly sensitive to demonstration choice, yet most methods select demonstrations using external query-demonstration similarity. Such criteria can miss model-specific signals: Similar demonstrations may activate different internal features and downstream behaviors. We introduce PULSE (Paired Utility Localization over Sparse Encodings), an SAE-based framework for identifying model-internal features associated with demonstration utility and using them for demonstration selection. Using a small labeled discovery set, PULSE samples candidate demonstration sets, measures their zero-shot-relative utility under the target model, and scores SAE features by how their activation differences align with utility differences. The top positive and negative coordinates form a sparse utility-localization vector. We use this vector in two complementary ways: as a signed score for controlled complete-set ranking, and as PULSE-Retriever, which converts its magnitude into a feature-relevance mask for scalable pool-scale retrieval. Across classification, generation, and reasoning benchmarks, PULSE-Retriever improves over the strongest baseline by 2-3 accuracy points, 0.6-0.9 BLEU-4, and 3.2 exact-match points, respectively, while controlled ranking validates the identified features encode a predictive set-level utility signal. Feature inspection and cross-dataset experiments suggest that the identified features capture task-relevant, dataset-conditioned patterns, yet retain utility signals that partially transfer across datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/aohenuo/PULSE.

</details>


### [92] [From Outcomes to Strategies: Learning Strategy Utility for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32482)
*Ruikang Zhang,Xiao An,Xuli Shen,Jiaxing Sun,Xiaoyi Yu,Jin Zeng,Jiang Wu,Tong Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce SURE to learn relative strategy utility via pairwise preferences and a frozen Strategy Reward Model; integrating this into GRPO leads to consistent pass@1 improvements and compute savings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Terminal correctness alone fails to assess high-level strategies (e.g., theorem selection, subgoal decomposition) independently of their execution; need a method to quantify strategy quality under a fixed executor.

Method: Separate high-level strategies from detailed reasoning; collect strategy-conditioned rollouts and teacher-generated contrast pairs; train a Strategy Reward Model on pairwise preferences to predict relative utility; freeze this model during RL and score extracted strategies, combining that score with correctness and format rewards in a sequence-level GRPO objective.

Result: SURE improves average pass@1 by 1.87%, 2.64%, and 2.93% across three policy backbones, matches or outperforms stronger reward baselines, and requires substantially less GRPO-stage compute.

Conclusion: SURE learns a relative strategy utility model and integrates it into RL (GRPO) to improve downstream problem-solving. It yields modest but consistent gains in pass@1 and competitive accuracy with lower compute during the GRPO stage.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has substantially improved mathematical reasoning. However, terminal correctness alone provides limited insight into the quality of high-level strategies, such as theorem selection and subgoal decomposition, when considered separately from their subsequent execution. This paper studies strategy utility, which is defined as the likelihood that a strategy supports a correct downstream solution under a given executor. We introduce SURE, a framework for learning and leveraging relative strategy utility. In this framework, high-level strategies are separated from their detailed reasoning. Based on the pairwise preferences constructed from strategy-conditioned rollouts and teacher-generated contrasts, a Strategy Reward Model is learned to estimate relative strategy utility. During reinforcement learning, the frozen reward model reads only the extracted strategy, whose score is combined with the correctness and format rewards in a sequence-level GRPO objective. Compared with outcome-and-format GRPO baselines, experiments show that SURE improves average pass@1 by 1.87%, 2.64%, and 2.93% across three policy backbones. Our method also achieves competitive or better accuracy than stronger reward baselines while requiring substantially lower GRPO-stage compute.

</details>


### [93] [Separating Diagnosis from Disease Representation: Dual-View EEG Learning with Neural-Dynamics-Guided Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32483)
*Jiaying Wang,Shouqian Shi,Yutong Chen,Xu Yang,Jie Chen,Xingyu Pan,Lei Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMD-EEG 用“固定头皮光谱专家+源空间低秩稀疏变形”二视图架构，实现可读的解剖-频率-滞后病态表示并在多种脑病上取得良好分类与迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环神经调控需要个体化、结构化的脑态表示（具体到区域、频段和时间尺度），而传统传感器空间与源空间方法各有利弊：传感器空间保留诊断信号但缺乏解剖信息，源空间提供解剖但可能损失预测性能，后验归因又与预测分离。为此作者把诊断与解剖表示分离建模并在决策层融合。

Method: 提出 Dual-view Multiscale Deformation for EEG (DMD-EEG)：一端固定为头皮光谱专家负责诊断信号（sensor-space），另一端在 46 个 ROI ×5 频段×4 滞后尺度上，以健康神经动力学先验为基线，用低秩+稀疏的迭代变形来建模病理源态（source-space）。两分支仅在决策层融合，来源状态被显式定义为可读的 ROI-频率-滞后坐标。

Result: 在三类疾病（MDD、FEP、PD）上进行了验证：决策级融合在 MDD 和 FEP 上达到与最强单一分支相当的性能，在 PD 上优于源分支；FEP 中源分支贡献最大且变形作用显著。高显著性 ROI-频率-滞后坐标对应已知病理回路，且 MDD 表示可按秩迁移至使用不同电极布局的未见队列。

Conclusion: DMD-EEG 将传感器空间的光谱判别与源空间的病理性低秩稀疏变形表示分离并在决策层融合，从而在保留强诊断信号的同时提供可读的解剖-频率-滞后状态。该方法在 MDD、FEP、PD 数据集中表现良好：在 MDD 和 FEP 上与最佳单一分支匹配，在 PD 上超过源分支；源空间的高显著性坐标与已知疾病回路一致，且 MDD 状态可部分迁移至不同电极编排的队列。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based closed-loop neuromodulation calls for a subject-specific structured state, as opposed to a single disease probability, specifying which brain regions are deviant, at which frequencies, and at which lags. Sensor-space models keep the strongest diagnostic evidence without anatomy, source-space models give anatomy at a loss of predictive signal, and post-hoc attributions stay outside the prediction. We separate the two instead of forcing them into one representation, and propose DMD-EEG (Dual-view Multiscale Deformation for EEG), which keeps a fixed scalp spectral expert for diagnosis and models the source-space disease-related representation as a low-rank, sparse, iterative deformation of a healthy neural-dynamics prior in a $46$-region-of-interest (ROI) $\times$ $5$-frequency $\times$ $4$-lag (autocorrelation-timescale) space. The two experts meet only at a fixed decision level, so the source state is architecturally separate from the scalp expert. Across major depressive disorder (MDD), first-episode psychosis (FEP), and Parkinson's disease (PD), decision-level fusion matches the strongest single expert on MDD and FEP and exceeds the source branch on PD. On FEP the source expert is the strongest branch, the task where the deformation contributes most. The source state is an explicit ROI-frequency-lag attribution defined in a shared source coordinate system across montages, which we treat as an anatomically-coordinated predictive representation whose coordinates are directly readable and hypothesis-generating. The highest-saliency coordinates align with established disease circuitry (fronto-limbic-temporal regions in MDD, motor-cortex beta in PD), and the MDD state transfers by rank to an unseen cohort recorded with a different montage.

</details>


### [94] [Towards Scalable Data Diversification for Language Model Pretraining via Leverage Score Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32484)
*Zailin Ma,Quzhe Huang,Yujun Li,Congyuan Rao,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lev 用杠杆分数进行可扩展的逐步多样化采样，在速度与多样性-质量折中上优于现有方法，并在Web与代码域上带来实际下游收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语料筛选存在质量与多样性之间的基本矛盾：纯粹的质量过滤会导致多样性崩溃（排斥少数域数据），而现有分散化方法要么间接作用、要么计算代价高昂。目标是设计既高效又能保证域多样性的采样策略。

Method: 提出了一种基于杠杆分数（leverage scores）的迭代采样方法：对数据进行嵌入，计算样本相对于已选集合的杠杆分数，用以衡量每个样本对扩增行列式体积（determinantal volume）的贡献，从而选择能最大扩展体积的样本。关键实现避免了昂贵的协方差矩阵重算，保证了可扩展性。

Result: 实证上，Lev 在速度上比基线快至 72×，在 Vendi 多样性评分上比强分散基线 DiSF 提升 9.2%。在 CommonCrawl 的 Web 数据选择上，七个下游任务的准确率最多提高 1.31%。在代码域（StarCoderData）上，所选子集将 bits-per-byte 降低 3.08% 比 DiSF 更优。同时发现质量过滤（如 DCLM-fastText）在跨域（向代码内容）会丧失足够的数据，导致代码性能下降。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：Leverage Score Sampling（Lev）通过基于杠杆分数的逐步选择策略，在不需反复重算协方差矩阵的情况下有效增加嵌入数据的行列式体积，从而在可扩展性和多样性间取得良好折中。Lev在多项基准上显著加速选择过程、提升数据多样性（Vendi 分数）并改善若干下游任务与代码建模的性能，表明在预训练数据筛选中应将多样性意识融入质量过滤流程。

Abstract: Data selection for language model pretraining faces a fundamental tension between quality and diversity. While quality filtering is empirically effective, it often induces diversity collapse: by favoring texts similar to high-quality reference corpora (e.g., educational or QA-style data), it systematically excludes valuable data from underrepresented domains. In contrast, diversified selection preserves domain balance and encourages robust downstream performance, yet existing methods either focus on coverage-oriented objectives that indirectly enhance diversity, or directly optimize for diversity via costly covariance matrix recomputation that limits scalability. To address these issues, we introduce \textbf{Leverage Score Sampling (Lev)}, which iteratively selects samples that maximally expand the determinantal volume of the embedded data via leverage scores, a computationally efficient criterion that eliminates matrix recomputation and enables scalable selection. Empirically, Lev delivers up to $72\times$ speedup and improves dataset diversity, measured by the Vendi score, by $9.2\%$ over the strong diversification baseline \textbf{DiSF}. On CommonCrawl (CC) web data selection, Lev improves accuracy across seven downstream tasks by up to $1.31\%$ over existing baselines. For domains where robust quality criteria are inherently difficult to define (e.g., code), Lev serves as an effective unsupervised curation alternative: on StarCoderData, the selected subset reduces bits-per-byte by $3.08\%$ over DiSF. Notably, we uncover a cross-domain collapse of quality filtering: CC data filtered by DCLM-fastText fail to retain sufficient code-related content, yielding inferior code performance relative to Lev-selected data. These findings advocate for integrating diversity-aware practices into quality filtering for more effective data curation in language model pretraining.

</details>


### [95] [When Helpful Text Hurts: Option-Redirecting Bias in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32489)
*Tam Le Thi Thanh,Hoang Tran Van,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le,Thanh Duc Ngo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过可控诊断协议揭示了辅助文本如何按三维属性影响三模态VQA，发现“支持干扰选项且与问题对齐但与图像矛盾”的文本最具破坏性，并通过无训练的推断时干预缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工作想明确不同类型的不可靠辅助文本在固定图像-问题-选项背景下如何影响答案选择，特别是哪类文本最易致使模型出错，以及如何修正这种失效。

Method: 提出Textual Reliability Ladder诊断协议，将辅助文本沿图像一致性、问题相关性和选项支持三轴进行可控分解；在多个数据集与VLM上评估不同类型文本的影响；并提出无训练的推断时干预策略——噪声稳定性引导（noise-stability steering）与动态接地（dynamic grounding），以抵消预测重定向。

Result: 发现支持干扰选项且与问题对齐但与图像矛盾的文本导致最大准确率下降（最高达53.1%），错误集中于特定错误选项；提出的推断时干预显著减少了重定向错误，同时在可信文本下基本保持性能。

Conclusion: 辅助文本的可靠性在三模态VQA中需要在决策层面理解：最具破坏性的文本并非最不真实的，而是那些与问题对齐、与图像矛盾并支持特定干扰选项的文本，会系统性地将模型预测重定向至错误选项。

Abstract: In tri-modal visual question answering (VQA), auxiliary text is commonly used to complement visual and textual inputs, yet its reliability is often uncontrolled. While prior work studies modality conflicts in general, it remains unclear how different types of unreliable auxiliary text affect answer selection under fixed image-question-option contexts. In this work, we show that the most harmful auxiliary text is not necessarily the most factually incorrect, but the one that aligns with the question while contradicting the image and favoring a specific distractor, leading to systematic redirection of model predictions. To isolate this effect, we introduce the Textual Reliability Ladder, a controlled diagnostic protocol that decomposes auxiliary text along three axes: image consistency, question relevance, and option support. Across multiple datasets (ScienceQA, VCR, A-OKVQA, Causal-VidQA) and recent VLMs, we find that such distractor-supporting text induces the largest accuracy drops (up to 53.1%) and concentrates errors on specific incorrect options. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a training-free inference-time intervention that explicitly counteracts this redirection effect via noise-stability steering and dynamic grounding, reducing redirected errors while largely preserving performance under faithful text.
