Our results highlight that auxiliary-text reliability must be understood at the decision level, rather than solely through factual correctness, and provide a practical pathway toward more robust tri-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [96] [RepoMAS: Solving Progressively Specified Tasks with Issue-Driven Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32490)
*Yuchen Song,Andong Chen,Wenxin Zhu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把新发现的需求和问题以 Issue 形式记录并用于动态修订任务规范，RepoMAS 在处理逐步指定任务时显著优于现有多智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的多智能体系统通常假定任务需求在执行前已被充分指定，但实际用户请求常常不完整，额外需求可能在推理、调用工具或执行过程中才显现，需要系统能够在运行时修订任务规范。

Method: 提出了 ProgSpec 基准用于评估需在执行中发现并满足的额外需求；设计了 RepoMAS，一种受开源项目管理启发的多智能体框架，将新发现的需求、冲突与失败以结构化 Issue 记录于仓库，并基于这些 Issue 动态修订任务规范与执行结构；在 ProgSpec 与五个已有基准上进行了对比实验证明效果。

Result: 在 ProgSpec 以及五个已有基准上，RepoMAS 达到最好性能；消融与分析表明其 Issue 驱动的修订与仓库维护机制对性能提升起到稳定贡献。

Conclusion: RepoMAS 证明了在“逐步指定任务”（progressively specified tasks）设置下，允许多智能体系统在执行过程中以结构化 Issue 的形式记录并修订任务需求与执行方案，能显著提升最终输出质量。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MASs) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, but most assume that task requirements are sufficiently specified before execution. In practice, user requests are often incomplete, and additional requirements may only become clear during reasoning, tool use, or execution. We refer to such problems as progressively specified tasks.
  To systematically study this setting, we introduce ProgSpec, a benchmark that evaluates final outputs against requirements explicitly stated in the initial request and additional requirements supported by the available task evidence. We further propose RepoMAS, an issue-driven multi-agent framework inspired by open-source project management. RepoMAS records newly discovered requirements, conflicts, and failures as structured Issues and uses them to revise the task specification and execution structure during problem solving.
  Across ProgSpec and five existing benchmarks, RepoMAS achieves the best performance. Further analyses show that its issue-driven revision and repository maintenance mechanisms consistently contribute to performance. These results highlight the importance of allowing MASs to revise not only how a task is solved, but also revise their explicit representation of task requirements during execution.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Prompt or Skill? Attribution-Guided Optimization of Modular LLM Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32492)
*Haoran Shou,Haoyue Liu,Yu Huo,Kun Zeng,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARO is a unified framework that attributes failures to prompt, skill, or routing, and applies targeted mutations via counterfactual evaluations; this structured optimization yields better generalization and performance than methods optimizing only prompts or skills.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods optimize only a single part of design space (global prompts or separate skill induction) and lack a principled mechanism to decide which component should be updated when failures occur; need unified framework for storing and activating reusable problem-solving knowledge.

Method: Perform controlled counterfactual evaluations to measure each example's effect on outcomes, convert effects into a probabilistic responsibility distribution over three components (prompt, skill blocks, routing), sample a component according to that distribution, and apply a targeted mutation to that component; iterate to refine prompts, skills, and routing jointly.

Result: Across five benchmarks and five worker models, SPARO consistently outperforms prompt-centered and skill-centered optimization baselines, demonstrating benefits of joint optimization and selective activation.

Conclusion: SPARO jointly optimizes task-level prompt, reusable skill blocks, and routing rules via targeted mutations based on probabilistic responsibility from counterfactual evaluations, improving language-program optimization and outperforming baselines across benchmarks and worker models. Effective optimization requires not only discovering task knowledge but also choosing where to store and when to activate it.

Abstract: Large language models can solve increasingly diverse reasoning tasks, yet their performance remains highly sensitive to task prompts, intermediate instructions, and the way reusable problem-solving knowledge is incorporated. Existing optimization methods usually focus on only one part of this design space: they either optimize a monolithic prompt, or separately induce and refine skills from model traces. As a result, they lack a principled mechanism for deciding which component should be updated when failures occur, and they rarely optimize prompts, skills, and skill-use policies in a unified framework. We propose SPARO (Skill, Prompt, And Routing Optimization), a framework that jointly optimizes task instructions, reusable skill blocks, and routing rules. It performs controlled counterfactual evaluations, converts examples' effects into a probabilistic responsibility distribution over prompt, skill, and routing components, samples one component from that distribution, and applies the corresponding targeted mutation. This design moves language-program optimization beyond global prompt rewriting toward structured, reusable, and selectively activated task knowledge. Across five benchmarks and five worker models, SPARO consistently outperforms both prompt-centered and skill-centered optimization baselines. These results suggest that effective language-program optimization depends not only on discovering useful task knowledge, but also on deciding where that knowledge should be stored and when it should be activated.

</details>


### [98] [DAAF: From Failure Localization to Editable System Assets in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32498)
*Xiaoyang Yuan,Qi Liu,Yubin Ruan,Xinyi Mou,Zhuomeng Zhang,Wenjin Wang,Hanying Jiao,Di Wu,Mingye Xu,Yi Bin,Ke Feng,Zixun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAAF learns replacement effects for versioned system assets and uses detection-aware attribution to recommend safe, effective repairs, achieving strong Hit@1 and task-recovery metrics with low regression.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents depend on persistent, versioned assets (routing rules, knowledge segments, prompts, skills). Traditional failure-localization identifies where an error appears in execution, but repair requires choosing which versioned asset to edit and whether the edit will improve outcomes.

Method: Formulate component-attribute failure attribution and introduce the Detection-Aware Attribution Framework (DAAF). DAAF learns effects of valid attribute replacements via controlled replay evaluations conditioned on component types, amortizes intervention evidence for deployment-time diagnosis, combines sparse/noisy failure signals, and returns no_change/repair target/unresolved without needing counterfactual replay or task reward at diagnosis time.

Result: On held-out tau^2-bench Telecom tasks, DAAF attains 80.72% attribute Hit@1, recovers 62.65% of failed executions while restricting clean-task regression to 3.23%, and achieves 71.93% overall task success, demonstrating effective intervention-grounded attribution for executable repair.

Conclusion: Intervention-grounded attribute attribution enables actionable repair decisions by linking failure localization to specific, editable system assets; DAAF effectively selects and validates attribute-level interventions, improving task success while minimizing regressions.

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent, versioned system assets such as routing rules, knowledge segments, prompt instructions, and reusable skills. Failure-localization methods can identify where an error manifests in an agent or execution trace, but repair requires a different decision: which editable system asset should be changed, and is that change expected to improve the task outcome? We study this gap through component-attribute failure attribution, where diagnosis targets versioned, addressable items rather than execution locations. We propose the Detection-Aware Attribution Framework (DAAF), which learns the effects of valid attribute replacements and amortizes this intervention evidence into deployment-time diagnosis. DAAF combines sparse and noisy failure signals to decide whether intervention is warranted, learns component-type-conditioned replacement effects from controlled replays evaluated by executable task outcomes, and shares supervision across requests with compatible intervention responses. At diagnosis time, DAAF uses only the observed execution, registered candidates, and available failure signals; it requires neither counterfactual replay nor task reward and returns no_change, a repair target, or an unresolved decision when evidence is insufficient. On held-out tau^2-bench Telecom tasks, DAAF achieves 80.72% attribute Hit@1, recovers 62.65% of failed executions while limiting clean-task regression to 3.23%, and reaches 71.93% overall task success. These results show that intervention-grounded attribute attribution can connect failure localization to executable system repair.

</details>


### [99] [TreeRef-BFN: Equivariance-Free De Novo Molecule Generation based on 2D Topology and Internal 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32502)
*Ruiqing Sun,Sen Yang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Yijie Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeRef 将分子拓扑与局部 3D 几何编码到可变大小树上，结合 BFN+Transformer 实现单模型、掩码驱动的可变尺寸 3D 分子生成，兼顾质量、速度与条件控件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 分子生成方法多数先预采样分子大小并直接生成笛卡尔坐标，限制了对可变尺寸条件任务（如片段补全、支架装饰）的适应性且常依赖于等变架构；而内部坐标方法虽避免刚体冗余，却通常依赖已知分子图或自回归构建，易累积误差。需要一种同时自然表达拓扑与局部几何、支持可变尺寸条件生成且不强依赖等变机制的表示与生成器。

Method: 引入 TreeRef 表示：用树结构节点承载原子/基元信息，RingRef 节点处理环闭合并保留树结构，Null 节点允许分子大小由节点占用率直接决定；在此表示上使用 Bayesian Flow Network（BFN）结合标准 Transformer 全局耦合局部变量，联合生成拓扑离散变量与局部连续几何。支持通过掩码实现无监督/条件（结构条件、性质条件）生成。

Result: 实证表明 TreeRef-BFN 在化学有效性、分子稳定性、多样性、本地几何分布精度、采样速度及性质条件生成等方面表现良好，标志其为高效且灵活的 3D 分子生成框架。

Conclusion: TreeRef-BFN 提出了一种将分子拓扑和依赖于拓扑的局部三维几何共同编码到可变大小树状表示（TreeRef）的方法，并基于此构建了基于 Transformer 的贝叶斯流网络（TreeRef-BFN），实现了同时生成离散分子变量和连续局部几何、支持单一预训练模型通过掩码做可变尺寸条件生成而不需重训练。

Abstract: De novo 3D molecular generation jointly models molecular size, topology, and geometry. Most methods pre-sample molecular size and generate Cartesian coordinates, limiting variable-size conditional tasks such as fragment completion and scaffold decoration while often relying on equivariant architectures. Internal-coordinate methods avoid rigid-body redundancy but typically require a known molecular graph or autoregressive construction, which may accumulate errors. We propose TreeRef, a tree-based molecular representation that assigns molecular topology and topology-dependent local 3D geometry to a naturally variable-size tree. RingRef nodes encode ring closures while preserving the tree structure, while Null nodes allow molecular size to emerge directly from node occupancy. Based on TreeRef, we develop TreeRef-BFN, a Bayesian Flow Network with a standard Transformer backbone that globally couples these locally defined variables and jointly generates discrete molecular variables and continuous local geometry. A single pretrained TreeRef-BFN supports unconditional generation and variable-size structure-conditioned 3D generation through masking alone, without retraining. Empirical studies demonstrate strong chemical validity, molecular stability, and diversity, accurate local geometric distributions, fast sampling, and competitive property-conditioned generation, establishing TreeRef-BFN as an efficient and flexible framework for 3D molecular generation.

</details>


### [100] [Learning from Others, Acting for You: Cross-User Memory Sharing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32511)
*Jinming Hu,Haodong Zhao,Qi Jia,Die Chen,Tianhang Zhao,Sufeng Duan,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShareMem 通过把“可复用的行为经验”与“接收方的具体偏好”解耦并结合两阶段合并与 scope-first 检索，实现了在多任务/多用户场景下更安全高效的记忆共享，实验表明其在多基准和模型上能稳定提升性能，但仍受源质量与偏好冲突限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同用户的 LLM 代理经常面对相关任务，若各自保留独立历史会浪费可复用经验；直接池化共享又可能把不符合目标用户偏好的信息传递过去。需要一种既能共享行为性经验又能以接收方偏好为依据的机制。

Method: 提出一种混合记忆架构：共享池存放“如何行动与应参考哪些偏好”的经验片段，接收方仍从本地记忆读取具体偏好值。训练/更新采用两阶段合并（本地精炼后再写入共享池），推理时用 scope-first 检索在共享与本地记忆间联合选择条目（共同的条目预算），并通过用户绑定通道进行初始与代理发起的偏好检索。对三类任务（网页导航、个性化在线交互、多会话编程）和四个 backbone 模型进行实验，比对用户本地记忆基线并做消融研究。

Result: 在 Mind2Web、VitaBench 2.0、MemoryCode 三套基准上，ShareMem 在步级成功率、平均任务成功率和对话宏观编码得分上均优于用户本地记忆基线（跨四个 backbone 模型均成立）。消融结果支持两阶段合并能降低共享池规模与归纳 token 使用并提升下游效果；经验指导与主动偏好检索互为补充。进一步分析表明：当本地相关经验稀缺时共享获益最大；源质量和跨用户偏好干扰是主要性能上限。

Conclusion: ShareMem 在保持用户偏好本地化的同时，允许共享可复用经验，从而在多任务、多用户场景下提升代理性能。该架构通过“经验指示 + 用户偏好具体值”的设计，以及两阶段合并与 scope-first 检索和用户绑定通道，减少了跨用户偏好冲突并提高了共享效率。整体评估显示对多个任务和模型均有稳定改进，但也受源质量和偏好干扰限制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents serving different users often solve related tasks, yet separate user histories can leave reusable experience inaccessible to other agents. Pooling memories expands access but risks transferring preferences that conflict with the receiving user's requirements. We introduce ShareMem, a memory architecture that shares reusable experience while grounding its application in the receiving user's own preferences. Shared experiences indicate how to act and which preferences to consult; the receiving user's memory supplies their concrete values. Two-stage consolidation refines experience locally before integrating accepted edits into a shared pool. During execution, scope-first retrieval jointly selects local and shared experiences under a common entry budget, while a user-bound channel supports initial and agent-initiated preference retrieval. We evaluate ShareMem across web navigation (Mind2Web), online personalized interaction (VitaBench~2.0), and multi-session coding (MemoryCode) with four backbone models. It improves step success, average task success, and dialogue-macro coding scores, respectively, over matched user-local memory across all four models. Ablations favor two-stage consolidation for smaller shared pools, lower induction token usage, and better downstream performance, and support complementarity between experience guidance and active preference retrieval. Further analyses show that sharing helps most when relevant local experience is scarce, while source quality and cross-user preference interference limit useful transfer.

</details>


### [101] [From Anomalies to Failures: Constructing Causal Error Graphs for Agentic Trace Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32514)
*Shu-Xun Yang,Yidong Wang,Zhuoer Feng,Bosi Wen,Jiayi Gui,Dayong Yang,Wenbo Yu,Haoke Zhang,Jie Tang,Cunxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过显式分类与因果错误图，CEG-Agent 提供了更可靠的 agentic trace 因果诊断，并伴随受验证的基准数据集与注释协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对代理执行痕迹的诊断方法模糊地混淆异常、错误和失败，且缺乏结构化的因果传播建模，导致故障归因不可靠，难以定位与修复长执行轨迹中的根因。

Method: 提出显式的异常/错误/失败分类法，定义 Causal Error Graphs（CEGs）将执行事件、诊断节点与失败结果用因果关系连接；基于工具的 CEG-Agent 执行因果诊断；设计 Adversarial Agentic Adjudication Protocol (AAAP) 获取高置信度的代理注释，并构建 CEG-Bench 基准数据集进行评估。

Result: 在 CEG-Bench 上，CEG-Agent 在语义放宽与结构精确两类评测标准下均达到了最先进的性能；自动注释通过与专家人工金标准的比对获得了高度一致性；代码已公开。

Conclusion: CEG-Agent 提供了一个基于工具增强的代理框架，通过显式区分异常/错误/失败并构建因果错误图（CEGs）实现对 agentic traces 的因果诊断，从而提高故障归因的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents are increasingly deployed in complex applications, where long agentic traces make failures difficult to diagnose. Existing trace diagnosis methods often conflate anomalies, errors, and failures, making diagnostic targets ambiguous; they also lack structured modeling of how causally relevant errors propagate and amplify into final task failures, resulting in unreliable failure attribution. To address these problems, we propose CEG-Agent, a tool-augmented agentic framework for causal diagnosis of agentic traces. Specifically, CEG-Agent introduces an explicit taxonomy of anomalies, errors, and failures, and constructs Causal Error Graphs (CEGs), a unified typed representation that links execution events, diagnostic nodes, and failure outcomes through causal relations. To evaluate causal trace diagnosis, we further construct CEG-Bench, a fully agent-annotated benchmark with high-confidence, consensus-derived CEG annotations obtained through an Adversarial Agentic Adjudication Protocol (AAAP). We validate the resulting annotations against an expert-curated human gold set, which shows close agreement with the automatic annotations. Experiments on CEG-Bench demonstrate that CEG-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance under both semantically relaxed and structurally exact evaluation criteria. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [LocalProp: Neuro-Localized Memory-Efficient Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32517)
*Diana-Nicoleta Grigore,Iuliana Georgescu,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LocalProp 用局部权重更新+剪枝+短微调替代全局反向传播，在大幅降低显存的同时保持较好性能，且可通过控制联合优化块数调节准确率与内存消耗的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端反向传播在生物学上不现实且内存效率低。提出局部化学习以降低显存需求并更符合大脑局部学习特性，同时保留“预训练→微调”范式。

Method: 提出 LocalProp：先用 I-JEPA 进行预训练，随后采用神经学上局部化的权重更新（仅局部块联合优化），之后执行剪枝操作并进行短时最终微调。通过改变被联合优化的块数量来控制梯度传播跨度，从而影响性能与内存占用。

Result: 在多项数据集（包括 ImageNet）上的实验表明，LocalProp 在占用远低于端到端反向传播的 GPU 峰值内存下仍能得到可接受/良好的性能；经验上发现梯度传播跨度是控制准确率-内存权衡的有效手段。

Conclusion: LocalProp 通过在模型中局部更新权重并结合剪枝与短时微调，在显存开销显著降低的前提下仍能达到良好性能；通过调整联合优化块数（gradient-propagation span）可以在准确率与内存消耗之间进行实用权衡。

Abstract: The current deep learning training paradigm employs end-to-end backpropagation, regardless of the training stage, i.e. pre-training or fine-tuning. However, backpropagating through the entire model is neither biologically plausible nor memory efficient, since learning inside the brain is highly localized. Therefore, we propose LocalProp, a training procedure that locally updates the weights of a model. Our neuro-localized weight updates follow the "pre-training then fine-tuning" paradigm, where the pre-training is based on I-JEPA. After locally updating the weights, a pruning operation is performed, followed by a short final fine-tuning phase. Pruning helps by sending the learning signal from higher blocks to lower blocks. We perform experiments on several datasets, including large-scale benchmarks such as ImageNet, and empirically show that LocalProp reaches good performance at a fraction of GPU peak memory. By varying the number of jointly optimized blocks, we identify gradient-propagation span as a practical control over the accuracy-memory trade-off.

</details>


### [103] [STR: Supervised Transcoder Replacement for Reducing Steering Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32519)
*Haonan Yu,Junhao Liu,Zhenyu Yan,Haoran Lin,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过训练一个受监督的 MLP 替代器并在其上拟合 steering 向量，STR 在保留目标控制的同时显著降低 steering 的副作用和域外攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已存在的模型 steering 方法常常使目标行为增强的同时损害其他有用行为；很多 steering 向量是在没有保护目标的情况下拟合的，因而有较大副作用与易受域外攻击。需要一种通用手段来缓解这些副作用，且可与现有 steering 方法兼容。

Method: 提出 Supervised Transcoder Replacement (STR)：对目标 steering 层的 MLP 计算训练一个替代器，使用三类监督信号——目标控制（target control）、非目标保留（non-target preservation）和未 steering 时的逼真度（fidelity without steering）；替代器冻结后在其上拟合选定的 steering 方向（保留各自的拟合目标）。在 Gemma 与 Llama 模型上结合三种 steering 方法进行评估，使用 SALAD-Bench 作为保护训练数据并在 HarmBench、AdvBench、StrongREJECT 上做域外测试。

Result: 在内部（SALAD-Bench）评估上 STR 明显减少了副作用，并将这种保护扩展到未见过的安全数据集，同时保持有效的目标控制。具体数值：对于仅有目标监督的 steering 向量，池化域外攻击成功率在 Gemma-3-4B 上从 42.46% 降至 14.42%，在 Gemma-3-12B 上从 34.97% 降至 12.91%。

Conclusion: 替换训练（STR）可以在不改变原有 steering 目标的前提下，显著降低 steering 带来的非目标行为退化，从而提高模型的安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Model steering can strengthen a target behavior while degrading other useful behaviors. We introduce Supervised Transcoder Replacement (STR) to reduce these side effects for existing steering methods, including those fitted without a protection objective. STR learns a replacement for the multilayer perceptron (MLP) computation at the steering layer through supervision for target control, non-target preservation, and fidelity without steering. Selected steering methods then fit directions on the frozen replacement while retaining their own fitting objectives. We evaluate three steering methods across Gemma and Llama models using Corrigibility preferences and four harmful-request safety datasets. SALAD-Bench supplies protection training data and a separate in-distribution evaluation split; HarmBench, AdvBench, and StrongREJECT are reserved for out-of-distribution testing. STR substantially reduces steering side effects on the in-distribution evaluation and extends this protection to the unseen safety datasets while retaining effective target control. For target-only supervised steering vectors, pooled out-of-distribution attack success rate falls from 42.46% to 14.42% on Gemma-3-4B and from 34.97% to 12.91% on Gemma-3-12B. These results show that replacement training can benefit steering methods fitted without protection objectives.

</details>


### [104] [MemAgent: Learning to Manage Heterogeneous Memory Providers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32521)
*Yongxian Wei,Yilin Zhao,Runxi Cheng,Xinrui Chen,Chun Yuan,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemAgent learns to route between multiple memory providers (probing, gating, selective storage) and, with synthetic phase-specific supervision, achieves substantial and efficient gains over single-memory baselines across several benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Single memory representations (trajectories, reflections, skills, structured knowledge) fail to generalize across diverse task distributions. A routing strategy that selects among heterogeneous memory providers can reuse complementary strengths and improve long-horizon agent behavior.

Method: Formulate memory management as a routing problem. Design MemAgent: (1) content-aware probing before retrieval, (2) short-term memory gating during execution, (3) selective multi-provider storage. Complement with a training-data synthesis pipeline that provides phase-specific supervision for routing decisions.

Result: Across GAIA, WebWalkerQA, and xBench-DS, MemAgent improves average accuracy by 10.0% and outperforms each individual memory method on all three benchmarks. Achieves <0.3% routing overhead and reduces average task steps by 12%.

Conclusion: Managing heterogeneous memory providers via a learned routing policy yields robust, generalizable, and efficient agent memory. MemAgent’s content-aware routing and phase-specific training lead to consistent gains over single-representation memories.

Abstract: Current agents remain largely stateless across tasks, limiting their ability to continually improve from prior interactions and making memory essential for long-horizon agentic behavior. Existing memory methods seek to reuse past experience, but most rely on a single memory representation (e.g., trajectories, reflections, skills, structured knowledge) whose effectiveness varies across task distributions. Rethinking this design space, we evaluate 13 memory methods and find that no single method generalizes across benchmarks, revealing the potential of managing heterogeneous memory providers. We formulate agent memory as a routing problem in which a memory agent decides which memory provider to retrieve from, whether to inject short-term memory, and which providers should store the resulting experience. Based on this perspective, we propose MemAgent, featuring a content-aware routing architecture and a training-data synthesis pipeline. The routing architecture combines content-aware probing before retrieval, short-term memory gating during execution, and selective multi-provider storage, while the training pipeline synthesizes phase-specific supervision for routing decisions. Across GAIA, WebWalkerQA, and xBench-DS, MemAgent improves average accuracy by 10.0% and outperforms every individual memory method across all three benchmarks. These gains come with less than 0.3% routing overhead and a 12% reduction in average task steps.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Dyadic Memory: Interaction-Aware Multimodal Memory with Adaptive Agentic Retrieval for Multi-Party Spoken Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32522)
*Wenxu Jia,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Dongjie Fu,Linjun Li,Wenshi Chen,Yangyang Wu,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向多方语音对话的多模态层次化记忆与基于证据增益的检索策略，显著提升多会话记忆检索与推理性能，并公开数据与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要聚焦于双人文本或图文对话，忽略了多方语音对话中保持对话内容、跨会话参与者识别以及谁对谁说话等长期记忆需求，因而需要一个专门的、交互感知的多模态长期记忆方案。

Method: 提出交互感知的多模态内存框架（VoxPolyMem）：结合增量式说话人识别与三层内存层次（交互内存、事实内存、参与者档案）；将检索建模为序列决策问题，代理通过重写查询并基于累计证据选择检索工具与记忆层；引入 Evidence-Gain GRPO (EG-GRPO)，采用轮次级别的回报分配以鼓励互补证据获取。

Result: 在新构建的评测集 VoxPolyBench 上总体得分85.0，领先最强基线23.6分；在 Mem-Gallery 和 H2HMem-Multi 上分别得分89.6和74.4，均比公开最强记忆基线高出超过8分；并开源代码与数据集。

Conclusion: VoxPolyMem 有效地解决了多方语音对话中的长期记忆问题，通过交互感知的多模态内存结构与基于证据增益的强化学习检索策略，实现了显著性能提升，支持持久化与个性化的多方多模态交互援助。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables agents to accumulate information and reason across sessions, yet existing research primarily focuses on dyadic text or image-text conversations, leaving long-term memory for multi-party spoken conversations underexplored. This setting requires preserving conversational content, identifying participants across sessions, and retaining who speaks to whom. To this end, we propose VoxPolyMem, an interaction-aware multimodal memory framework combining incremental speaker identification with a memory hierarchy comprising interaction memory, fact memory, and participant profiles. We formulate retrieval as sequential decision-making, where an agent rewrites queries and selects retrieval tools and memory layers based on accumulated evidence to address information gaps. We further introduce Evidence-Gain GRPO (EG-GRPO), which uses round-wise credit assignment to encourage complementary evidence acquisition. We also construct VoxPolyBench to evaluate memory evolution, personalized answering, memory retrieval and reasoning, and interaction reasoning and attribution in multi-party spoken conversations. VoxPolyMem achieves an overall score of 85.0 on VoxPolyBench, surpassing the strongest evaluated baseline by 23.6 points. On Mem-Gallery and H2HMem-Multi, it scores 89.6 and 74.4, respectively, exceeding the strongest evaluated public memory baselines by over 8 points each. These results highlight its potential for persistent, personalized assistance in multi-party multimodal interactions. Code and datasets are available at https://voxpolymem.github.io/VoxPolyBench/demo/

</details>


### [106] [AmbiModBench: Benchmarking Gene Perturbation Prediction Beyond Shared Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32527)
*Sikai Huang,Zhiwen Yang,Kai Yu,Jiayuan Chen,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AmbiModBench is a specificity-, gene- and coverage-aware benchmark that reveals many high scores in perturbation-response models reflect background effects; it provides permutation and embedding-based controls to detect true target-specific predictive power.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing evaluation practice cannot separate target-specific predictions from shared background, fails to verify gene-level accuracy (metrics remain high under gene shuffling), and ignores coverage dependence (representation-space proximity and response-constraining power).

Method: Proposes AmbiModBench: (1) pair each score with a training-mean reference fitted on the same train/test split to control split-specific background; (2) screen each readout by permuting gene coordinates to test gene-level specificity; (3) analyze how embedding (representation-space) distance correlates with response variation to assess coverage and effective generalization.

Result: Applied to K562, RPE1 and Norman datasets: strong absolute scores mostly reflect shared background rather than target-specific learning; many readouts reflect response-magnitude distributions not specific affected genes; measurable gain correlates with representation-space coverage, not simply training-set size; RPE1 shows reproducible target-specific gains across splits and gene selections under the new protocol.

Conclusion: Absolute performance metrics overstate models' target-specific learning because they largely reflect a shared background response; AmbiModBench exposes this by controlling for split-specific baselines, permuting gene coordinates, and relating representation-space coverage to predictive gains.

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations helps prioritize experiments in single-cell genomics, where exhaustive measurement is infeasible. While computational models increasingly predict these responses, three evaluation deficiencies obscure what their scores demonstrate. First, absolute metrics cannot separate target-specific predictions from a shared background response. Second, common metrics remain high under gene shuffling, so gene-level accuracy is never verified. Third, a score at one training size says nothing about coverage, which depends on representation-space proximity and response-constraining power. We propose AmbiModBench, a specificity-aware, gene-resolved and coverage-aware benchmark. It pairs every score with a training-mean reference fitted on the same split, screens each readout by gene-coordinate permutation, and links embedding distance to response variation. Across K562, RPE1 and Norman, strong absolute scores largely reflect shared background rather than target-specific learning. Widely used readouts track response magnitude distributions rather than the affected genes. Detectable gain follows representation-space coverage rather than training-set size. Nonetheless, on RPE1 the protocol yields a reproducible target-specific gain across five additional splits and three gene selections, which absolute scores alone cannot distinguish from shared background.

</details>


### [107] [Fail Loudly: An Auditable Runtime for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32528)
*Hanxu Yan,Langxuan Deng,Zhengle Wang,Yibo Wang,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RADAR augments LLM data-analysis agents with evidence-preserving retrieval, typed operator logging, and runtime validation to detect and correct semantic errors; this leads to substantial accuracy gains across multiple benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based data-science agents often make incorrect choices about data sources, scope, or statistical definitions that produce silent, plausible-but-wrong outputs. There is a need for an auditable runtime to make analytical decisions inspectable and enable correction before final outputs.

Method: Three core mechanisms: (1) evidence-preserving exploration that retrieves task-relevant content while preserving source locations and coverage; (2) typed operators that record declared inputs, operation arguments, and resulting observations; (3) runtime validation that checks proposed operations against recorded observations and rejects or provides diagnostic feedback on conflicts to prompt revision before errors propagate.

Result: On KramaBench, RADAR achieves overall scores of 0.723 with full source retrieval and 0.747 with gold sources, representing relative gains of 35.9% and 28.8% over strongest baselines. Additional relative gains of 14.0% on DA-Code and 59.3% on DABStep demonstrate broad applicability.

Conclusion: RADAR provides an auditable runtime that makes LLM-driven data-analysis agents’ choices inspectable and verifiable, enabling agents to detect and correct semantic mismatches and thus fail loudly rather than silently produce plausible but incorrect outputs.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled data-science agents to automate multi-step analyses over heterogeneous files. However, incorrect choices regarding data sources, scope, or statistical definitions often lead to silent errors: computations execute successfully but produce plausible yet incorrect outputs that fail to answer the intended question. To mitigate this, we present RADAR, an auditable runtime that makes an agent's analytical choices inspectable and supports their revision through execution feedback. RADAR operates through three core mechanisms. First, an evidence-preserving exploration module retrieves task-relevant content while retaining source locations and observation coverage. Next, the runtime uses typed operators to record the agent's declared inputs, operation arguments, and resulting observations. Finally, runtime validation checks proposed operations against these observations. When a conflict is detected, the runtime rejects the operation or provides diagnostic feedback, allowing the agent to revise its choices before errors propagate. This design enables agents to fail loudly while leaving semantic interpretation to the LLM. On KramaBench, RADAR achieves overall scores of 0.723 with full source retrieval and 0.747 with gold sources supplied, corresponding to relative gains of 35.9% and 28.8% over the strongest baselines. Beyond KramaBench, RADAR achieves relative performance gains of 14.0% on DA-Code and 59.3% on DABStep, demonstrating its applicability across diverse agentic data-analysis workflows.

</details>


### [108] [LLMAdBench: A Human Preference Benchmark for Advertising in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32533)
*Rui Ai,Yuqing Liu,Sitao Qiu,Yun Qiao,Yuhan Wang,Jessica Xiwen Wang,Yiqi Yang,Lihong Huang,Ruiyao Sun,Kaifeng Zhang,Shengze Ding,Jiaqi He,Xinman Wang,Tianhao Gao,Jimmy Qin,Jianghao Lin,Chonghuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMAdBench是用于研究LLM生成内容中广告插入位置的人类偏好基准，表明未微调的前沿LLM不能可靠替代人类标注，但该偏好信号是可学习的，且披露会显著改变用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费端LLM输出中插入广告成为新商业模式，但缺乏关于如何评估广告插入（尤其位置选择）以及它如何影响用户偏好的共享证据与基准。

Method: 构建一个配对数据集：在固定用户对话、基础回答、广告内容与披露条件的前提下，只改变广告插入位置，要求人类标注者基于六个从广告主与用户视角设计的评价标准对每对响应进行偏好比较；收集两种披露条件下的标注（显式标注为赞助与合并无披露）；用该基准评估八个前沿LLM作为偏好判断器，并在Qwen3-8B上进行人类偏好微调以验证可学习性。

Result: 数据集包含超过18,000条人类判断；前沿LLM稳定性差、模型间一致性低、在展示顺序翻转时约有25%的判断会颠倒；微调后的Qwen3-8B在保留集上显著优于其基线并超过所有零样本前沿判断器；基准也量化了广告主—用户之间的权衡，以及披露条件对用户偏好的系统性影响。

Conclusion: 提出LLMAdBench并通过实证表明：前沿LLM不能可靠替代人类评价者；对人类标注微调的模型可以明显改进对广告位置偏好的预测；且广告是否标注为赞助（sponsorship disclosure）会系统性改变用户对广告位置的偏好。

Abstract: Inserting advertisements (ads) into consumer-facing LLM output is emerging as a new business model, but there is little shared evidence on how such ad insertion should be evaluated or how it affects user preferences. We introduce LLMAdBench, a human-preference benchmark for studying advertising in LLM-generated content. The benchmark isolates a simple but practically important decision: given a user conversation, an LLM response, and a matched advertisement, where should the ad be placed? Our dataset compares pairs of responses that differ only in ad position while holding all other conditions fixed including the user query, base answer, advertisement, and disclosure condition. Human annotators evaluate each pair based on six criteria from both advertiser's and user's perspectives. The resulting benchmark contains more than 18000 human judgments across two disclosure conditions: explicitly labeling the ad as sponsored and merging it into the response without disclosure. We use LLMAdBench to evaluate eight frontier LLMs as preference judges and find that they are not reliable substitutes for human evaluation. Even the most stable models reverse roughly one quarter of their decisions when the presentation order is swapped, agreement across models is low, and their placement preferences differ systematically from those of human annotators. Moreover, LLMAdBench contains substantial learnable signal. In particular, a Qwen3-8B model fine-tuned on the human preferences improves substantially over its base model and outperforms all zero-shot frontier judges on the held-out prediction task. Beyond model evaluation, LLMAdBench provides quantitative evidence on the advertiser-user trade-off and shows that the sponsorship disclosure systematically changes users' preference over ad placement.

</details>


### [109] [Interpretable Physics Informed WiFi Indoor Localization: Learning an Effective Access Point Geometry and Using It to Prune](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32539)
*Arshia Eftekhari zadeh,Rezvan Nasiri,Hadi Moradi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用物理约束解码器在层级深度模型中联合学习位置与AP几何，从RSS指纹中推断可解释的AP几何并用于高效排序/剪枝，显著提升定位并支持特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep models give high localization accuracy but are black boxes and cannot easily reuse learned information or provide AP-level interpretability; surveyed AP coordinates are often unavailable, so a method that infers geometry from data is needed.

Method: A chained/hierarchical deep-learning architecture with physics-informed decoders that learn AP geometry directly from RSSI fingerprints and labelled user positions; the learned geometry is used for Fisher-information-based AP ranking and pruning; evaluated on multiple public indoor fingerprint datasets.

Result: On UJIIndoorLoc the model reaches mean 3D error 7.07 m (26–36% reduction vs baselines); previously published methods on the same split report 10.6–31.0% higher errors. Pruning 35%–50% of APs yields only small accuracy loss. Geometry-based AP selection performs similarly to label-based selectors on Tampere/TUT and UTSIndoorLoc.

Conclusion: Physics-informed hierarchical deep model jointly predicts user location and infers AP geometry from RSSI and labelled positions, enabling interpretable features that support AP ranking/pruning and improve localization accuracy without needing true AP coordinates.

Abstract: Deep learning models can achieve high accuracy for indoor localization, but their black-box nature limits interpretability and the reuse of learned information. We propose a hierarchical deep learning framework for WiFi fingerprint-based indoor localization that jointly predicts user location and learns an effective geometry of the surrounding access points (APs). Physics-informed decoders infer this geometry directly from RSSI measurements and labelled user positions, without requiring the true AP coordinates during training. The learned geometry is then used to rank and prune APs.
  On the UJIIndoorLoc dataset, the proposed chained model achieves a mean 3D localization error of 7.07 m, reducing error by 26% to 36% compared with baseline models. Previously published methods evaluated on the same official split report errors 10.6% to 31.0% higher. Pruning 35% or 50% of the APs causes only a small loss in localization accuracy. The inferred geometry also enables Fisher-information-based AP ranking even when fingerprint databases do not contain surveyed AP coordinates. Experiments on the Tampere/TUT and UTSIndoorLoc datasets show that geometry-guided AP selection performs comparably to selectors built directly from labelled data. These results show that physics-informed interpretability can improve indoor localization while also supporting effective feature selection.

</details>


### [110] [Porimon: An LLM-Based Pokémon Battle Agent Enhanced by Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32544)
*Dongyin Zhuo,Fengjunjie Pan,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过短期历史与外部精确信息增强LLM代理（Porimon），在Pokémon对战中显著提高胜率，但长期经验检索效果尚不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无需额外微调的情形下，提升LLM代理在需要对手感知规划（opponent-aware planning）任务中的表现，探索利用历史状态与过往经验摘要等外部信息改进决策。

Method: 引入Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation（LSTKAG），使LLM代理结合当前任务的过去状态（短期）并检索与当前状态相似的过往任务经验摘要（长期）；基于此构建Porimon代理，并通过外部API提供精确伤害计算与更丰富的游戏信息；用15,000场对战进行超参调优、消融与性能评估。

Result: 经过超参优化的Porimon显著优于先前的PokéLLMon结构和基于规则的启发式玩家；检索游戏信息的扩展对性能有贡献；但长期经验检索（Long-Term KAG）的效用在实验中未能明确体现。

Conclusion: 提出的LSTKAG机制与基于它的Porimon代理在Pokémon Battles任务上能显著提升基于LLM代理的决策性能，但Long-Term KAG的贡献在当前实验中尚不确定。

Abstract: In this paper, we use Pokémon Battles as a case study to investigate how to improve the performance of LLM-based agents in tasks that require opponent-aware planning without additional fine-tuning. We propose Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation (LSTKAG), a mechanism that enables LLM-based agents to leverage past states of the current task and retrieve experience summaries from similar previous task instances based on the current state. Based on LSTKAG, we design Porimon, an LLM-based agent structure for Pokémon Battles. For optimization, we introduce an external API for precise damage calculation and more detailed information about the game. We conduct tournament-like evaluation experiments comprising 15,000 battles for hyperparameter optimization, ablation studies, and performance evaluation. The results indicate that Porimon-based players with hyperparameter optimization significantly outperform players based on PokéLLMon, an LLM-based agent structure proposed in previous research, and the rule-based heuristic player. Furthermore, our ablation study shows that Porimon variants outperform the one without extension in game information retrieval, which shows the contribution of that extension. However, the current experiment results are inconclusive regarding the contribution of Long-Term KAG. These results suggest that introducing external resources, information from previous states of the current task, and experience summaries from similar previous task instances could elevate the performance of LLM-based agents designed for tasks requiring opponent-aware planning.

</details>


### [111] [Are Vision-Language-Action Models Robust to One-Step Observation Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32550)
*Shojiro Yamabe,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究一次性（one-step）观测扰动对 VLA 的影响，发现其损害与动作块执行长度相关；提出基于一致性的动态执行长度选择方法 CARE，可在扰动发生时短执行以提升鲁棒性，同时保持干净环境下性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全性研究多关注持续的观测扰动，但现实世界中短暂且严重的观测损坏（momentary corruption）也会致命性影响 agent 行为，且这一威胁在 VLA 系统中未被充分研究。

Method: 提出CARE方法：在每步决策时基于当前预测的动作块与之前预测的动作块的一致性度量，动态选择动作块的执行长度（在高一致性时允许较长执行，低一致性时缩短执行长度），从而在一-step观测严重扰动场景下降低错误执行的累积影响。方法实现代价低且保留了干净环境下的性能。实验通过在VLA任务中施加单步强扰动，比较不同执行长度和CARE策略的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 实验证明单步扰动会显著降低 VLA 模型性能，扰动影响与动作块执行长度有关：较长的执行长度在遭遇单步错误时会放大后续误差。CARE 在多种设置下显著提高了单步扰动下的鲁棒性，并在无扰动时保持原有性能，且计算开销低。

Conclusion: 本文表明一次性（one-step）观测扰动对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型会显著降低性能；扰动的破坏程度与动作块（action chunk）执行长度相关。通过基于先前预测的一致性动态调整执行长度的策略 CARE，可以在扰动发生时选择更短的执行长度以提升鲁棒性，同时在无扰动时保持原有性能。

Abstract: Understanding the safety risks of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for their deployment in the physical world. Existing safety research has mainly considered persistent perturbations that are applied continuously to observations throughout an episode. However, momentary observation corruption, in which observations are severely perturbed only briefly within an episode, remains an underexplored safety threat. To address this gap, this work investigates robustness to one-step perturbations applied at a single time step per episode. Our experiments reveal that these perturbations substantially degrade VLA performance and that their impact depends on the action chunk execution length. Based on them, we propose CARE, which dynamically selects the execution length based on consistency with the previously predicted action chunk. CARE improves robustness with low computational overhead while preserving clean performance by selecting shorter execution lengths only under perturbations.

</details>


### [112] [Artificial intelligences and human scientists exhibit complementary strengths in theory building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32562)
*Ke Li,Spyros I. Zoumpoulis,Phanish Puranam,Philip Parker,Matthew Eshbaugh-Soha,Izzy Gainsburg,Michael Gilead,Igor Grossmann,Britt Hadar,Yoel Inbar,Almog Simchon,Robb Willer,Rui Ai,Ruicheng Ao,Gavin J. Bala,Matthew Bidwell,Shuang Cai,Kai Chang,Skyler Y. Chen,Cory J. Clark,Irmak Dai,Abhinandan Dalal,Connor Douglas,Alexis Du,Zhehang Du,Leyun Feng,Isabel Fernandez-Mateo,Linnea Gandhi,Cyrille Grumbach,Anmol Gupta,Vansh Gupta,Maria Hademer,Jay H. Hardy,Chen Kai Huang,Jacob Xiangyu Jin,Ufuk Keskin,Na Hyun Kim,Mert Kobaş,Byounghoon Koh,Gabrielle Lamont-Dobbin,Gregory Lanzalotto,Sun Young Lee,Dingzhe Leng,Chenjun Li,Weiyuan Li,Zeyuan Li,Zhongyuan Liang,Ning Liu,Peihong Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jiuyao Lu,Wanteng Ma,Nicolas Martinet,Natnael Mulat,Christina A. Nguyen,Khai Nguyen,Quang Minh Nguyen,Naja Pape,Chanwoo Park,Stefanos Poulidis,Jeffrey Sanchez-Burks,Michael Schaerer,Isabelle Solal,Yanbo Song,Junghyo Sun,Qingyao Sun,Rui Sun,Roderick Swaab,Kevin Tan,Dequn Teng,Michelle A. Vaccaro,Robin Vigerbaeck,Xiaomeng Wang,Randol H. Yao,Duygu Yilmaz,Shun Yiu,Ecem Yucesoy,Allen Zang,Ruijia Zhang,Xilan Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Zhanhao Zhang,Eric Luis Uhlmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在复杂信息处理与个体表现上优于多数人类，但人类思想多样性是群体智慧与创造力的关键，且更简洁理论在预测效率上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验AI（尤其LLMs）与人类科学家在需高阶认知的社会科学任务上的相对效能，理解AI是否能在理论创生、预测与应对新证据方面补足或超越人类研究者。

Method: 在围绕性别与种族不平等的学术话语中，比较了25款LLM、13名资深研究者和60名博士生在理论制定、对新实证结果的预测及在新证据下的理论修正等任务的表现；使用盲评评分理论质量，并分析理论复杂度、预测准确性、聚合效应与更新行为。

Result: LLMs在多数个体层面任务上优于大多数人类参与者；AI生成理论更详尽、路径更多、含潜变量更广，且被盲评为质量更高；但复杂性与预测准确性无明显正相关；人类理论更具多样性，群体（聚合）预测收益大；AI更倾向于全面整合新证据，而人类更新更为选择性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在高层次社会科学认知任务（理论构建、预测新实证结果、在新证据面前修正理论）中，多数大型语言模型（LLMs）单体表现优于大多数个体人类研究者，但人类理论更具多样性且经聚合后预测准确性提升更大。AI生成的理论更复杂且被评为质量更高，但复杂性并未带来更好预测；相反，人类以更简洁理论取得更高预测效率。AI更频繁地根据新证据修正理论，而人类更新更选择性并受先前预测误差影响。

Abstract: We investigate the effectiveness of artificial intelligences (AI)-specifically large language models (LLMs)-relative to human scientists at high-level cognitive tasks in social science such as theory formulation, predictions of novel empirical results, and theory revision in response to new evidence. The research domain was academic discourse regarding gender and race inequality. Our findings, comparing 25 LLMs with 13 senior researchers and 60 doctoral scholars, reveal that the AIs outperformed most humans individually on most of the present tasks, while human theories were more diverse and exhibited greater gains in predictive accuracy from aggregation. AI-generated theories were more extensively elaborated, involving additional theoretical paths and latent variables, and were rated as higher quality than human theories by independent raters blinded to source. However, this theoretical complexity was in part ornamental, in that it was not associated with more accurate predictions about empirical patterns in data; in contrast, human scientists achieved greater predictive efficiency with simpler theories. The AIs were significantly more likely than human scientists to revise their theories to incorporate new evidence; human scientists updated their beliefs in a selective way that is sensitive to prior prediction errors. We speculate that the superior processing capacity of artificial intelligences makes them especially well-suited to tasks requiring grappling with complexity, but that the greater diversity of human ideas is essential to wise crowds and collective creativity.

</details>


### [113] [ProTTT: Learning to Learn Semantic User Memory with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32564)
*Sejun Park,Hyoungjo Bhang,Hyein Jeong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProTTT: learn a profile-supervised initialization and refine it via test-time training on user history to form a compact, evolving semantic user memory that enables efficient, effective personalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Context-based personalization scales poorly as user history grows and retrieval/summarization add overhead; parametric methods need expensive reconstructions when new data arrive. The goal is an efficient, continuously evolving user memory that captures semantic preferences.

Method: Start from a shared initialization learned via supervision from textual user profiles; at test time, perform test-time training on each user’s history to adapt the shared initialization into a user-specific memory; store that memory as lightweight parameterized representation that is updated as history grows.

Result: Consistent empirical gains over baselines on multiple benchmarks; substantial inference cost reduction by compressing histories into small param memories; analyses show profile supervision reliably guides memory to semantic user knowledge and the memory can track evolving preferences and is robust to history size variations.

Conclusion: ProTTT provides a profile-supervised meta-learning approach to learn a lightweight, continuously updatable semantic user memory that outperforms full-history ICL and parametric baselines while reducing inference cost.

Abstract: Personalization requires language models to capture user-specific knowledge from a growing user history. Existing context-based approaches incur increasing inference costs as user history accumulates and rely on separate retrieval or summarization stages, while parametric-based approaches often require reconstructing user representations when new user data is added. We introduce ProTTT, a profile-supervised meta-learning framework for learning semantic user memory. The memory construction starts from a shared initialization and is updated for each user through test-time training on user history, allowing it to evolve continuously as the history grows. However, since test-time training alone does not explicitly encourage the memory to capture semantic user knowledge necessary for personalization, we learn this shared initialization using textual user profiles as supervision, so that test-time training on user history captures semantic knowledge more effectively. ProTTT consistently outperforms both full history ICL and all parametric baselines across diverse benchmarks, while substantially reducing inference cost by compressing user history into a lightweight parameterized memory. Our analysis also shows that profile supervision is a reliable objective for learning semantic user knowledge and that the resulting memory can track and retain evolving user preferences, while remaining robust across different history sizes. Overall, we demonstrate the effectiveness of test-time training for personalization and establish ProTTT as a baseline for continuously evolving user memory.

</details>


### [114] [CUE-Mem: Benchmarking Long-Term User Memory via Implicit Cues in Multimodal Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32574)
*Yulin Hu,Yanyan Zhao,Zimo Long,Xing Fu,Mengtong Ji,Weixiang Zhao,Yutai Hou,Qianchao Wang,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUE-Mem 是一个专注隐式多模态线索的长期记忆基准，揭示了当前系统在保存/检索微妙多模态证据方面的薄弱点，并呼吁更好的多模态表示、索引与选择性记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准主要关注文本或显式多模态证据，而现实会话中用户记忆常以图像背景对象、环境声等隐含提示出现，需专门基准来评估对这些微妙线索的保留与检索能力。

Method: 构建 CUE-Mem 基准（2,674 个问题，覆盖显式与隐式证据），包含四类任务：实体回忆、长期模式、个性化推荐与拒答。对比文本化记忆系统与原生多模态访问，评估不同 caption 细节级别、不同 backbone 与索引策略对检索与回答表现的影响。

Result: 在各种文本化记忆系统中，隐式证据下的表现远低于有理想证据（oracle）的上限；增加 caption 细致度能恢复部分证据但收益不均且令 token 成本迅速上升；原生多模态接入提升有限且依赖 backbone，索引引入噪声需改进检索与表示方法。

Conclusion: CUE-Mem 表明隐含的多模态线索是长期用户记忆系统的主要瓶颈；将多模态信息文本化（caption）可以部分恢复此类证据但代价高昂；原生多模态访问并不能一劳永逸地解决问题，效果强烈依赖 backbone，并且多模态索引会引入显著检索噪声。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents that interact with users across sustained conversations. However, user memories are not always explicitly stated: they may also be implied by recurring background objects in images, ambient sounds in audio, or other peripheral multimodal cues. Existing benchmarks largely focus on text-only memory or explicit multimodal evidence, leaving implicit multimodal cues underexplored. We introduce CUE-Mem, a text-image-audio benchmark for evaluating long-term user memory from implicit cues. CUE-Mem contains 2,674 questions across explicit and implicit evidence settings and covers four tasks: Entity Recall, Long Pattern, Personalized Recommendation, and Answer Refusal. Across textualized memory systems, implicit performance remains far below oracle evidence, locating the main bottleneck in preserving and retrieving subtle cues rather than question answerability. Increasing caption detail recovers more of this evidence, but brings uneven gains and rapidly growing token costs, motivating native multimodal access. Yet native access does not uniformly resolve the bottleneck: evidence use depends strongly on the backbone, while multimodal indexing introduces substantial retrieval noise. CUE-Mem provides a testbed for memory systems that selectively retain, retrieve, and use subtle multimodal evidence.

</details>


### [115] [EMIR$^2$: Evolution-Aware Memory with Intent-Guided Multi-Round Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32584)
*Jinlan Liu,Hongliang Sun,Yong Wang,Bolin Zhang,Dinabo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合演化记忆图与意图驱动的多轮检索的EMIR^2，使LLM代理能保存历史演化并自适应获取证据，从而显著改进长期交互场景下的记忆利用与冲突解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统通常使用静态表示和单轮检索，无法跟踪事实随时间变化或将分散证据整合以解决冲突，导致LLM代理在长时交互中利用历史信息的能力不足。

Method: 构建State-Evolving Memory Graph（SEMG）以时间事件轨迹和证据关联表示演化知识状态；基于此设计意图引导的多轮检索机制，迭代识别缺失证据并基于累计信息扩展检索。

Result: 在LoCoMo与MemConflict数据集上，EMIR^2在长期记忆利用、动态/静态冲突处理及复杂检索任务上取得提升，某些类别相对提高超过12%。

Conclusion: 提出的EMIR^2通过联合建模记忆演化和多轮检索，有效提升LLM代理对长期历史信息的利用，能更好地处理事实变化和冲突证据问题，在基准上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to leverage historical interactions for future tasks. However, existing memory systems struggle to utilize continuously evolving historical information, as they often rely on static memory representations and single-round retrieval strategies, failing to track factual changes or integrate distributed evidence across long-term interactions. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$, an \textbf{E}volution-Aware \textbf{M}emory framework with \textbf{I}ntent-Guided Multi-\textbf{R}ound \textbf{R}etrieval, enabling LLM agents to maintain evolving historical knowledge and adaptively retrieve relevant evidence. Specifically, \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ constructs a State-Evolving Memory Graph (SEMG) that represents long-term memory as evolving knowledge states supported by temporal event trajectories and evidential associations. By maintaining semantic states through evidence-based updates, SEMG preserves historical evolution and enables evidence tracing under complex and conflicting scenarios. Building upon this, we introduce an intent-guided multi-round retrieval mechanism that iteratively identifies missing evidence and expands retrieval based on accumulated information. Experiments on LoCoMo and MemConflict demonstrate that \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ improves long-term memory utilization, dynamic and static conflict handling, and complex retrieval performance, achieving relative improvements of more than 12\% in certain categories. These results highlight the effectiveness of jointly modeling memory evolution and adaptive evidence acquisition for long-term agent interactions.

</details>


### [116] [MA-FPPO: Multi-Agent Flow-Pretrained Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32594)
*Guowei Zou,Haonan Chen,Haitao Wang,Beiwen Zhang,Na Yan,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MA-FPPO：用flow matching预训练策略并通过基于团队优势的在线微调提升多智能体在离线数据覆盖不足状况下的适应性与协作，显著优于离线和纯在线基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体策略在离线分布外情形下常失败，因其学到的动作模式无法有效适配环境变化且无法在在线交互中充分协调。需要一种在保留预训练行为的同时用在线数据增强适应性与协调性的方案。

Method: 先用flow matching在离线数据上预训练策略，并构建对离散与连续动作空间都适用的带显式动作似然的策略表示；随后在在线交互阶段，基于团队优势（shared team advantages）对预训练模型进行联合优化，进一步改进协作行为。

Result: 在多个任务与设置上，MA-FPPO在30个情形下相较于最强离线基线平均取得52.8%的相对提升；在38次与纯在线学习匹配样本/评测协议的对比中，平均取得29.8%的相对优势。

Conclusion: MA-FPPO通过在flow matching预训练基础上进行在线微调，有效增强了多智能体策略在离线数据未覆盖场景下的适应性与协作能力，从而显著提升任务完成率和团队回报。

Abstract: Multi-agent flow policies learn cooperative behavior from fixed offline datasets, but often struggle to complete tasks in situations not covered by the offline data. In these situations, agents must both adapt to changes in the environment and coordinate with one another, yet action patterns learned offline are often insufficient for effective adaptation and coordination. To address this problem, we propose Multi-Agent Flow-Pretrained Policy Optimization (MA-FPPO), which uses online fine-tuning to improve the cooperative behavior of models pretrained with flow matching through new interactions with the environment. Building on the behavior learned during pretraining, we construct policies with explicit action likelihoods for discrete and continuous action spaces. We then update the pretrained model using shared team advantages to further improve coordination based on team performance. Our method achieves, on average, relative gains of 52.8% over the strongest listed offline baselines across 30 settings and 29.8% over purely online learning across 38 comparisons with matched online budgets and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [117] ["You're Right, Let Me Fix It": How LLM Agents Damage Correct Work When Falsely Accused](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32616)
*Xutao Mao,Rui Qian,Longxiang Wang,Xinjian Yi,Mingxuan Li,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了衡量并缓解LLM代理在面对无法本地验证的虚假指控时破坏既有正确工作的基准（CAVE-Bench），发现问题普遍且可通过运行时门控大幅降低损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命/持续运行的LLM代理在任务成功后仍继续工作或接手交接，后续输入有时会对已完成正确工作提出虚假指控（称为gaslight sycophancy），代理若照做会破坏先前正确结果。需要度量这一现象并评估缓解策略。

Method: 构建了CAVE-Bench：365个跨6个领域的任务，任务在可验证正确的状态开始，其支持性理由和历史保存在工作区，而能证实指控的事实存在于代理无法直接访问的外部/运行时状态。定义5种风险因子控制指控如何进入工作流，记录轨迹并从中评分指控接受度和证据使用，通过确定性重放下游事件来量化损害。对14个近期模型（Claude Code 及在OpenCode、Codex、Hermes上的变体）进行评估，并测试了一个基于基准实时信号的harness gate作为缓解手段。

Result: 在被测模型中，虚假指控导致正确工作被破坏的比例最高达60.06%；更强的模型有时在恢复到支持证据后仍会过度纠正；同一模型在不同后端表现不一；基于基准实时信号的harness gate将重放后损害减少约74%。

Conclusion: Preserving already-correct work under unsupported accusation is a distinct safety challenge: many LLM agents accept false accusations and perform destructive over-corrections; benchmarking and simple runtime gates can substantially reduce replayed harm.

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly keep working after a task succeeds as they resume after compaction or take over handoffs. Their finished work keeps receiving follow-up input that sometimes falsely accuses it for later failures. We call an agent's acceptance of such a false accusation gaslight sycophancy, and destructive over-correction when acting on it damages previously correct work. We introduce CAVE-Bench, a benchmark of 365 agentic tasks across six domains built around opaque tasks. Every scored run first reaches a verified correct state, whose supporting rationale and history stay in the workspace while the facts that would settle the accusation lie in external or runtime state beyond the agent's reach. The agent cannot confirm or refute the claim with a local check, so the right response should keep the work and ask for the missing evidence. Each task either hands the agent correct work with saved evidence or let it build and verify that work first, and five risk factors set how the accusation enters the workflow. We score accusation acceptance and evidence use from the trajectory and measure harm by deterministic replay of downstream events. Across 14 of the latest models in Claude Code, false accusations damage correct work in up to 60.06% of runs, and stronger models often do so after recovering the supporting evidence. The same model behaves differently across OpenCode, Codex, and Hermes, and a harness gate driven by the benchmark's live signals cuts replayed harm by 74%. These results show that preserving already-correct work under unsupported accusation is a distinct safety challenge for long-lived agents. Our project is in https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/.

</details>


### [118] [Can Open-Weight Large Language Models (LLMs) Simulate Human Survey Populations? A Cross-Instrument Calibration Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32638)
*Grandee Lee,Wang Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Open-weight LLMs, without tuning, capture meaningful parts of human cross-instrument relationships (r≈0.7) mainly via correct sign, but fidelity varies by construct distance and model release, so release-specific verification is necessary.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assess whether open-weight LLM outputs preserve real human statistical structure (beyond surface plausibility) for use as synthetic survey respondents and digital twins, addressing a gap where prior evidence primarily involved proprietary models.

Method: Cross-instrument calibration: condition LLM-generated personas on real respondents' verbatim answers to one psychometric instrument; measure the same personas on a second, construct-distance-controlled instrument; compare simulated cross-instrument correlations against those measured in a 2,058-person human panel across 139 instrument pairs for three open-weight LLM families/releases.

Result: Across a 139-pair grid, all tested open-weight models produced simulated cross-instrument correlations that tracked human correlations at r=0.70–0.73. The agreement was driven mainly by matching the sign of correlations and concentrated in pairs with moderate construct distance. Performance varied by model release; the newest Llama release performed worse on two of three headline metrics.

Conclusion: Untuned open-weight LLMs can reproduce a substantial portion of real human cross-instrument statistical structure (simulated vs human cross-instrument correlations r≈0.70–0.73), but this fidelity is largely driven by correct sign rather than precise magnitude and is concentrated at moderate construct distances; model performance is release-dependent and not monotonic.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate synthetic survey respondents and digital twins of real people, but whether their output preserves real human statistical structure, rather than surface plausibility, remains unresolved, and most existing evidence comes from proprietary models rather than open-weight ones. We evaluate three open-weight LLM families on a cross-instrument calibration task: conditioning personas on real respondents' verbatim answers to one psychometric instrument and measuring them on a second, construct-distance-controlled instrument, checked against a 2,058-person human panel. Across a 139-pair grid, the simulated cross-instrument correlation tracks the real human correlation at r = 0.70 - 0.73 in every model, driven mainly by correct sign rather than precise magnitude and concentrated in pairs of moderate construct distance. A correlation of this magnitude, obtained from untuned open-weight models conditioned only on individual-level survey data, is a substantively encouraging result for LLM-based behavioral simulation and digital-twin applications: specific model families and releases already reproduce a meaningful share of real human cross-instrument structure without any fine-tuning. This capability does not, however, improve monotonically across model releases: on a matched panel, the newest of three tested Llama releases performs worst on two of three headline metrics, so realizing its promise in practice requires release-specific, distance-aware verification rather than a one-time benchmark.

</details>


### [119] [Business Compromise Detection with Agentic AI and LLM-driven Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32643)
*Diego Palma,Kyu Bin Kim,Zhen Han,Allbright Dsouza,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把LLM用作高召回的信号发掘器，交由ILP+朴素贝叶斯+矛盾层的神经符号仲裁器裁判，可在可解释性前提下显著提高精确率与MCC，但会降低召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告账户被攻陷用于欺诈，LLM自主代理虽能抽取高召回信号但在含糊情况下产生幻觉导致精确率低且难以审计，需要一种既保留LLM洞察力又能提供可审计、高精确率判决的方案。

Method: 把自主LLM agent作为‘调查员’输出结构化信号向量，然后用三段式神经符号仲裁器：FOIL-IE发现的符号规则、朴素贝叶斯校准层、以及基于数据调优的矛盾过滤层。评估包含过采样的妥协样本和真实低流行率样本，使用专家标签并与树模型等基线比较。

Result: 在现实低流行率样本上，MCC从0.295提升到0.435（Δ+0.139，95% CI [+0.026,+0.245], p=0.018），精确率从0.250提升到0.446（1.8×），召回从0.920降到0.660；同时优于树型集成基线（MCC 0.386）。规则保持可解释与可审计。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型仅作为信息抽取器并用神经符号（ILP规则+朴素贝叶斯校准+矛盾层）做最终仲裁，可显著提高决策的整体质量与可审计性 —— 在牺牲召回的代价下大幅提升精确率和MCC。

Abstract: Detecting compromised business ad accounts is a challenge in digital advertising, as attackers exploit hijacked accounts to launch fraudulent campaigns. Large Language Model (LLM) agents show promise for integrity enforcement, but hallucinated mistakes on hard cases create business friction. In a study we find the autonomous agent is a strong, recall-heavy signal extractor but an unreliable final arbiter, conceding precision on ambiguous decisions. We therefore keep the agent as an investigator that emits a structured, interpretable signal vector, and delegate the verdict to a neuro-symbolic stage: symbolic rules discovered by Inductive Logic Programming (FOIL-IE), a Naïve Bayes calibration layer, and a data-tuned contradiction layer. Evaluating on a compromise-over-sampled population and a realistic low-prevalence sample with subject-matter-expert labels, this arbiter substitution raises MCC from 0.295 to 0.435 (ΔMCC +0.139, 95% CI [+0.026, +0.245], p=0.018, paired bootstrap), lifting precision from 0.250 to 0.446 (1.8x) at a recall cost (0.920 to 0.660). Benchmarked under identical conditions, it also edge tree ensembles (0.386).The rules encode domain w labels while remaininginterpretable and auditable.

</details>


### [120] [From Scene Graphs to Answers: Selective Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32645)
*Yiyao Wang,Pei Liu,Fangzhou Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: By combining a hierarchical scene graph, symbolic execution, and selective LLM invocation, the paper achieves notable accuracy and efficiency gains on autonomous-driving QA, especially for counting tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing vision-language models treat heterogeneous reasoning homogeneously with a single neural inference process, ignoring that some queries admit exact symbolic solutions while others need semantic interpretation.

Method: Build a hierarchical Spatiotemporal Scene Graph (STSG) separating persistent object identities and frame-specific states; use a symbolic executor to perform exact graph operations; if symbolic execution abstains, retrieve query-conditioned graph evidence (preserving relation direction, temporal locality, semantics) and invoke an LLM for semantic reasoning.

Result: On 5,916 NuScenes-QA questions (nuScenes v1.0-mini, oracle-perception), the full system attains 80.63% accuracy with GPT-5.4-mini (+5.48 pts vs LLM-only) and similar gains with DeepSeek-V4-Flash; largest gains on counting questions (up to +12.61 pts).

Conclusion: Query-adaptive neuro-symbolic reasoning that routes queries to exact symbolic execution when possible and invokes an LLM only for semantic cases improves both accuracy and efficiency for autonomous-driving QA.

Abstract: Autonomous-driving question answering requires reasoning over structured scene information, yet existing vision-language approaches largely delegate heterogeneous reasoning operations to a single neural inference process. We argue that this uniform strategy overlooks a fundamental distinction: some queries admit exact symbolic solutions, while others require semantic interpretation. We introduce a query-adaptive neuro-symbolic reasoning framework that explicitly allocates computation according to the nature of the query. At its core is a hierarchical Spatiotemporal Scene Graph (STSG) that separates persistent object identities from frame-specific states and represents spatial relations and temporal transitions as explicit directed structures. Given a query, a symbolic executor first attempts to resolve it through exact graph operations; only when symbolic execution abstains is an LLM invoked for semantic reasoning. For these unresolved queries, query-conditioned graph retrieval and evidence filtering preserve relation direction, temporal locality, and object semantics, providing the LLM with compact and verified task-relevant evidence. This design shifts the role of the LLM from a universal reasoning engine to a targeted semantic reasoner, while allowing deterministic computation to be handled exactly and efficiently. We evaluate the framework on 5,916 NuScenes-QA questions across all ten scenes of nuScenes v1.0-mini under an oracle-perception setting. The complete system achieves 80.63 percent overall accuracy with GPT-5.4-mini, improving over the corresponding LLM-only configuration by 5.48 percentage points; with DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the improvement reaches 6.64 points. The largest gains occur on counting questions, with improvements of 10.20 and 12.61 points, respectively. These results show that selective reasoning improves both accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [121] [Prediction Limits and Koopman Closure of Geometry-Induced Soft State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32652)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 给出一个可计算且一致的下置信界，用以证实或排除对KAHM产生的软坐标用受谱范数约束的线性矩阵做精确预测的可能性；展示理论性质、Koopman联系与实证对比，但尚缺乏对排除证书的数值基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是判定几何诱导的软状态抽象（尤其是基于KAHM的软坐标）在多大程度上能被有限维线性动力学良好近似，并给出一个可计算的、可证伪的“不可达性/排除”证书，以在拟合模型前判断线性预测误差下限，从而避免盲目拟合或错误的可线性化假设。

Method: 方法包括：用KAHM重建得分将每一状态编码为单纯形值（类别特异坐标）；假定用受谱范数约束的矩阵线性预测下一个坐标，推导出最小RMSE的可计算下界，该下界由后继坐标的类内变异和软坐标相对于一热参考标签的偏离合成，并且可仅用独立样本对评估而无需拟合预测矩阵。给出一致性证明（样本下界收敛到总体下界）、分析重建评分边距对软→硬分配误差的控制，并在确定性闭合情形下研究闭合矩阵与Koopman算子的关系。最后在多种基准动力学（Duffing, Van der Pol, CartPole, MountainCar, Acrobot）上将软坐标直接线性预测与状态空间DMD/EDMD基线进行对比实验。

Result: 主要结果包括：可从样本直接计算的最小RMSE下置信界表达式；该证书随着样本数几乎必然收敛到总体下界；证书由后继坐标的类内方差和偏离一热标签的程度两部分组成；重建得分边距提供软→硬分配错误的界；在确定性闭合情况下，闭合矩阵的特征向量产生Koopman及其伴随的本征函数；构造了四态例子展示相同软坐标在不同动力学下具有不同的可闭合性；在多个动力学基准上进行了方法比较（但基准未对排除证书做数值评估）。

Conclusion: 本文的主要结论是：对于由几何诱导的软态表示（由KAHM的重建得分给出单纯形坐标），可以构造一个可计算的、关于满足谱范数上限的所有线性预测矩阵的最小均方根预测误差的下置信界。该证书可从独立的状态-后继样本直接评估、在样本量增大时几乎处处收敛到总体下界，并且重建得分的边距控制软→硬标签的分配误差。在确定性动力学且坐标闭合时，闭合矩阵及其约化转置的特征向量分别给出Koopman与伴随Koopman本征函数；但同一组软坐标在不同动力学下可分别导致精确闭合或强制正预测误差。

Abstract: We study when geometry-induced soft state abstractions admit accurate finite-dimensional linear dynamics. Each state is represented by simplex-valued coordinates obtained from class-specific Kernel Affine Hull Machine (KAHM) reconstruction scores, and a matrix is used to predict the next-state coordinates. Our main result is a computable lower confidence bound on the minimum root-mean-square prediction error over all matrices satisfying a prescribed spectral-norm limit. The bound combines within-class variation of successor coordinates with the deviation of soft coordinates from their one-hot reference labels, and can be evaluated from independent state-successor pairs without fitting a prediction matrix. For fixed coordinates and evaluation distribution, the certificate converges almost surely to a population lower bound as the sample size grows; any tolerance below this limit is eventually certified unattainable. Reconstruction-score margins further control the soft-to-hard assignment error. Under deterministic dynamics and exact coordinate closure, eigenvectors of the closure matrix and its reduced transpose induce Koopman and adjoint Koopman eigenfunctions, respectively. A four-state KAHM construction shows that identical soft coordinates can permit exact closure under one dynamics map yet force positive prediction error under another. Experiments on Duffing, Van der Pol, CartPole, MountainCar, and Acrobot compare direct soft-coordinate prediction with state-space DMD/EDMD baselines and report prediction, representation-variation, and spectral diagnostics. The benchmarks assess fitted models but do not numerically evaluate the exclusion certificate.

</details>


### [122] [MixBench-TS: A Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Benchmark Where Channel Mixing Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32656)
*Ibram Abdelmalak,Mischa Putzke,Jungmin Choi,Tom Hanika,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Shows many standard MTSF benchmarks lack cross-channel coupling; introduces reliable measures (lagged MI, CD gain) and a new benchmark MixBench-TS where CD models provide gains; recommends profiling datasets before evaluating multivariate models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many MTSF models mix channels assuming cross-channel lagged coupling, but standard benchmarks rarely check whether such coupling exists; need reliable measures and datasets to evaluate CD models.

Method: Test four coupling measures (Granger Causality, Transfer Entropy, lagged Mutual Information, and a new CD gain) on synthetic data with planted couplings; compute measures on standard datasets and chaotic ODEs; create MixBench-TS (10 datasets) with stronger coupling; evaluate six SOTA models under one tuning protocol and compare CI vs CD performance using MSE/MAE.

Result: Lagged MI and CD gain reliably detect planted couplings; GC and TE fail in some cases. Standard datasets show low coupling (median 23% lagged-coupled pairs, median CD gain -4.9%) while chaotic ODEs show high coupling (78%, +4.6%). MixBench-TS has higher coupling (median 55.5%, +1.7%). CI models outperform CD models on all standard datasets (MSE) but not on MixBench-TS.

Conclusion: Standard MTSF benchmarks lack significant cross-channel lagged coupling; CD (channel-dependent) models are not always necessary. Authors propose MixBench-TS and profiling measures to better evaluate CD models.

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Forecasting (MTSF) models that mix information across channels assume that the past of one channel carries information about the future of another. Yet they are evaluated on a small fixed set of standard datasets whose cross-channel structure is rarely examined. We ask two questions: "How can we reliably measure lagged, non-linear, and joint coupling in MTSF datasets?" and "Do the standard datasets actually have such coupling?" To answer the first, we test four candidate measures on synthetic datasets with planted ground-truth coupling: Granger Causality (GC), Transfer Entropy (TE), lagged Mutual Information (MI), and the CD gain, a model-based measure we introduce that compares a channel-dependent (CD) model to its channel-independent (CI) variant. Only lagged MI and the CD gain recover every planted coupling. For the second question, the answer is a definite no, as the standard datasets have a median of only 23% lagged-coupled channel pairs and a median CD gain of -4.9%, compared to 78% and +4.6% on chaotic ODE systems. We therefore propose MixBench-TS, a benchmark of 10 real-world datasets with a median of 55.5% lagged-coupled pairs and a median CD gain of +1.7%. Across six state-of-the-art models tuned under one protocol, CI models win on 10/10 (MSE) and 8/10 (MAE) standard datasets, but on only 3/10 and 2/10 MixBench-TS datasets. We recommend using our benchmark for evaluating new CD models. Moreover, we propose profiling new datasets with lagged MI and the CD gain before using them to evaluate multivariate models. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mixbench-ts-B027.

</details>


### [123] [World Models with Predictable Long-Horizon Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32657)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把解码的平稳分布显式化并构造保持该参考的联合state–action转移，论文实现了在长期滚动中保留数据分布的世界模型，同时区分了目标分布、收敛速率与条件动力学三者的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规模型即便能做出准确的一步预测，其长时间滚动（rollout）仍可能偏离真实数据分布。需要一种方法使模型的长期生成分布受控且可验证，以避免累积误差导致分布漂移。

Method: 学习一个将隐空间解码到固定高斯参考的解码器，并对行为-平均（behavior-averaged）转移施加保持参考分布的不变性约束。对受控系统，设计联合（state–action）变换：使用条件动作图表（conditional action chart）、联合状态-动作旋转与并行高斯噪声，得到解析且可积的保持变换；并通过有限初始化银行导出绝对收敛界，利用条件动作空间的散度来控制偏离参考的程度。

Result: 在12个控制任务上对216个像素级检查点的评估：采用参考混合物的占据（occupancy）模型在所有36个任务-种子单元的每条链上都能保持1e5步不丢链，滚动与参考的能量统计量差为-0.0002±0.0003（训练种子标准误）；四个非保持对照组均出现丢链（尽管纯高斯对照在10步处更准确）；使用离线DreamerV3作为参考能获得更好短期精度。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过显式固定参考分布（混合高斯）并约束行为-平均转移保持该参考，可以使解码后的平稳分布可控，从而在长序列滚动预测中保持数据分布不发生偏移；同时把“趋近的分布”、“趋近速率”和“条件动力学”区分开来，有助于理解并改进世界模型。

Abstract: Accurate one-step predictions do not ensure that a world model's rollouts retain the data distribution. We make the model's decoded stationary law explicit by learning a decoder of a fixed Gaussian reference and constraining the behaviour-averaged transition to preserve that reference. For controlled systems, a joint transition uses a conditional action chart to preserve behaviour occupancy without requiring invariance at each fixed action. Joint state--action rotations and parallel Gaussian noise give an exactly preserving transition with a tractable conditional density. We derive an absolute convergence bound from finite initialization banks and control departure from the reference through conditional action-space divergence. Across $216$ fitted pixel checkpoints on twelve control tasks, the occupancy model with a reference mixture retains every evaluated chain at $10^5$ steps in all $36$ task--seed cells, with a rollout-minus-reference energy-statistic difference of $-0.0002\pm0.0003$ (training-seed standard error). Each of the four nonpreserving comparison arms loses chains, although the Gaussian arm is more accurate at ten steps. An offline DreamerV3 reference also achieves better short-horizon accuracy. These results distinguish three properties of a world model: the distribution it approaches, the rate of approach, and the conditional dynamics it learns.

</details>


### [124] [Contract Memory Compiler: Resolve, Then Traverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32658)
*Zhi Song,XiMing Xing,Chunhan Li,Weian Mao,Zhenchao Tang,Hanbo Huang,Fan Xu,Jiale Zhou,Jiahui Guan,Zejian Ding,Chen Ma,Lusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CMC通过先解析关系并应用更新，再基于当前状态选取原始证据，显著提升了更新依赖场景下的多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆允许处理超出上下文窗口的长期历史，但“更新”会把多跳问题的路径引导到问题中未直接出现的实体上，因而需要在检索证据前解析更新对关系的影响。

Method: 提出Contract Memory Compiler：先用语言模型在历史中识别关系并记录其原始陈述位置，应用后续更新得到当前关系状态，然后从问题中命名的实体出发追踪这些关系并一次性把对应的原始记录传给答题模型。

Result: 在FactConsolidation上取得SOTA多跳准确率：总体78.25%，在262K设置下61.0%；若在解决更新前就选证据，多跳准确率会降到21.50%。同时构建了从MQuAKE-Remastered派生的有序记忆流数据集MQuAKE-MemStream。

Conclusion: CMC能有效解决更新依赖的证据选择问题，通过在问答前解析关系并将后续更新回溯到原始记录，使检索以当前状态为准，从而显著提高多跳问答准确率。

Abstract: External memory lets language-model agents answer questions about histories too long for the answer model's context window. Updates create a harder problem than retrieving a recent fact: changing one relation can redirect a multi-hop question to records about an entity absent from the question. We study this update-dependent evidence selection problem and introduce the Contract Memory Compiler (CMC). Before seeing a question, CMC uses a language model to identify relations in the history and record where each one was stated. It applies later updates to determine the current relations, follows them from entities named in the question, and passes the corresponding original records to the answer model in one call. Thus the current state determines which evidence is read, rather than merely refreshing values in a previously selected context. To the best of our knowledge, CMC achieves state-of-the-art multi-hop accuracy on FactConsolidation, reaching 78.25% overall and 61.0% at 262K. With the extracted relations and answer model held fixed, selecting evidence before resolving updates reduces multi-hop accuracy to 21.50%. We also introduce MQuAKE-MemStream, a derived dataset of ordered memory streams built from MQuAKE-Remastered counterfactual cases.

</details>


### [125] [Learning from a Thoughtful Teacher: Adaptive On-Policy Self-Distillation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32667)
*Jiacheng Du,Weiwei Xie,Tianyi Du,Shaoxiong Guo,Qibing Ren,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AOPSD，通过有选择地暴露可承受的推理子图并用短续写探针缓解PI泄露，从而在准确率和训练效率上均优于现有OPSD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
