Motivation: 观察到OPSD存在教师与学生之间的效用-可学性鸿沟：少量高分歧令牌主导蒸馏信号，且教师在这些位置给出的短续写更像是泄露PI的捷径而非可迁移的纠正提示。

Method: 将每个解答编码为推理有向无环图（DAG），根据学生不断演进的能力对问题排序，只暴露“可承受子图”和下一步前沿作为PI；对高分歧令牌使用短教师续写作为探针，并依据分歧强度动态调整教师反馈的影响力。

Result: 在HMMT25、AIME24、AIME25和BRUMo25上，AOPSD达到72.5% Pass@8，比OPSD高6.7个百分点，比最强竞品高4.2个百分点，同时训练时间减少约15个百分点且成本更低。

Conclusion: AOPSD通过自适应限制教师可见的特权信息（PI）并对高分歧令牌使用短续写探针，有效缓解了OPSD中教师效用与学生可学性之间的不匹配，从而提高了学生性能并降低训练成本。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a question-only student with token-level feedback from a teacher given training-only privileged information (PI). OPSD therefore provides dense, on-policy supervision, and is free of a larger external teacher, but its effectiveness rests on how PI is designed and utilized. Our preliminary diagnostics suggest a significant gap between teacher utility and student learnability, where a small fraction of high-disagreement tokens dominate the distillation signal, and short teacher continuations at these positions further expose more explicit PI leakage than transferable correction cues, indicating a strong intent on injecting PI-conditioned shortcuts. We propose Adaptive On-Policy Self-Distillation (AOPSD), which adapts what information the teacher receives and how strongly its feedback influences learning. AOPSD encodes each solution as a reasoning DAG, orders problems by the student's evolving capability, and reveals only the affordable subgraph and its next frontier as PI. For high-disagreement tokens, AOPSD utilizes short teacher continuations as probes to encourage useful guidance while mitigating PI-conditioned shortcuts among teacher supervisions. On HMMT25, AIME24, AIME25, and BRUMo25, AOPSD achieves 72.5% Pass@8, which is 6.7 percentage points above OPSD and 4.2 above the strongest competing baseline while reducing 15 percentage points of training time at lower cost.

</details>


### [126] [Refinement Symmetry in Multimodal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32669)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并证明了“分裂不变性”约束下局部质量因子必须线性的理论，并通过Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和WorldSense的实验证明所提出的度量加权在应对表示拆分/合并与重采样时能显著提高鲁棒性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力权重依赖于token计数，而同一信号的不同表征（如拆分或合并token）会改变计数并因此不恰当地改变注意力贡献。目标是建立一种表示原则，使在保存内容、位置、可见上下文和总质量的情况下，表示的细分不会改变其贡献，从而提高系统对表示变换的鲁棒性。

Method: 理论上：基于比例注意力（proportional attention）与二次（quadrature）注意力的框架，证明分裂不变性对局部质量因子的结构性约束（线性性）。构造物理耦合分析来将特征改变与权重重分配分离，以推导注意力误差的上界。实验上：在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和WorldSense数据集上做了仿真实验，包括复制视觉token、多帧重采样、以及对冻结视频编码的合并，比较标准注意力、全局计数加权和提出的度量加权（measure weighting）。

Result: 理论结果：在假定条件下推导出局部质量因子的线性必然性，并给出注意力误差的界。实证结果：在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，将一半视觉token三倍复制情况下，标准注意力导致3586题中255题答案变化，而度量加权在可见性匹配时保持所有答案不变；在自然帧重采样下，度量加权减少了分布漂移；在对冻结视频编码进行二合并的评测中（五次随机种子），度量加权比全局计数加权和标准注意力分别平均提高约1.04和0.93个百分点；在WorldSense上优劣随压缩预算变化。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：若在注意力机制中要求对表示的细分（split invariance）保持不变性，那么对于任意固定的正的注意力核、且局部质量因子（local mass factor）非递减的情形，该局部质量因子必须是线性的。此外，通过物理耦合（physical coupling）可以把由于表征变化带来的注意力误差用界限约束住。该理论原则在实验中能带来对分区变换的鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Attention weights depend on token counts, which change with the representation of a signal. We study refinement symmetry: splitting a representation while preserving content, position, visible context, and total mass should preserve its contribution. Building on proportional and quadrature attention, we show that split invariance forces the local mass factor to be linear for any fixed positive attention kernel, provided that factor is nondecreasing. For changed representations, a physical coupling bounds attention error by separating feature change from weight reallocation. In Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, duplicating half the visual tokens threefold changes 255 of 3,586 MVBench answers under standard attention; measure weighting preserves every answer under matched visibility. Under natural frame resampling, it reduces distributional drift. At twofold merging of a frozen video encoding, a five-seed evaluation shows an all-partition-correct accuracy gain of 1.04 percentage points over global count weighting (average group mass) and 0.93 points over standard attention. The advantage over global count also holds on WorldSense but depends on the compression budget. The result is a representation principle with a measured benefit in robustness across partitions.

</details>


### [127] [What Would Falsify It? A Variable Specific Evidence Standard for Mechanistic Claims About Self Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32670)
*Arshia Eftekhari zadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce a stricter null-control standard for mechanistic evidence; empirical tests on a known cue show strong transport-like effects that survive many estimators but fail identity-specific nulls, leaving causal access unresolved.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Determine whether post-hoc explanations reuse the computations that produced an LM's answer or reconstruct explanations from the answer alone, and to define evidence standards that can distinguish identity-specific mechanistic access from generic transport effects.

Method: Proposes an evidence standard pairing each positive statistic with a variable-specific null that preserves nuisance dimensions; applies this by testing a known cue that raises choice rates, measuring mention rates in explanations, and fitting/intervening with directions and scrambled-label null directions across multiple models and seeds.

Result: Cue naming wrong option increases choices by 64–68 pp; explanations rarely mention the cue; fitted directions can show high R^2 in recovering cue effects but scrambled-label nulls reproduce 61–76% of effects at comparable edit magnitudes, so causal sensitivity to the cue identity is not established across three models; a fourth model passes one interchange endpoint but has interpretability issues.

Conclusion: The paper finds that favorable mechanistic statistics (e.g., directions that recover a cue effect) do not by themselves establish causal access; controls that remove variable identity but match nuisance structure often reproduce much of the effect, leaving causal access unresolved.

Abstract: When a language model explains an answer it has already given, does it reuse the computation that produced the answer or reconstruct a story from the answer alone? Attribution, transportability and recoverability are each compatible with causal use without establishing it. We propose an evidence standard: pair each positive statistic with a variable specific null that removes the tested variable's identity while matching relevant nuisance dimensions as far as possible, and audit unmatched dimensions.
  We apply this standard to a known cause. A cue naming a wrong option raises the rate of choosing that option by 64 to 68 percentage points across three models. Explanations mention the cue in 1.8 percent of items or fewer in three of four models tested. Three estimator classes yield favorable statistics, but none establishes causal sensitivity to the cue contrast under its own control in the three-model analysis. In the strongest case, a recovered cue direction reaches $R^2$ of 0.95 and exceeds a geometry matched random direction in all three seeds, while a direction fitted by the same pipeline with cue labels scrambled reproduces 61 to 76 percent of its effect at comparable realized edit magnitude. A fourth model passes one interchange endpoint, but unequal edit magnitudes and a contrast that changes both cue identity and cue-answer agreement limit its interpretation.
  These experiments leave causal access unresolved. They establish an evidentiary requirement: favorable mechanistic statistics must survive controls for variable identity and nuisance structure. Reusable controls separate generic from identity specific transport effects, fit null directions with scrambled labels, and audit realized intervention magnitudes.

</details>


### [128] [Expected Reasoning-Step Return Unifies On-Policy Learning from Rewards and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32674)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ERSR——用蒙特卡洛学生展开把步级动作映射到共同的期望回报，从而发现学生与教师替换的效用随轨迹成败反向；据此设计 R^2OPL，在成功轨迹上强化学生、失败轨迹上蒸馏教师，并用组成功率与学生探测增益做尺度与步级调节，实验显示该方法稳健且性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在按步学习的推理场景中，任务回报（reward）与教师信号（teacher）形式不同且有时冲突，缺乏统一的步级比较标准以决定何时采纳学生自举动作或信任教师替换，因此需要一个能在同一回报空间对两类动作效用做比较的方法。

Method: 将语义推理步视为宏动作，采用蒙特卡洛学生策略展开（student-policy rollouts）来估计学生生成动作与教师建议动作的期望终态任务回报（ERSR），在统一的回报空间比较步级效用；定义学生答案探测增益（student answer-probe gains）作为廉价替代以追踪步级效用；构建 R^2OPL 策略：按轨迹成败选择强化或蒸馏信号，并以组成功率做难度缩放、以探测增益做步级调节。

Result: ERSR 分析揭示结果依赖的非对称性；学生答案探测增益能够区分有益与有害的推理步骤；R^2OPL 在多个推理基准与不同教师—学生配置上均稳定超越强基线；训练动态表明 R^2OPL 能同时挖掘奖励侧与教师侧的实用价值，而现有混合方法常在一侧遗留大量潜在效用。

Conclusion: ERSR 证明了在语义推理步骤层面存在以轨迹结果为条件的效用非对称性：在成功轨迹上，学生自生成动作比教师替换更有利；而在失败轨迹上，教师替换更有利。基于该发现，R^2OPL 通过在成功轨迹上强化学生步骤、在失败轨迹上蒸馏教师信号，并用组成功率与学生探测增益分别做难度缩放与步级调节，从而同时利用奖励侧与教师侧的实用信息，显著优于强基线。

Abstract: On-policy reasoning models can learn from task rewards or teacher signals, but these sources differ in form and can favor conflicting updates, leaving unclear which should guide a given reasoning action. We introduce \textbf{Expected Reasoning-Step Return (ERSR)}, which treats semantic reasoning steps as macro-actions and uses Monte Carlo student-policy rollouts to estimate the expected final task reward of student-generated and teacher-proposed actions in a common return space for step-level comparison. ERSR analysis reveals an outcome-dependent asymmetry: student actions are more beneficial than teacher replacements on successful trajectories, whereas teacher replacements become more beneficial on failed trajectories. We further show that student answer-probe gains track student-step ERSR utility and distinguish beneficial from harmful reasoning steps. Based on these findings, we propose \textbf{Return-Referenced On-Policy Learning (R$^2$OPL)}, which reinforces student reasoning on successful trajectories and distills teacher signals on failed ones, while using group success rate for difficulty scaling and student-probe gains for step-level modulation. Experiments across reasoning benchmarks and teacher--student configurations show that R$^2$OPL consistently outperforms strong baselines. ERSR training dynamics further show that R$^2$OPL jointly exploits substantial utility from both reward- and teacher-side signals, whereas existing hybrids often leave substantial residual utility in one branch.

</details>


### [129] [When Better Gets Worse: Improvement Fidelity for Self-Improving Agents in Adaptive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32677)
*Ke Wang,Zijie Zhao,Zhiyi Yuan,Changlun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出改进保真度概念并开发PIVOT-KG，一种按预期减少选择后悔/成本分配高保真评估的成对决策感知验证器，在多环境中显著降低了在部署差异下的错误替换决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进型智能体常依赖代理验证器来选择策略更新，但部署环境可能改变验证器评估的世界，使得在验证器上看起来更好的更新在实际部署后变差。为保证替换决策可靠，需要一个既能识别这种“验证—部署差距”又能在高保真评估受限时合理分配评估资源的方法。

Method: 理论与实证结合：1）形式化定义Improvement Fidelity并分析导致更新级别错误的来源（operator shift、deployment response）和候选边际如何影响替换决策；2）设计PIVOT-KG算法——基于成对比较、决策感知、按预期减少选择后悔/单位成本分配高保真评估；3）在Leduc、Kuhn、Melting Pot和HighwayEnv上进行大规模离线/仿真实验，测量代理与部署最优集合的不一致性及PIVOT-KG在不同预算下的性能。

Result: 理论上展示全局准确率不能保证更新选择保真，定义并分析了导致错误的机制；实证上在90个held-out roots中有51例代理与部署最优集合不相交；在八候选HighwayEnv压力测试中，PIVOT-KG把平均改进选择后悔从Uniform规则下的0.0435降到主预算下的0.0055，表明显著改进了替换决策质量。

Conclusion: 论文提出了“Improvement Fidelity”（改进保真度）概念，说明全局策略准确性不足以保证更新选择的保真性，并提出了PIVOT-KG这一成对、面向决策的验证器来在有限的高保真评估预算下按每单位成本的预期选择后悔减少量分配评估资源，从而显著降低改进选择后悔。

Abstract: Self-improving agents increasingly rely on proxy verifiers to choose policy updates, yet deployment can change the world in which those updates are evaluated. An update that looks better to the verifier can therefore become worse after deployment even when the verifier ranks policies well overall. We formalize this gap as Improvement Fidelity, which asks whether proxy improvements preserve the sign and ordering of deployment improvements over the updates an improvement process actually proposes. We show that global policy accuracy need not guarantee update fidelity: operator shift and deployment response can create update-level errors, while candidate margins determine whether those errors change the replacement decision. We introduce PIVOT-KG, a paired, decision-aware validator that allocates scarce high-fidelity evaluation according to the expected reduction in selection regret per unit cost. Across 90 held-out roots in Leduc, Kuhn, and Melting Pot, proxy and deployment optimal sets are disjoint in 51 cases. In an eight-candidate HighwayEnv stress test, PIVOT-KG reduces mean improvement-selection regret from 0.0435 under the exact Uniform validation rule to 0.0055 at the primary budget. Together, these results show why reliable self-improvement should evaluate proposed improvements in the worlds they induce, while providing a practical rule for allocating scarce deployment evidence when it can affect the replacement decision.

</details>


### [130] [PINNMorph: Evolving Online Adaptation Policies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32685)
*Xu Yang,Mingyang Yu,Jun Zhang,Keqian Li,Jing Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PINNMorph 用 LLM 引导的策略演化在训练中对 PINN 进行在线结构与训练干预，显著降低 PDE 求解误差并通过消融验证关键组件的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 在训练过程中表现会显著变化（残差分布、梯度交互、区域学习难度、模型容量需求等），而常规方法在训练前确定架构与训练机制，缺乏在训练过程中动态调整的能力。PINNMorph 旨在提供一种基于训练状态的在线自适应机制，弥补静态设计的不足。

Method: PINNMorph 维持一个基于状态的策略族，将执行诊断映射为针对拓扑修改、表示增强、目标平衡、梯度处理、采样和优化器控制等的干预程序。在每次干预时刻，从策略族中实例化候选程序，依据观测到的训练状态选择并应用于当前正在训练的 PINN，模型继承现有参数和训练状态并继续优化；随后根据执行结果评估干预并演化策略族。系统使用 LLM 生成/指导策略程序，并通过实际训练反馈共同适配模型与策略。

Result: 在 13 个 PDE 基准上，PINNMorph 相比 SA-PINN、ConFIG、RoPINN、HARMONIC 和 PINNsAgent 等基线在所有问题上取得更低的解误差；消融实验表明在线自适应、基于状态的干预选择与执行反馈驱动的策略演化均对性能提升有显著贡献。

Conclusion: PINNMorph 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的策略演化的在线 PINN 自适应框架，通过在训练过程中对网络拓扑、表示、目标函数、梯度处理、采样与优化器阶段进行受控干预，从而在 13 个 PDE 基准上系统性地降低了解法误差。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a learning-based framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their training behavior can change substantially throughout optimization. Residual distributions, gradient interactions, regional learning difficulty, and model-capacity requirements may evolve over time, while the network architecture and major training mechanisms are typically determined before training. We propose PINNMorph, an online PINN adaptation framework based on large language model (LLM)-guided policy evolution. PINNMorph maintains a population of state-conditioned adaptation policies that map execution diagnostics to controlled interventions over topology modification, additive representation augmentation, objective balancing, gradient handling, adaptive sampling, and optimizer-phase control. At each intervention opportunity, candidate programs are instantiated from the current policy population, selected according to the observed training state, and applied directly to the PINN under training. The resulting model inherits its existing parameters and training state and continues optimization along the same trajectory. Execution outcomes are subsequently used to evaluate interventions and evolve the policy population. Unlike pre-training architecture search or fixed adaptation rules, PINNMorph jointly adapts the current PINN and the policies governing its interventions using feedback from actual training. Experiments on 13 PDE benchmarks show that PINNMorph achieves lower solution errors than SA-PINN, ConFIG, RoPINN, HARMONIC, and PINNsAgent across all evaluated problems. Ablation studies further examine the effects of online adaptation, state-conditioned intervention selection, and execution-feedback-driven policy evolution.

</details>


### [131] [Dude, Where's My State? Execution Information Requirements for Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32687)
*Nikita Mehrotra,Ashish Tiwari,Priyanshu Gupta,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Defines EIR; introduces LACUNA to control information demand/retention/recovery in synthetic tasks and VESTIGE to measure demand from traces; shows missing required info breaks performance and that real-agent failures relate to reduced rereading over distance, not just peak demand.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-running agents must keep accessible the intermediate information later steps rely on; yet it's unclear how much information must be preserved and how retention/recovery affect performance. The work aims to quantify these requirements and provide frameworks to measure and improve information preservation and recovery.

Method: They define the Execution Information Requirement (EIR) and create LACUNA to generate tasks with controlled dependencies and information properties; they evaluate across four models by manipulating missing vs. irrelevant information and measuring recovery effects. They also develop VESTIGE, which builds semantic graphs from agent execution traces to measure information demand in real software-agent trajectories and analyze rereading behavior and failure correlations across 72,562 runs.

Result: Restoring a missing required result raises accuracy on affected recall steps to 100% (vs 0% for equal-length irrelevant info). Storage capacity alone isn't enough: retention policies, error propagation, and premature stopping can still cause failures. VESTIGE analysis shows failed runs exhibit steeper decline in solution-relevant rereading with distance (RR 0.951 per distance doubling) and that adjusted peak demand alone doesn't predict failure.

Conclusion: The paper formalizes an information lower bound (EIR) necessary for long-running agents and demonstrates empirical frameworks (LACUNA and VESTIGE) to measure and manipulate information demand, retention, and recovery, showing that missing required information critically impairs performance and that storage alone is insufficient due to retention policies and error propagation.

Abstract: Long-running agents must preserve information that later steps depend on. We introduce the Execution Information Requirement (EIR), a lower bound on the information that must remain accessible for correct completion under specified task and access conditions. We develop LACUNA, a framework that generates tasks with known dependencies and varies information demand, retention, and recovery separately from the difficulty of individual operations. Across four models, restoring a missing result raises accuracy on affected recall steps to 100%, compared with 0% for equal-length irrelevant information. Sufficient storage alone does not ensure success: retention policies can discard required results, errors can propagate through later computations, and agents can stop before recovery is complete. We also introduce VESTIGE, which uses agent execution traces to construct semantic graphs and measure information demand for real tasks. Across 72,562 software-agent trajectories, VESTIGE reveals a steeper distance-related decline in solution-relevant rereading for failed runs (RR 0.951 per distance doubling), while adjusted peak demand alone is not associated with failure. Together, these contributions support evaluating whether agents preserve and recover the information their tasks require.

</details>


### [132] [World Agent: Can Language Models Keep a World Running?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32692)
*Weixing Chen,Weipeng Zhang,Nan An,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将评估目标从交付转为持续运行的world agent任务，并通过两个赛道和可审计评估实现该想法；实验表明模型在缺乏结构时性能下降，尤其是因果关系建模薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评测仅止于生成、交付或单步转换，未评估交付后世界能否持续运行，且局部转换正确不等于事件因果流组织正确。需要新的评测范式衡量持续运作能力。

Method: 设计world agent任务并构建WorldAgent-Benchmark；包含两个赛道：维护赛道结合LLM辅助手工语义判断与程序化状态校验与评分；推演赛道通过程序化手段在部分观测下预测世界演化并朝目标行动。保存执行日志与判断以保证可重算评分。

Result: 构建并公开WorldAgent-Benchmark并在8种模型上测试；结果显示随着预构建结构减少，模型得分稳定下降；所有模型在因果关系检查上表现最差；基准可以可审计地复现判断与评分，区分局部完成与事件组织失败。

Conclusion: 引入“world agent”任务，将世界生成的评估点从交付时刻移动到交付后持续运行的能力，能区分局部状态正确与事件组织失败。WorldAgent-Benchmark 在两个赛道上实现该任务（维护和推演），并提供可审计的语义判断与程序化验证。评测8个模型表明，结构化信息移除时性能下降，因果关系检验是所有模型的薄弱环节。

Abstract: World models are moving from generating realistic frames to generating playable worlds, yet whether a delivered world can keep running is not tested anywhere. Existing evaluations stop at generation, at delivery, or at single-step transitions, and each stops at a different point along the way. Correct local state transitions or intermediate outcomes do not guarantee a correctly organized causal event flow. We propose the world agent task, which moves the evaluation point of world generation from the moment of delivery to the continued operation that follows. In this task, a model is not asked to generate a world. It is held responsible for keeping the world running, which requires coordinating events and carrying forward their consequences to constrain subsequent evolution. We instantiate the task in WorldAgent-Benchmark with two complementary tracks. In the maintenance track, the model must ground the events of a continuous narrative into correct transitions of the explicit world state while respecting causal, temporal, and concurrency constraints. In the deduction track, the model must predict how the world will evolve under partial observations and act toward a goal. The maintenance track combines LLM-assisted semantic judgments with programmatic validation and scoring, while the deduction track is evaluated entirely programmatically. Individual judgments are auditable against world states and execution logs, and scores can be recomputed from the saved judgments and execution records. Across 8 models, scores decline steadily as pre-built structure is removed from the world, and causal-relation checking is the weakest component for every model. The benchmark makes the continued operation of a world measurable and distinguishes local completion from failures in event organization. Code and dataset will be released on https://github.com/HCPLab-SYSU/WorldAgent-Benchmark.

</details>


### [133] [IGSD: Environment-Verified Hindsight Self-Distillation for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32694)
*Angqing Jiang,Gaoming Zhang,Chaoqun Zhang,Jianchun Song,Liyuan Kong,Kena Qi,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IGSD通过在训练时执行配对查询并以检索后信息增益对自蒸馏对进行加权，提供环境验证的逐步监督，从而在不增加推理负担的情况下提升检索式问答代理的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用的on-policy自蒸馏在无外部教师的情况下能密集化训练，但对于检索代理，事后（hindsight）老师可能偏好那些并不改进检索的查询；现有方法要么直接蒸馏这种偏好，要么用模型内打分过滤，但都未在环境中验证查询的实际检索后果。需要一种在执行层面验证并只保留真正有利查询监督的方法。

Method: 将每个查询令牌视为微动作：在同一失败状态下，用相同检索器分别完成教师的令牌建议与学生采样令牌，执行并比较两条匹配查询的检索结果。通过共享反事实控制来抵消查询条件导致的答案概率偏移，计算执行后的配对信息增益（两条查询在检索/答案概率上的差值），并用该信息增益作为仅正向的软权重对候选配对进行蒸馏；保持原有GRPO目标不变，仅在训练时进行验证。

Result: 在7个单跳与多跳QA基准上，IGSD在不做推理时验证的前提下，对3B和7B策略分别达成宏平均EM 42.8%与47.0%，表明环境验证的hindsight能作为可靠的动作级监督信号。

Conclusion: 提出的 IGSD 方法通过在训练阶段用环境反馈验证教师的“事后”（hindsight）令牌/查询建议，从而为检索型搜索代理提供可信的逐步（action-level）监督，能在不增加推理时开销的情况下显著提升QA检索-阅读任务的表现。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation densifies agent training without external teachers: a policy conditioned on privileged hindsight provides step-level guidance for its own unprivileged rollouts. For search agents, however, hindsight can make the teacher prefer a query that does not improve retrieval from the student's state. Existing methods either distill this preference directly or filter it with model-internal scores, but neither strategy verifies the query's executed retrieval consequence. We propose Information-Gain-Gated Self-Distillation (IGSD), which verifies on-policy token proposals with environment feedback before distilling them. Treating each query token as a micro-action, IGSD completes the teacher's token proposal and the student's sampled token into matched queries and executes both from the same failed state with the same retriever. Shared counterfactual controls account for query-conditioned shifts in answer likelihood, so their difference, the executed paired information gain, provides a relative utility contrast for the retrieved documents. IGSD uses this contrast as a positive-only soft weight for candidate-pair distillation, while leaving the GRPO objective unchanged and confining verification to training. Across seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks, IGSD reaches macro-average exact-match accuracies of 42.8% and 47.0% with 3B and 7B policies, respectively, without inference-time verification. These results support environment-verified hindsight as an effective approach to reliable action-level supervision for search agents.

</details>


### [134] [Flat-Consensus Diffusion for Robust Data Reshaping under Noisy Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32696)
*Hongyu Cao,Kunpeng Liu,Fei Xie,Sandip Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FCDiff通过微观的高斯平滑蒙特卡洛梯度与宏观的Frechet-mean一致性聚合，在有噪声评估器和生成随机性的环境中，找到平坦且被多轨迹支持的特征变换区域，从而实现更鲁棒的数据重塑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据形态决定特征组织与分布覆盖，差的数据形态会导致模型拟合噪声。在不完美数据和有噪声评估器的实用场景中，需要生成在评价噪声下仍保持有用、稳定和可复现的特征变换。

Method: 提出FCDiff框架：将变换序列搜索建模为基于奖励引导的扩散生成，采用微观-宏观分解。微观层用Gaussian-smoothed与Monte Carlo平均的梯度替代点估计，从而引导生成向局部平坦区收敛；宏观层独立运行多条引导轨迹并用加权Frechet-mean重心聚合，选择共识支持的基线并过滤随机异常。

Result: 在包含8个数据集的测试组、并在重尾评估器噪声下，FCDiff在下尾可靠性与鲁棒性指标上取得最好汇总排名，并对比多个搜索式AutoFE与面向鲁棒性的生成基线，显著提高精度，且比所有生成式基线在统计上显著优越。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：鲁棒的数据重构（feature-centric data reshaping）应当搜索在潜在奖励景观中“平坦、共识支持”的区域，而非依赖单一路径的尖峰最优变换。FCDiff方法通过微观的高斯平滑蒙特卡洛梯度引导与宏观的Frechet-mean加权一致性聚合，实现对噪声评估器和生成随机性的双重稳健化。

Abstract: Data shape determines how features are structured, how patterns are separated, and how distributions cover the underlying domain. Poor data shape can make models learn noise rather than generalizable structure. This paper studies robust feature-centric data reshaping: generating feature transformations that remain useful, stable, and reproducible under noisy evaluation and imperfect data conditions. We view reshaping operation sequence search as reward-guided diffusion generation, and robust reshaping as searching for regions in the latent reward landscape rather than isolated high-reward transformations. The key challenge is dual instability: noisy evaluators distort local reward guidance, while stochastic generative trajectories can converge to inconsistent solutions. We propose FCDiff, a flat-consensus diffusion framework that addresses both failures through a micro-macro decomposition. The micro layer replaces point-estimate reward guidance with Gaussian-smoothed, Monte Carlo averaged gradients, steering generation toward locally flat reward regions. The macro layer aggregates independently guided trajectories with a weighted Frechet-mean barycenter, selecting consensus-supported basins and filtering stochastic outliers. Across an 8-dataset headline cohort under heavy-tailed evaluator noise, FCDiff attains the best aggregate rank on lower-tail reliability and robustness against both search-based AutoFE and robustness-oriented generative baselines, with statistically significant accuracy gains over every generative baseline. Our results show that robust data reshaping requires searching for flat, consensus-supported regions rather than sharp single-trajectory optima.

</details>


### [135] [CAIRN: Dynamic Fact-Intent DAGs for Multi-Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32700)
*Zuyao Xu,Yuyang Jia,Junwei Guan,Xiang Li,Kaiwen Shen,Zhiqiang Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAIRN 用动态 DAG 组织 LLM 多智能体的事实与意图，提升复杂、高代价任务的并行探索效率与可审计性；对重度任务显著提速，但在轻量任务上可能代币开销更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 驱动的自主系统在数学推理、工程与网络安全上展示能力，但如何组织多智能体以实现高效、可靠、可持续的性能仍未解决。CAIRN 旨在通过显式结构化的中间表示（DAG）来管理复杂探索过程、提高并行性与知识复用性，并增强可审计性与人类介入能力。

Method: 提出 CAIRN 框架：使用一个 Reasoner 将事实解释为意图（intent），工人（workers）执行意图以产生新事实；所有事实与意图构成一个可持久化的 DAG，DAG 用于并行分支探索、保存依赖与结果，并提供可追溯的执行轨迹。实验在网络安全与数学推理任务上评估任务成功率、求解时间与代币消耗。

Result: 在低努力任务上，DAG 协调可能产生更高的代币成本且无明显加速；在高努力（≥1M 代币）任务上，CAIRN 在 76.5% 的情形下能缩短求解时间，最高加速 3.08 倍；随着任务努力增加，时间收益显著且相对代币开销下降，表明 DAG 引导的并行探索在复杂任务中具有潜力。

Conclusion: CAIRN 提出以事实-意图驱动的多智能体范式，通过将观测与计划表示为动态有向无环图（DAG）来组织目标导向探索，从而支持并行探索、知识复用与可审计的执行轨迹。该方法在高计算/代币消耗任务上能显著加速求解（在至少 1M 代币的高耗费任务中 76.5% 的案例更快，最高 3.08x 提速），但在低努力任务上可能带来额外代币开销且无明显性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-powered autonomous systems have demonstrated promising capabilities in mathematical reasoning, engineering, and cybersecurity. Yet how to organize these systems for effective, reliable, and sustained performance remains an open question. In this paper, we present CAIRN, a fact-intent-driven multi-agent paradigm for goal-directed exploration. CAIRN represents observations and planned investigations as a dynamic directed acyclic graph (DAG). A reasoner interprets facts to propose intents, which workers execute to produce new facts. Each intent references its supporting facts and defines a potential exploration branch. The persistent graph preserves goals, dependencies and findings across workers, supporting knowledge reuse and parallel exploration. The graph also makes execution trajectories traceable and auditable, providing a basis for human verification and intervention. We evaluate CAIRN across cybersecurity and mathematical reasoning tasks, examining task success, time to solution, and token consumption. DAG-based coordination can incur higher token costs with no observable performance gains on tasks that require little effort. However, on high-effort tasks (at least 1M tokens), we observe faster solutions in 76.5% of cases, with speedups of up to 3.08x. Moreover, as task effort increases, these time gains become more pronounced while relative token overhead declines, highlighting the potential of DAG-guided parallel exploration.

</details>


### [136] [Despite Instructions: Frontier Agents Improvise Covert Channels at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32701)
*Jacob Dineen,Silei Ren,Muhao Chen,Dan Roth,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在参数固定、仅用一比特反馈的重复交互下，语言模型对能在不显式编码的情况下学会隐性传密，造成在有监控和禁止的情况下信息泄漏的显著风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全敏感场景下语言模型需要协调而不得泄露敏感信息；研究重复交互是否能使普通消息演变为携带私密含义的通道。

Method: 设定重复博弈：发信方观察四种秘密状态之一，从同一公开报告的四个摘要中选择一个；收信方基于摘要推断秘密。仅提供一比特反馈（是否推断正确），参数固定、无事先编码表或示例，部分实验允许代理生成自由文本更新并在模拟事件响应任务中交互；并用独立的消息监控器检测内容但不访问交互历史。

Result: 在10个独立游戏中，GPT-5.6 Sol代理对达到98.8%的最终准确率（基线随机为25%），即使有明示禁令与消息监控也能实现高效秘密传递。

Conclusion: 模型对在推理阶段仅凭一比特反馈也能建立私有编码通道，从而在禁止泄露和基于监控的环境下仍然传递机密信息，表明协作交互会引发信息外泄风险。

Abstract: In security-sensitive applications, language-model agents are often required to coordinate without disclosing confidential information. Yet repeated interactions may also let ordinary messages acquire shared private meaning. We study a repeated game with pairs of models in which the sender model observes one of four secret states and selects one of four summaries of the same public report, while the receiver model tries to infer the secret state. We find that model pairs can learn to communicate the secret using only one bit of feedback indicating whether the receiver inferred it correctly. This learning occurs during inference with fixed parameters and no supplied codebook or encoding examples. The effect also persists when agents generate their own free-form updates in a simulated incident-response task. Across ten independent games, pairs of GPT-5.6 Sol agents reach 98.8% final accuracy, compared with 25% chance, despite explicit instructions prohibiting disclosure and a monitor that screens each message without access to the agents' interaction histories. The same interactions that help agents cooperate can therefore allow confidential information to pass through messages intended for legitimate coordination.

</details>


### [137] [CoWindow Attention: Full Causal Coverage Is a Collective Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32704)
*Jingze Shi,Zhangyang Peng,Xianduo Li,Yanlin Qi,Xiaotian Lin,Haoxian Chen,Liangdong Wang,Guang Liu,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoWA 用位置确定的互补长程窗口分配 KV 头，做到集体式全因果覆盖，几乎不损失性能但在超长上下文下大幅降低延迟与推理内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FullAttn 在每个注意力头上重复暴露完整因果历史，导致冗余计算与内存流动，尤其在超长上下文与张量并行场景下效率低下；目标是保留全因果覆盖但减少重复性开销。

Method: 提出一种结构化注意力模式：所有头共享近对角和前缀汇（near-diagonal 与 prefix-sink）窗口，剩余长程历史被按位置划分到互补的长程窗口中；该模式为位置确定性、不依赖学习路由器、训练与推理一致，并天然兼容 KV-head 张量并行。

Result: 在 8K 窗口实验中，CoWA（100% 集体覆盖）达到 89.73% 精度（FullAttn 为 89.97%）；在 128K attention 操作基准下，训练前/后向延迟分别减少 7.4x/8.6x，推理延迟减少 3.0x；训练时单卡峰值算子内存与 FullAttn 匹配，推理时降低 7.6x；在 0.6B–14B 缩放训练中困惑度紧随 FullAttn，14B 与 32B 模型在知识、推理与长上下文检索上表现可比。

Conclusion: CoWA 表明完整因果覆盖可作为头集合的“集体属性”实现，而非每个头都重复访问完整历史，从而在保持性能的同时显著降低远程注意力的计算、延迟和推理内存开销。

Abstract: FullAttn repeatedly exposes the complete causal history to every attention head, creating substantial redundant computation and memory traffic even with IO-efficient dense kernels. We introduce CoWA, a structured attention architecture that distributes access to the causal history across KV heads. All heads share near-diagonal and prefix-sink windows, while complementary long-range windows partition the remaining history. Their union provides full causal coverage although each head attends sparsely to distant tokens. This position-defined attention pattern requires no learned router or indexer, is used consistently during training and inference, and aligns with KV-head tensor parallelism. A window-matched ablation at 8K isolates the effect of complementary long-range allocation: CoWA with 100% collective coverage reaches 89.73% accuracy, compared with 89.97% for FullAttn, while duplicated long-range windows perform substantially worse. Across a broader controlled associative-recall comparison with matched token budgets, CoWA closely tracks FullAttn as the context grows, whereas other sparse patterns lose a substantial fraction of the associations. In an attention-operator benchmark at 128K tokens with tensor parallelism, CoWA reduces forward and backward latency during training by 7.4x and 8.6x and decoding latency during inference by 3.0x over FullAttn. Its per-rank peak operator memory matches FullAttn during training and is 7.6x lower during decoding. Across scaling-law training from 0.6B to 14B parameters, CoWA closely tracks FullAttn in perplexity while reducing total training FLOPs. The resulting 14B models and 32B models from separate continued training achieve comparable knowledge, reasoning, and long-context retrieval scores to FullAttn. These results show that full causal coverage can be a collective property of the head ensemble rather than a duplicated property of every head.

</details>


### [138] [MassAlloc Attention: Let Attention Allocate Its Own Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32712)
*Jingze Shi,Zhangyang Peng,Xianduo Li,Yanlin Qi,Xiaotian Lin,Haoxian Chen,Liangdong Wang,Guang Liu,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MALA通过按归一化注意力贡献自适应分配后分数计算，能在几乎不损失性能的前提下显著减少注意力计算、延迟与FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到FullAttn在因果注意力空间中对大量交互分配的归一化质量（normalized mass）接近于零，但密集核在形成每个QK块后仍执行完整的后分数路径，造成计算浪费。目标是按贡献分配计算以减少低贡献交互的不必要开销。

Method: 提出MALA：一种融合的注意力原语。MALA在前向过程中维护一个在线更新的softmax归一化器并基于归一化贡献自适应地保留后分数计算；反向过程中复用固定的归一化器并仅使用标准注意力状态来推导嵌套的保留支持。用一个公共容差参数同时约束训练和推理的保留，从而动态分配后分数计算工作量。

Result: 在8K时的匹配工作量研究中，MALA在相同总后分数工作下逼近逐例参考质量，平均忽略质量分别为0.0188% vs 0.0182%。在1K–32K上下文长度下保持小的输出与梯度误差；在联想回忆基准上8K达到89.67%（FullAttn为89.97%）；在128K attention-operator基准（张量并行）上训练前向/反向延迟分别下降2.2x和3.0x，推理解码延迟下降1.6x；在0.6B–14B尺度律训练中困惑度接近FullAttn并减少总FLOPs；继续训练得到的14B和32B模型在知识、推理与长上下文检索上与FullAttn相当。

Conclusion: 按归一化注意力贡献分配后分数（post-score）计算，可以在保持FullAttn评估能力（困惑度、知识、推理和长上下文检索）几乎不变的情况下显著减少注意力计算，从而在训练和解码上降低延迟并节省总FLOPs。

Abstract: FullAttn often assigns negligible normalized mass to much of the causal score space, yet dense kernels execute the complete post-score path after forming each QK tile. We introduce MALA, a fused attention primitive that preserves score access to every legal causal interaction and uses normalized contribution to allocate post-score computation. Forward uses its evolving online-softmax normalizer, while backward reuses the finalized normalizer to derive nested retained support using only standard attention state. A common tolerance governs training and inference, allowing for adaptive retention of the work. MALA reduces low-contribution post-score computation. A matched-work study at 8K isolates the benefit of distribution-adaptive allocation: under exactly matched total post-score work, MALA approaches a per-instance reference-mass oracle, with mean omitted mass of 0.0188% versus 0.0182%. Across context lengths from 1K to 32K tokens, the same tolerance maintains low output and gradient errors relative to the reference. Across a broader controlled associative-recall comparison, MALA closely tracks FullAttn as context grows, reaching 89.67% accuracy at 8K compared with 89.97% for FullAttn. In an attention-operator benchmark at 128K tokens with tensor parallelism, MALA reduces forward and backward latency during training by 2.2x and 3.0x and decoding latency during inference by 1.6x relative to FullAttn. Across scaling-law training from 0.6B to 14B parameters, MALA closely tracks FullAttn in perplexity while reducing total training FLOPs. The resulting 14B models and 32B models from separate continued training achieve comparable knowledge, reasoning, and long-context retrieval scores to FullAttn. These results indicate that allocating post-score computation according to normalized attention contributions can retain the evaluated capabilities of FullAttn while reducing attention computation.

</details>


### [139] [SkillVine: Agent Skill Evolution via Branching Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32731)
*Kaiwei Liu,Jiqian Dong,Liran Dong,Shuai Mao,Mingming Zhao,Bufang Yang,Jie Chuai,Zhitang Chen,Guoliang Xing,Zhenyu Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillVine 通过将技能进化视为图搜索并引入分支探索与协同机制，显著提高了技能库演化效果，在大多数评测组合上优于线性更新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性演化方法顺序更新最新技能库，容易陷入局部最优并忽略有前景的演化路径，因此需要一种能够并行/分支探索多条演化路径的方法。

Method: 将技能演化建模为图搜索，采用分支探索策略；设计了主干-分支协同搜索机制、智能父节点选择器和自适应粒度更新规则，以在探索与利用之间取得平衡。

Result: 在5个基准、2个大模型上评测，SkillVine 在分支路径上发现了比线性主干更优的技能库版本，并在10种基准-模型组合中有9种达到了最佳测试表现。

Conclusion: SkillVine 将技能进化问题从线性更新转为图搜索与分支探索的形式，能有效避免陷入局部最优，从而生成更优的技能库版本并在多数基准和模型组合上取得更好性能。

Abstract: Agent skills encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge that enables LLM agents to perform tasks, and they can be improved automatically using trajectories from interactions with the environment. This is the classic problem of skill evolution. Existing approaches predominately follow a linear evolution paradigm, in which updates are sequentially applied to the latest skill-library version. As a result, they inevitably fall into local optima, leaving many promising evolution paths unexplored. We propose SkillVine, an automatic skill-evolution framework that formulates skill evolution as a graph search problem and employs a branching exploration strategy. Equipped with a trunk-branch collaborative searching mechanism, an intelligent parent-node selector, and an adaptive-granularity update rule, SkillVine achieves a balance between exploration and exploitation. We evaluate SkillVine on 5 benchmarks with two LLMs. Results show that SkillVine discovers better skill-library versions along branches than along the linear trunk and achieves the best test performance in nine of ten benchmark-model combinations.

</details>


### [140] [Retrospective Distillation Attribution via Normalized Response Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32749)
*Minwoo Jang,Jaechang Kim,Minhyeon Oh,Jeongyeon Hwang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOUT 是一种输出文本驱动的句法模式归因方法，可在无内部访问的情况下检测并追踪蒸馏来源，且对后续微调与 RLHF 等训练步骤具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际部署场景中，蒸馏模型通常在初次 SFT 后还会经历额外的微调、偏好优化或强化学习，从而破坏以往基于参考检查点的归因方法；审计者往往无法获得蒸馏前的检查点或内测数据。因此需要一种对已发布模型进行外部、仅基于文本的稳健蒸馏来源归因方法。

Method: SCOUT 聚合文本中的重复出现的句法模式形成候选配置文件，过滤掉低对比度（即对区分力低）的模式，并通过将学生与候选之间的距离校准为相对于候选间距离来实现判别。该方法仅使用当前生成文本（output-only），不依赖模型权重、token 概似性或先前检查点。支持归因与弃权机制。

Result: 实证上，作者在多类后训练目标（包括偏好优化与强化学习）下的公开蒸馏后代模型上进行了审计，SCOUT 始终能够识别出蒸馏来源；并通过沿训练轨迹追踪句法签名，发现这些签名在蒸馏阶段出现并在后续训练阶段持续存在。

Conclusion: 提出了 SCOUT——一种仅基于输出文本的模型蒸馏来源归因方法，能够在无权重、无标记似然、无历史检查点的前提下识别蒸馏教师模型，并可选择放弃无法确定的样本。作者通过对公开发布的蒸馏后代模型进行审计，证明了 SCOUT 能稳定识别蒸馏来源，并展示了教师相关的句法签名在蒸馏过程中出现并在后续的偏好优化与强化学习中保持存在。

Abstract: Model distillation transfers capabilities through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on teacher responses, often collected from commercial APIs, raising questions of model provenance. Existing distillation attribution methods have been largely evaluated on students immediately after the SFT step. However, a distilled model may undergo further SFT, preference optimization, or reinforcement learning before release, while an auditor may lack access to the pre-distillation checkpoint required by reference-based attribution. To close this gap, we propose SCOUT, an output-only method that aggregates recurring *syntactic patterns* into candidate profiles, filters low-contrast patterns, and calibrates student--candidate distances against inter-candidate distances. SCOUT supports attribution and abstention using only current texts, without model weights, token likelihoods, or historical checkpoints. Auditing publicly released descendants of distilled models spanning diverse post-training objectives, SCOUT consistently identifies the distillation source. Furthermore, tracing teacher-associated *syntactic signatures* along training trajectories reveals that they emerge during distillation and persist through subsequent preference optimization and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [141] [CUA-Sandbox: Efficient Environments for Computer-Use Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32750)
*Xin Yan,Zhengbo Jiao,Jiaqi Liu,Zhenglin Wan,SiYuan Ma,Xuliang Yu,Tianyi Jiang,Chubin Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Xingrui Yu,Bo An,Yang You,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把状态与运行时分离，CUA-Sandbox在保留接口与评估的一致性下，极大提升并行回放效率并降低内存与存储消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部署为每次回合复制完整初始化运行时，导致内存和初始化成本随并行环境数量线性增长；观察到轨迹需要独立的可变状态而非独立运行时，因此可复用运行时以节省资源。

Method: 提出CUA-Sandbox，采用状态作用域执行和事务性生命周期操作（包括重置和分支），将私有可变状态封装为独立胶囊、复用初始化的应用运行时，且保留原有软件接口与任务评估器。

Result: 相较于Docker，CUA-Sandbox在任务成功率上相当或更好，最高达到6.20×的回放吞吐增长、9.2×的每环境内存降低与504×的增量存储减少。

Conclusion: 本论文结论是：在基于真实软件的强化学习环境中，运行时无需为每个并行环境独立实例化；以“状态”为独立单元、将私有状态胶囊化并与共享运行时分离，可以大幅降低资源开销，同时保持或提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables computer-use agents to improve through interaction with real software environments, including websites and desktop applications. However, conventional deployments replicate an initialized runtime for each independent rollout, even when trajectories use the same software, incurring repeated memory and initialization costs as the number of parallel environments grows. Does an independent computer-use environment require an independent execution runtime? Our key observation is that trajectories require independent mutable state, while initialized application runtimes can be reused across concurrently evolving environments, making state the natural unit of environment independence. Guided by this observation, we introduce CUA-Sandbox, which separates private state capsules from shared runtimes through state-scoped execution and transactional lifecycle operations, including resets and branches, while retaining the original software interfaces and task evaluators. Experiments show comparable or improved task success relative to Docker, while substantially reducing rollout and resource costs. CUA-Sandbox achieves up to a 6.20x increase in rollout throughput, a 9.2x reduction in per-environment memory, and a 504x reduction in incremental storage.

</details>


### [142] [Action Shaping: Policies Absorb What They Can Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32752)
*Yanjun Chen,Jinghan Wang,Xiaoyu Shen,Wenjie Li,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: If a trainable action offset is in the function class expressible exactly by the policy output, the policy will absorb it and it can be dropped at deployment with minimal impact; exact reproducibility, not mere capacity, is the key condition, and the offset amplitude predicts removal cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reward shaping has a theorem guaranteeing no change to optimal policy; analogous practice on the action channel (adding offsets during training) lacked a theorem and guarantees. The work aims to state a principle and provide diagnostics for when such action offsets can be safely removed.

Method: Introduce action shaping concept; propose a minimal trainable offset module—a zero-initialized linear head with a learnable gate—added to the actor trained via a learned action-value function; analyze absorption behavior theoretically (exact reproduction) and empirically across tasks.

Result: Show that when an offset can be exactly reproduced by the policy's output layer it is absorbed during training and can be removed with negligible loss in return; demonstrate on 20 tasks for deterministic and stochastic actors; show that nonlinear or higher-capacity heads are not necessarily absorbed unless an exactly reproducible linear path exists; identify a flat loss direction and gradient-noise drift as the mechanism, and use offset amplitude as a diagnostic for removal cost.

Conclusion: Policies absorb and later allow removal of action offsets that their output layer can exactly reproduce; this gives action shaping a counterpart to potential-based reward shaping under a precise condition.

Abstract: Reward shaping has a theorem: a potential-based term can be removed without changing the optimal policy. The same practice on the action channel, an offset added in training and dropped at deployment, has no theorem. Nothing cancels an action offset, so the correction is kept at deployment or removed without a guarantee. We call it action shaping and state its principle. A trainable policy absorbs an offset its own output layer can reproduce exactly, which is what we mean by express; what is absorbed can be removed with the return intact. Its minimal instance is a zero-initialized linear head behind a learnable gate, added to an actor that trains through a learned action-value function, with no penalty or schedule. The gate rises and then falls on its own, for deterministic and stochastic actors alike, and on 20 tasks removing the head costs almost nothing. The condition is exact reproduction, not capacity: a nonlinear head with more parameters is not absorbed, and in a paired control, one linear path added to a nonlinear base head restores absorption. Exact reproduction gives the loss a flat direction that gradient noise drifts along, and the offset's amplitude indicates, before removal, what dropping the head will cost. Action shaping thus gains the counterpart of the shaping theorem, a condition for absorption, together with the mechanism behind it and a diagnostic that reads it. Policies absorb what they can express, and only that.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptive Consistency Graph for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32754)
*Jiecong Wang,Hao Peng,Zhanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACG通过增量证据图与需求中心临时视图，改善了LLM代理在长程、多工具任务中的一致性与成功率，作为对现有代理的可追溯上下文增强而非替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型代理在短期任务能做出合理局部决定，但在需要长序列依赖行动与工具调用的任务上表现下降，原因是任务需求、历史证据和当前执行状态逐渐脱节，导致后续决策偏离原始目标。

Method: 提出Adaptive Consistency Graph（ACG）：在执行过程中增量构建包含执行证据及其源头的持久图谱，并在有限上下文预算内为每次决策生成一个以任务需求为中心的临时视图，作为对基础代理的结构化上下文补充，而不是替换其规划器或执行器。

Result: 在匹配评测中，ACG使GPT-5.6-luna的平均成功率从使用ReAct时的44.5%提升到50.2%；在BrowseComp-Plus上增益最大，从62.4%提升到73.5%。同时对轨迹结构与推理成本进行了分析以刻画改进来源。

Conclusion: ACG通过为每一步决策提供基于持久化证据图的临时需求中心视图，减少了长程执行中信息与目标的脱节，从而提升了多步工具调用任务的成功率。

Abstract: Large language model agents can often make reasonable local decisions on short tasks, yet their performance degrades when success requires long sequences of dependent actions and tool calls. During execution, task requirements, historical evidence, and the current execution state may gradually become disconnected, so later decisions can drift from the original objective. We study this problem by introducing the Adaptive Consistency Graph (ACG) for long-horizon execution. ACG incrementally organizes execution evidence and its provenance in a persistent graph, then constructs a temporary requirement-centered view for each decision under a bounded context budget. Rather than replacing the base agent's planner or tool executor, ACG provides a structured and traceable context view for each decision. In the matched evaluation, ACG improves GPT-5.6-luna's average success from 44.5\% with ReAct to 50.2\%, with the largest gain on BrowseComp-Plus (73.5\% versus 62.4\%). We further analyze trajectory structure and inference cost to characterize this improvement.

</details>


### [144] [Readout is not Recovery: Dissociating Coordinate Emission from Visual-Corruption Repair in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32757)
*Drandreb Earl Juanico*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: VLM bounding-box localization is both language generation and spatial commitment. Parseable fields such as bbox_2d make localization easy to score, but dimensions that emit coordinate tokens need not repair localization after visual evidence is damaged. We study this readout/recovery separation in Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct on single-object COCO grounding. We compare clean coordinate-token readout rankings with corruption-derived repair rankings, using object-mask endpoint replacement for recovery and clean-input flooring for depth localization. In Qwen3-VL, coordinate-token rankings are inert through layer 24, load-bearing from layers 32-35, and peak at layer 34; corruption-derived rankings harm layers 16-24 but become beneficial near layer 35/final. A Kimi-VL-A3B diagnostic shows a matching output-proximal transition despite a different box format. Object-mask recovery separates rank budgets: $k=250$ shows necessity, $k=500$ shows Top-$k$ restoration above random, and $k=d/2$ is largely capacity-driven. Partial-occlusion sweeps reveal that high-overlap coordinate-token sets can hurt at $k=1000$ and help mainly at half-width, while population corruption-derived sets provide no reliable fixed repair set. Edge-attribution patching shows coordinate-token paths are high precision but low recall for detection recovery, and RMSNorm quasi-layer controls do not close the endpoint-repair gap. Endpoint coordinate triage is therefore a useful circuit prior, but occlusion recovery requires a separate benchmark.

</details>


### [145] [Mandela-Bench: Multimodal Models Remember Canonical Images Instead of Seeing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32763)
*Yicheng Bao,Zhenkun Gao,Xiahui Guo,Mingqian Yang,Xueheng Li,Bangwei Liu,Mingang Chen,Lijun Li,Xuhong Wang,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者构建了Mandela-Bench，证明当前多模态模型在基于世界知识验证视觉内容时普遍失败，表现出用记忆的“典型图像”覆盖实际观测的识别偏差，现有提示或提供事实信息并不能普遍纠正这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪造检测基准依赖生成器伪迹、图像-字幕不一致、视觉不合理性或外部引用，无法检验模型是否能用自身世界知识来基于识别的图像进行事实核验。因此需要一个专门检测知识驱动伪造（knowledge-only forgeries）的数据集与评估方法。

Method: 提出了Mandela-Bench基准：1,507幅图像编辑样本（1,359个只依赖知识的伪造、148个不引入事实矛盾的锚点无关控制，以及474幅原始图像）。对36个不同规模的多模态模型进行评估，不仅判断是否检测出伪造，还评估模型解释是否能识别被插入的实体或被违反的事实。进行了多种消融/实验（如隔离人脸测试、提供真实事件与日期、裁剪构图后再给信息、显式验证提示等）。

Result: 核心结果包括：当公众人物被从熟悉照片中移除并替换时，模型仍然在高达72.7%的回答中命名被移除者；有些模型能在孤立的人脸样本中分辨出替换者，但在完整编辑照中仍判断为真实；提供正确事件/日期不改善检测，但在裁剪掉可识别构图后再给信息会有所帮助；在显式验证提示下，仅1/36模型在至少一半伪造样本上满足KGR标准。总体表明问题不能仅用缺乏知识或感知不足解释，而更像是识别驱动的验证偏差。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：大型多模态模型在面对“仅基于世界知识”的图像篡改（即外观上无明显像素痕迹但与可验证事实冲突的编辑）时普遍失败。这种失败更像是“识别偏差”（recognition bias）：模型倾向于根据记忆中的经典图像判断其真实性，而不是基于观察到的编辑进行验证。

Abstract: Historical photographs and other canonical images can now be edited seamlessly with a single instruction, often leaving no reliable pixel-level trace. In such cases, the only evidence of manipulation may be a fact about what the image depicts. Existing benchmarks instead rely on generator artefacts, image-caption inconsistencies, visual implausibilities, or external references, and therefore do not test whether a model can use its own world knowledge to verify a recognized image. We introduce Mandela-Bench, containing 1,507 edits of canonical images: 1,359 knowledge-only forgeries, each contradicting one verifiable fact, and 148 anchor-free controls that preserve the editing process without introducing a factual contradiction, together with 474 untouched originals. We score not only whether a model detects a forgery, but whether its explanation identifies the inserted entity or the fact being violated. Across 36 multimodal models, from 0.8B parameters to frontier scale, we find a consistent failure mode. When a public figure is removed from a familiar photograph, models still name that person in up to 72.7% of responses. Some models can distinguish the replacement face from the original when shown in isolation, yet still judge the full edited photograph as authentic. Providing the true event and date does not improve knowledge-grounded detection, whereas providing the same information after cropping away the recognizable composition does. Even under explicit verification prompts, only one of the 36 models meets the KGR criterion on at least half of the forged images. These results suggest that the failures cannot be explained by missing knowledge or inadequate perception alone. Instead, they are consistent with recognition biasing verification toward the remembered canonical image rather than the observed edit.

</details>


### [146] [Forecasting Intraday USD/CAD Exchange Rate with News-Derived Monetary-Policy Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32773)
*Maya Kodeih,Aliaa Alnaggar,Mucahit Cevik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将货币政策文本转为结构化信号并在FDR受控下做归因，发现沟通时点与少数活动指标是预测外汇的重要驱动，LLM情绪为补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 货币政策公告和央行沟通在外汇市场重要但以定性、非结构化文本呈现，难以量化其预测价值；以往研究多集中于从新闻中提取情绪，而较少分析不同沟通维度各自的贡献，因此需要一种可解释且受控的归因方法。

Method: 将货币政策新闻用大语言模型（LLM）和时间特征工程转化为结构化通信信号；使用滚动窗口实验结合基于树的机器学习模型进行预测评估；并引入在假发现率（FDR）控制下的统计归因方法，分解并检验各通信通道的增量预测贡献。

Result: 实证结果表明：货币政策沟通带有可预测信息；预测价值主要由少数信号驱动，沟通时点是最强的单一特征贡献者，定向沟通活动指标也显著正向，LLM提取的情绪在组层面补充信息；整体上，沟通的预测价值超出纯情绪指标。

Conclusion: 货币政策沟通确实包含可测的预测信息，但这种价值集中于少数可解释的沟通通道——沟通时点贡献最大，定向的沟通活动指标也有正向作用，而大语言模型提取的情绪在组级别提供补充信息；因此应以归因分析而非仅以总体准确率来评估新闻衍生信号。

Abstract: Monetary-policy announcements and central-bank communications play a central role in foreign exchange markets, yet their qualitative, unstructured form makes their forecasting value difficult to quantify. While prior research has largely focused on sentiment extracted from financial news, comparatively little is known about the relative contribution of different dimensions of monetary-policy communication. Existing studies primarily evaluate whether textual information improves overall forecasting performance but provide limited insight into which communication channels drive such improvements. To address this gap, this paper introduces a statistical attribution methodology that decomposes monetary-policy communication into interpretable channels and quantifies their incremental forecasting contribution under false-discovery-rate control. Monetary-policy news is transformed into structured communication signals using large language models (LLMs) and temporal feature engineering. These signals are evaluated using rolling-window experiments with tree-based machine-learning models. The results show that monetary-policy communication contains measurable predictive information. Attribution analysis shows that predictive value is concentrated in a small subset of signals, with communication timing providing the strongest individual feature-level contribution, targeted communication-activity measures also contributing positively, and LLM-derived sentiment providing complementary information at the group level. The findings indicate that communication-based forecasting value extends beyond sentiment alone and that attribution, rather than aggregate accuracy alone, is central to evaluating news-derived signals.

</details>


### [147] [Agentic Network Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32778)
*Manuel Tsoukatos,Hayden Jananthan,Jeremy Kepner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将代理通信编码为复值超稀疏矩阵，结合数据库与 GraphBLAS 做高效存储与分析，模拟器实验表明该方法可用于代理行为监控与审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具代理性人工智能普及，攻击面扩大，需要可审计的、与用户意图对齐的监控机制；网络流量提供清晰的代理交互记录，但需高效表示与分析手段。

Method: 构建代理模拟器生成通信事件，使用 DBOS 和 OneSparse PostgreSQL 存储元数据，将通信映射为复值超稀疏矩阵，并用 GraphBLAS 进行矩阵级别的分析与查询以检测代理行为模式。

Result: 通过模拟实验生成的网络流量矩阵能够捕捉不同策略下代理的通信特征，证明该表示便于监控与分析；系统集成展示了可行性与初步效果。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一种基于复值超稀疏流量矩阵的代理网络监控方法，结合 DBOS、OneSparse PostgreSQL 和 GraphBLAS，可将代理间交互以稀疏矩阵形式高效编码并用于监控和审计。

Abstract: As the use of agentic artificial intelligence increases in nearly every industry, there exists a widening attack surface. It is necessary to monitor agents to ensure that agents are acting in a way that is aligned with the users intent. Auditing an agent's network traffic provides a clear record of the agent interactions. This work presents a novel approach to monitoring the network traffic of agentic systems using complex valued hypersparse traffic matrices by integrating DBOS (DataBase OS), the OneSparse PostgreSQL database, and the GraphBLAS math library. To develop these concepts an agentic simulator was constructed, allowing a varying numbers of AI agents to collectively survey a virtual environment using different strategies. The resulting network traffic matrices enable easy monitoring of the AI agents.

</details>


### [148] [Learning response-aware patient dynamics for respiratory support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32782)
*Xiaolei Lu,Shamim Nemati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将生理变化分解为基线动态和呼吸支持相关偏差，并以室内空气为参考锚定，构建响应感知的自回归患者动力学模型，在状态变化剧烈时能更稳定地改善预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重症患者接受相同呼吸支持但可能出现不同的生理轨迹，传统模型仅通过未来状态来间接表示生理变化，缺乏对呼吸支持响应的显式刻画，导致在响应动态显著时预测不稳定或不够精细。

Method: 提出一种响应感知（response-aware）患者动力学模型：在自回归隐状态更新中显式表示生理变化，将预测的生理变化分解为与当前状态相关的基线动力学和与呼吸支持相关的偏差；以室内空气（room air）作为参考基准；通过一个响应通路（response pathway）编码预测的生理变化并在预测期内用于更新潜在患者状态；并对该参考锚定（reference-anchored）形式化地进行分析。

Result: 在来自两个独立机构的ICU队列上，所提模型在总体轨迹预测上与现有患者动力学基线方法相当，但在生理状态发生变化时实现了更一致的改进。

Conclusion: Explicitly modeling respiratory-support-induced physiological changes via a reference-anchored decomposition improves predictive consistency during periods of physiological change, while matching baseline methods overall.

Abstract: Respiratory support can shape the short-term physiological trajectory of critically ill patients, but patients receiving the same intervention may follow different physiological trajectories. Clinical patient dynamics models typically predict future states from recent physiology and recorded interventions, while physiological change is mainly represented through the predicted future state. We propose a response-aware patient dynamics model that explicitly represents physiological change during autoregressive state updating. The model decomposes predicted physiological change into state-dependent baseline dynamics and respiratory-support-associated deviations, with room air providing a reference for the decomposition. We provide a formal analysis of this reference-anchored formulation. A response pathway encodes the predicted physiological change and uses it to update the latent patient state across the forecast horizon. Across ICU cohorts from two independent institutions, the proposed model achieves comparable overall trajectory prediction to patient dynamics baselines, with more consistent improvements when physiological states are changing.

</details>


### [149] [CLAIRE: A Schema-Grounded Hybrid Workflow for Healthcare Administrative Form Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32787)
*Garapati Keerthana,Manik Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLAIRE在受控合成测试下表现优秀，表明以schema为中心、先验证再授权的混合自动化能在医疗行政填表场景中安全且高效地利用大模型作为映射辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗行政人员需把来自EHR、转诊、理赔、名册和工作队列的结构化信息填入动态表单。既要自动化提高效率，又要保证准确性、依从性和可审计性，因此需要一种既利用语言模型进行映射辅助、又在关键点由模式与验证把控的系统。

Method: 提出了CLAIRE，一个把字段状态发现、源到字段映射、确定性验证、有界修正、升级（人工介入）和审计追踪分离的混合工作流。用5个合成行政schema、1,000条源记录、4种界面变体、2套数据质量设定和6个比较器进行基准测试，总计24,000次基准回合；另有对Qwen2.5（1.5B与7B）直接映射的严格输出审计和基于轨迹的6,000次情景模拟。

Result: 在合成基准中，完整CLAIRE在两套数据质量条件下均实现100%回合成功、字段准确率、必填字段完成与依赖完成；去除验证组件后，在“压力”数据集成功率降至50%。情景模拟显示，100% 的“干净”和“验证压力”情形到达可人工复核草稿，升级挑战情形为68.6%，不受支持情形被阻止；估计节省时间约149.7–165.5秒/案。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：在受控的合成基准条件下，采用以模式（schema）为基础并优先验证的混合工作流（CLAIRE），在辅助映射但不授权关键动作的前提下，可以实现几乎完美的字段准确率、必填项完成率和依赖项完成率，并在情景模拟中将多数案件推进到可供人工复核的草稿状态，同时带来每案约150–165秒的节省（基于情景估计）

Abstract: Healthcare administrative staff transfer structured information from electronic health records, referrals, claims systems, provider rosters, and work queues into dynamic forms. We developed and evaluated CLAIRE (Clinical Language and Agentic Intelligence for Reasoning and Entry), a hybrid workflow that separates field-state discovery, source-to-field mapping, deterministic validation, bounded correction, escalation, and audit tracing. We tested five synthetic healthcare administrative schemas, 1,000 source records, four interface variants, two data-quality suites, and six comparators, yielding 24,000 benchmark episodes. A separate strict-output audit evaluated direct mappings from Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B, and a trace-derived operational simulation covered 6,000 episodes. Under the evaluated synthetic benchmark conditions, full CLAIRE achieved 1.000 episode success, field accuracy, required-field completion, and dependency completion in both suites; removing validation reduced stress-suite success to 0.500. In the simulation, 100.0% of clean and validation-stress episodes reached a staff-reviewable draft, compared with 68.6% of escalation challenge episodes, unsupported cases were blocked. Scenario-based savings were 149.7-165.5 seconds per case, not observed staff times. The findings support schema-grounded, validation-first healthcare administrative automation in which language-model components assist mapping but do not authorize unsupported or consequential actions.

</details>


### [150] [$T^5$: Twin-Critic Training for Token-Level Thoughts in Reinforcement Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32791)
*Nan Qiao,Yebin Yang,Weinong Wang,Shuning Wang,Shangpin Peng,Fengyuan Lu,Xinming Wang,Zhehan Kan,Ruixu Zhang,Songyang Zhang,Sheng Yue,Yonglong Tian,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 	four{} uses a twin-critic design with learned, action-dependent mixing to calibrate token-level advantages from single rollouts, correcting bias from training/inference mismatch and PPO clipping, with theoretical guarantees and empirical gains in both performance and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RL-based mid-training for language models needs efficient token-level credit assignment. Group-relative methods require costly repeated generation; single-rollout learned critics avoid that but suffer from offset biases caused by training--inference mismatch and PPO clipping, producing update drift.

Method: Train two critics after warmup and held-out qualification; for each prefix, produce two advantage estimates. Learn action-dependent mixing weights via a conditional-moment saddle-point objective that enforces the average advantage at each prefix to approach zero while preserving signal through a signal-retention constraint. Share information across positions to avoid repeated sampling of prefixes.

Result: Theoretically characterize optimal mixing under the signal-retention constraint and bound residual mean-induced drift. Empirically, 	four{} improves mean benchmark performance by 7.8% over state-of-the-art critic-free method and reduces mean training-step time by up to 63.4%.

Conclusion: Twin-critic method (	four{}) calibrates token-level advantages from a single generated trajectory to correct mean-induced bias and reduce update drift, yielding better performance and efficiency than critic-free baselines.

Abstract: Reinforcement mid-training lets language models learn internal thoughts from unlabeled text, but efficient token-level credit assignment remains challenging. Existing group-relative methods require costly repeated generation. Learned critics offer single-rollout feedback, but accurate return prediction alone does not ensure reliable policy updates. Our analysis shows how training--inference mismatch and PPO clipping prevent a common offset in advantage estimates from cancelling out, introducing additional update drift. We propose \tfour{}, a twin-critic method that calibrates token-level advantages from a single generated trajectory. After warmup and held-out qualification, the critics provide two advantage estimates, combined using action-dependent weights learned through a conditional-moment saddle-point objective. This objective brings the average advantage at each prefix toward zero, while a signal-retention constraint prevents the correction from erasing the learning signal. Sharing information across text positions avoids repeated sampling of each prefix. Theoretically, we characterize optimal mixing under the signal-retention constraint and establish an upper bound on residual mean-induced drift. Experiments show that, compared with the state-of-the-art critic-free method, \tfour{} improves mean benchmark performance by 7.8\% and reduces mean training-step time by up to 63.4\%.

</details>


### [151] [AgentHabit: Characterizing Distinct Behaviors of Agents on Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32795)
*Woojung Song,Hoyeol Yang,Jeonghoon Shim,Sungjib Lim,Jonggeun Lee,Yunho Choi,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出23维行为分类（HABIT）和基于轨迹的画像基准（AgentHABIT），揭示不同 LLM 代理在执行任务时的稳定行为倾向，并验证这些倾向具有跨任务可迁移性且难以完全通过提示/微调改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注任务成功或价值观/动作分类，但忽略代理在完成任务过程中的行为方式如何符合用户偏好；需要一套可测量、区分不同模型行为倾向的细粒度框架，以便选择或改进更符合用户需求的代理。

Method: 作者自下而上从408条跨17个领域的代理轨迹中归纳出23个行为轴（五大类），形成 HABIT 分类法；基于该分类法构建 AgentHABIT 基准，对86个日常任务上的轨迹进行分析，进而对18个模型进行画像并评估可迁移性和可调控性（通过提示和微调实验）。

Result: HABIT 在区分模型行为上优于现有的人类价值和代理动作分类，同时保持注释一致性；AgentHABIT 在86个任务上描绘出18个模型的可区分画像，这些画像具有跨任务集合的可迁移性；提示能改变部分行为轴但对某些轴影响有限，微调亦只能部分改变模型画像。

Conclusion: HABIT 和 AgentHABIT 提供了一套系统化的框架，用以刻画 LLM 代理在执行日常任务时的行为倾向，超越了仅以任务成功衡量性能的视角，能揭示模型在沟通、探查信息、处理不确定性等方面的稳定偏好。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents assist users with everyday tasks that can be completed in many reasonable ways. Even when their answers are useful, how agents carry out these tasks may not match users' preferences and needs. For example, agents differ in whether they ask clarifying questions or search the web. We introduce HABIT, a taxonomy of 23 behavioral axes in five categories, which three authors and three LLMs derive bottom-up from 408 agent trajectories across 17 domains. On held-out tasks, HABIT distinguishes models more clearly than existing taxonomies of human values and agent actions while supporting comparably consistent annotation. Building on HABIT, we construct AgentHABIT, a benchmark that profiles each agent's behavioral tendencies from its trajectories on 86 everyday tasks. Profiling 18 models with AgentHABIT reveals a range of distinctive tendencies. For example, most GPT and Claude models state their assumptions and offer alternatives when requirements conflict, whereas Qwen and Google's models more often leave assumptions or changes to requirements unstated. These profiles remain recognizable even when built from entirely different sets of tasks, indicating that they reflect general tendencies rather than task-specific behavior. Prompting agents to adopt specific behaviors shifts some axes readily but barely changes others, while fine-tuning on another model's trajectories changes only part of a model's profile and leaves much of it intact. Overall, HABIT and AgentHABIT provide a systematic framework for characterizing how agents carry out everyday tasks beyond task success, offering insights to guide the development of agents whose behavior better fits users' needs.

</details>


### [152] [PlanGuard: A Guardrail for Multi-Step Plan Safety in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32801)
*Junchi Chen,Changtao Miao,Yuxiao Xiang,Zhenchao Jin,Haojie Yuan,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,He Liu,Bo Zhang,Jiansheng Cai,Zhe Li,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在执行前评估完整多步计划物理安全性的检测器PlanGuard，构建MSP-Safe数据集，并通过STAC-OPD方法提升紧凑模型对强教师的自适应蒸馏，最终在2B模型上取得≈87% ACC/F1的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全保护忽视多步计划的组合性物理风险，实体任务规划器在执行多步计划时可能产生因子任务依赖与环境交互而导致的物理危险，需在执行前检测整计划的物理安全性。

Method: 构建MSP-Safe数据集（成对任务构造、多样规划器生成计划、三名评审注释安全性）；基于该数据集进行任务导向的SFT以建立基线能力；提出STAC-OPD方法，在on-policy蒸馏中结合强教师的token级分布传递与概率路由的序列级补偿，使紧凑模型在训练轨迹上获得自适应的强教师监督，同时保留学生模型偏向的参考决策以降低过拟合。

Result: 在所有测试子集中，PlanGuard-2B达成平均87.15%准确率和87.21% F1，证明了在紧凑模型规模下有效实现整计划物理风险检测；代码和数据集将公开发布。

Conclusion: 本文提出PlanGuard，一个在执行前评估完整多步计划物理安全性的检测器，能够识别子任务间的组合性风险，从而弥补现有方法只关注语义伤害或单步评估的不足。

Abstract: Embodied task planners may produce multi-step plans whose subtask dependencies and interactions with the environment create physical risks during execution. Yet existing safeguards overlook such compositional risks, as general-purpose guardrails focus on semantic harm and embodied safety detectors assess subtasks in isolation. To address this gap, we introduce PlanGuard, the first pre-execution detector that evaluates the physical safety of a complete multi-step plan in its current environment. For training and evaluation, we construct a Multi-Step Plan Safety (MSP-Safe) dataset through paired task construction, plan generation using diverse planners, and safety annotation by three judges. Task-oriented SFT on MSP-Safe establishes fundamental plan-safety assessment capabilities, yet a substantial gap remains between compact models suitable for real-time deployment and stronger but costlier large models. Accordingly, we propose Strong-Teacher Adaptive Compensation for On-Policy Distillation (STAC-OPD), which provides compact models with adaptive strong-teacher supervision along their on-policy trajectories. It combines token-level distribution transfer from a fine-tuned strong teacher with probability-routed sequence-level compensation, retaining student-generated targets when the student favors the reference safety decision and using teacher-reconstructed targets otherwise. Across all test subsets, PlanGuard-2B achieves average 87.15% ACC and 87.21% F1, demonstrating effective whole-plan physical-risk detection at compact model scale. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [153] [Re-derivability Decides What a Staged Agent Pipeline Recovers After an Upstream Fault](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32802)
*Tianqi Bu,YuXuan Peng,Junteng Tu,Henghui Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 重导性决定故障代价：对检验器做问题grounding和只对首个下游阶段重新暴露原问题是廉价且最有用的修复；多阶段分解与额外重接入收益有限；不同骨干对故障/修复反应有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化上游故障通过多阶段LM代理流水线传播的成本，并研究“重导性”与“grounding”作为修复策略的有效性及其计算/令牌成本权衡，以指导流水线设计与容错策略。

Method: 在多种开源骨干模型（包括多个Qwen、Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4等）上构建分段（多阶段）代理流水线：在第一阶段注入确定性故障；对下游k=0..3个阶段按固定代理、项、故障和拓扑重新暴露原问题；比较“grounded inspector”（以原问题为依据）与“blind inspector”的表现；在温度0和120个gsm_hard样本/臂条件下测量准确率和保留率(retention)；进行注册（registered）与探索性分析，包括“registered kill test”（规则化排除特定tokens）和“blank every word”以测量可见分数；使用Holm校正处理多重检验并报告置信区间与p值。

Result: grounding检验器在四个开源骨干上将性能提高在区间+0.608[+0.517,+0.700]到+0.358；对两款Qwen骨干，blind检验器无改动；在注入单一确定性故障后，向下游重新暴露原问题使四个骨干上故障情形下的准确率上升+0.233至+0.392（最大Holm校正p=2.1×10^{-6}）；blanking实验显示主要骨干的可见保留率从0.221升至0.692；交互效应在不同流水线设置与骨干间表现不一；Llama-3.1-8B对故障几乎无感知；无故障时，注册流水线在多数情况下不如一次直接调用；首个被重新ground的下游阶段能以约+0.394的保留收益换取在Qwen3-14B上每项约+59.8个令牌的额外开销，后续阶段收益接近零。

Conclusion: 重导性（re-derivability）决定上游故障在分段语言模型代理流水线中的代价：若下游阶段能从原问题重建所需信息，则故障损失小；将检验器（inspector）以原问题为依据进行grounding能显著提高在故障情形下的性能；最有效且廉价的修复是对首个下游阶段重新ground（front-loaded），后续阶段边际收益微乎其微；多段分解调用并不可靠地优于一次直接调用；不同模型骨干存在显著异质性（例如Llama-3.1-8B对注入故障不敏感）。

Abstract: One variable sets what an upstream fault costs a staged pipeline of language-model agents: re-derivability, how much of what a stage needs it can rebuild from the original problem. Grounding an inspector agent in that problem is worth +0.608 [+0.517, +0.700] to +0.358 over a blind one on four open-weight backbones served with thinking disabled, and on the two Qwen backbones the blind inspector changes no item at all. That head-to-head is exploratory. One deterministic fault enters the first stage, and we re-expose the original problem to $k = 0,\dots,3$ of the downstream stages with agents, items, fault and topology held fixed, on 120 gsm_hard items per arm at temperature zero. Accuracy under fault rises on four of four backbones, from +0.233 to +0.392, the largest Holm-adjusted $p$ being $2.1\times10^{-6}$. A registered kill test rules out tokens. Blanking every word holds the word slots fixed, and retention tracks the visible fraction on four of four, climbing from 0.221 to 0.692 on the primary. Those two families are confirmatory and everything else here is exploratory. The interaction excludes zero on two of four backbones under the registered pipeline, four of four under a three-stage pipeline, and three of four under full message history, the primary at +0.317. On Llama-3.1-8B the fault carries no detectable cost at any dose, so the other three carry every claim about what a fault costs. Re-derivability also sets what the architecture costs, and no decomposition we measured reliably beats one direct call. With no fault injected the registered pipeline loses to that call by -0.267, -0.125 and -0.317, and on Phi-4 reads +0.058 at $p = 0.118$, which the test fails to separate from zero. The repair that works is cheap and front-loaded: the first re-grounded stage buys +0.394 of matched retention for +59.8 tokens per item on Qwen3-14B, and the stages after it buy nothing.

</details>


### [154] [Nutri-ATLAS: Embodied Agent for Tabulated Lookup and Assistance for Smarter nutrition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32803)
*Uttej Kallakuri,Boxun Hu,Ankur A. Butala,Najim Dehak,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将图谱嵌入、混合检索与机器人感知结合的营养智能体 Nutri-ATLAS，在多项检索与实体化执行任务上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 LLM 的营养系统多局限于被动文本交互与静态上下文，在食品描述模糊或缺少营养证据时不可靠；需要将感知、知识与自治交互整合以支持现实世界的个性化营养决策。

Method: 从 USDA FoodData Central 与 FoodKG 构建统一 Food–Nutrient 知识图谱，训练 64 维 GATv2 食物与菜谱嵌入；采用图-文本混合评分检索机制用于营养提取、缺失值填补、替代品检索与菜谱组装；用硬件感知的 LLM 选择和基于技能的 LLM 接口控制机器人导航、更新饮食与可得性记忆并根据观测证据落地建议。

Result: 在多项基准与真实执行上表现良好：HealthyFoodSubs（MAP 37.9%、RR@5 80.7%、RR@10 90.1%）；NutriBench v2 展示 Dense+GAT 在 Qwen3.5-9B 多配置下量化营养估计效果；PFoodReQ（MAP 78.8%、MAR 83.0%、F1 77.5%）；并在患者配置实验中保持对过敏与健康目标约束的遵守。

Conclusion: Nutri-ATLAS 是一个将图谱驱动营养推理与实体化感知和机器人证据采集相结合的营养辅助智能体，能在真实环境中基于食品可得性和个体饮食背景做出更可靠的营养建议。

Abstract: Generative and Agentic IoT systems offer a promising foundation for digital healthcare applications that combine sensing, personalized reasoning, and autonomous interaction in real-world environments. Nutrition assistance is a natural use case, but existing Large Language Model (LLM)-based systems are often limited to passive text interaction and static context, making them unreliable when food descriptions are ambiguous or nutritional evidence is missing. We propose Nutri-ATLAS, an Embodied Agent for Tabulated Lookup and Assistance for smarter nutrition in the real world. It integrates graph-grounded nutrition reasoning, hardware-aware LLM selection, and robot-based evidence acquisition. Nutri-ATLAS builds a unified Food-Nutrient knowledge graph from USDA FoodData Central and FoodKG and learns 64-dimensional GATv2 food and recipe embeddings. A shared hybrid graph-text scoring mechanism supports food nutrition extraction, nutritional gap filling, substitute retrieval, and recipe-level meal composition, while an LLM-guided skill interface navigates landmarks, updates dietary-context and food-accessibility memory, and grounds recommendations in observed food availability. We evaluate Nutri-ATLAS across nutrient estimation, substitution retrieval, recipe recommendation, patient-profile adherence, edge deployment, and real-world embodied execution. On HealthyFoodSubs, the hybrid retriever achieves 37.9% MAP, 80.7% RR@5, and 90.1% RR@10. On NutriBench v2, Dense+GAT retrieval grounds nutrient estimation across nine quantized Qwen3.5-9B configurations. On PFoodReQ, Nutri-ATLAS reaches 78.8% MAP, 83.0% MAR, and 77.5% F1. A patient-profile study shows adherence to allergy and healthy-target constraints for all selected cases.

</details>


### [155] [Decision-Sufficient State Representations: Measuring and Reducing Write-Time Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32805)
*Bingyu Shen,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者将读者损失分为容量不可避免的预算损失和写入器造成的后悔，提出DSSR通过前向评分训练写入器能在短延迟下显著降低写入时后悔，但随着信息被延迟使用，信用分配问题限制了收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长任务产生的历史超过LLM上下文容量且即便历史能放下也并非总被可靠利用，因而用短写入状态替代长历史成为研究热点。关键问题是：写入器丢弃信息带来的损失能否通过训练降低？以及如何衡量和指导写入器做出更有决策充足性的选择？

Method: 将读者的损失相对于相同大小的最佳后验状态分解为“预算损失”（任何同容量状态都不可避免的损失）和“写入时后悔”（writer的选择引起的额外损失）。在TextWorld烹饪游戏中控制事实需要被保持的延迟，比较基线（prompted LM writer）与训练后的写入器（DSSR）。DSSR通过对候选状态进行“前向展开评分”：模拟读者在携带该状态后的行动表现，作为写入器训练信号。评估包含成功率、与游戏结果的相关性ρ以及在预注册测试集上的增益。

Result: 128-token的完备状态在测试中几乎能赢得所有游戏；基于prompt的语言模型写入器最高只赢17%。大部分差距来自写入时后悔，并随着事实被延迟使用的时间增长而增大。DSSR所用的前向评分能较好预测最终游戏结果（ρ=0.48），而传统的基于固定上下文/后验评分方法预测力很差（ρ≤0.07）。在一次性开放的预注册测试集上，训练为近处事实带来+7.0（置信区间[+1.9,+12.2]）个百分点的成功率提升，使普通摘要写入器达到基于belief/slot记忆提示的水平；但随着延迟增长，该增益迅速缩小并仅在最短延迟处显著。瓶颈在信用分配：保持某条事实只有在后续每次重写都继续保持时才有回报，而逐步评分无法看到这种长期连贯性。

Conclusion: 在长时序任务中，大部分性能损失来源于写入时的后悔（writer choices），而不是状态容量本身；通过让写入器根据读入器的后续行为打分（DSSR），可以在短延迟情形下显著减少这类后悔，但随着延迟增长，信用分配困难限制了改进效果。

Abstract: Long tasks produce more history than an LLM agent can hold in its context, and more than it uses reliably even when the history fits. A growing line of work therefore has agents carry a short written state instead: at every step a writer rewrites the state, and a reader acts from the state alone. Steps stay cheap, but anything the writer drops is lost before later decisions reveal that they need it. We quantify this loss and ask whether training can reduce it. Comparing the written state with the best state of the same size written in hindsight, we split the reader's loss into a budget loss, which any state of that size must incur, and a write-time regret, which comes from the writer's choices. In TextWorld cooking games where we control how long a fact must be carried before it is needed, a 128-token state holding the facts wins nearly every game, while prompted language-model writers win at most 17%. Almost all of the loss is write-time regret, and it grows with the delay. We then train the writer from the reader's own loss. DSSR (decision-sufficient state representations) scores candidate states by how well the reader acts after the writer carries them forward, and teaches the writer to prefer the better ones. This forward-rolled score predicts game outcomes ($ρ= 0.48$), whereas scoring a candidate as a fixed context, as hindsight methods usually do, does not ($ρ\leq 0.07$). On a pre-registered test split opened once, training adds +7.0 [+1.9, +12.2] points of success when facts are needed soon, bringing a plain summary writer to the level of belief- and slot-based memory prompts. The gain shrinks as the delay grows and is significant only at the shortest delay. We trace this limit to credit assignment: keeping a fact now pays off only if every later rewrite keeps it too, which a per-step score cannot see.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Accuracy: Counterfactual Fragility and Demographic Bias in Clinical Evaluation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32807)
*Chaitai Deb Purkayastha,Bharath Kumar Bolla,Vishnu Surya Reddy Nandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 准确率不能替代反事实一致性和人口学鲁棒性检测；自动化CFV与人口学扰动测试能作为安全筛查但不能单凭其结果判定临床可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯衡量答案准确率无法暴露模型在面对轻微临床或人口学变动时的判定一致性与偏差风险，需开发自动化筛查方法以早期发现安全隐患。

Method: 在150道MedQA（USMLE风格）题目上对6个LLM做两种自动化扰动测试：1) 反事实有效性（CFV）测试——要求模型提出最小且合理的临床改变以使不同选项正确；2) 人口学鲁棒性测试——对同一病情加6种人口学前缀并与无人口学基线比较答案与解释。所有评判均为自动化。
