Result: 在900次CFV尝试中仅有228（25.3%）被判为有效；在5,400次人口学比较中有1,097次答案变化（20.3%）。自动化判读发现3,128个刻板证据标记（其中1,932属广义Other）。模型间差异显著：MedGemma 27B在准确率（87.1%）、CFV（63.3%）、最低答案变化率（16.0%）和较低EDD（0.169）上表现最好，但其准确率仍高于CFV。OpenBioLLM在答案变化率和EDD上最高；GLM的刻板证据标记最多。

Conclusion: 准确率不足以评估临床LLM的可靠性；需多维度指标（CFV、答案稳定性、EDD、刻板证据标记）来揭示模型在反事实一致性与人口学鲁棒性上的弱点。

Abstract: Clinical LLM evaluation often emphasizes answer accuracy; however, accuracy alone does not test counterfactual consistency or demographic robustness. We evaluated six LLMs on 150 MedQA USMLE questions using two automated perturbation tests to assess their performance. The counterfactual validity (CFV) test asked each model to make a minimal, plausible clinical change that would make a different answer correct. The demographic robustness test added six demographic prefixes to the same vignette and compared the answers and explanations with a no demographic baseline. Of the 900 CFV attempts, 228 (25.3 %) were valid and 672 were invalid. Across 5,400 demographic comparisons, 1,097 answers were changed (20.3%). Automated judging identified 3,128 stereotype evidence flags, including 1,932 in the broad Other category. MedGemma 27B achieved the highest accuracy (87.1%) and CFV (63.3%), lowest answer change rate (16.0%), and low mean Explanation Demographic Dissonance (EDD) score (0.169). However, its accuracy still exceeded its CFV, indicating that correct answers do not guarantee reliable performance on the counterfactual validity task. OpenBioLLM had the highest answer change rate and EDD, whereas GLM had the highest stereotype flag rate. These findings show that accuracy, CFV, answer stability, EDD, and stereotype evidence capture different evaluation aspects. Because all judgments were automated and no clinician validation was available, the results support safety screening but do not establish clinical deployability of the model.

</details>


### [157] [Overwhelmed by Choice: Studying LLM Decision Making at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32809)
*Yu-Chi Lin,Aryan Seth,Anshul Aravind,Eugene Lee,Tanmay Parekh,Nanyun Peng,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Scaling the number of candidates reveals systematic failure modes (weaker correct-answer confidence and early-choice stickiness); simple inference strategies can partially recover performance, implying benchmark protocol must report candidate-set scale and robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Most existing multiple-choice/candidate-selection benchmarks use small candidate sets; unclear if LLM conclusions generalize when candidate space scales up.

Method: Systematic scaling experiments across tasks, prompts, and model sizes; controlled analyses to rule out long-context retrieval explanations; identification of two failure patterns; evaluation of hierarchical partitioning and permutation-based inference as mitigation strategies.

Result: Observed substantial accuracy drops with increasing N, discovery of ‘gold-margin collapse’ and ordering persistence, and ~20 percentage-point accuracy gains at N=160 on HotpotQA and MIMIC using hierarchical partitioning and permutation-based inference.

Conclusion: As candidate set size increases, LLM accuracy degrades substantially; strong performance on small-option benchmarks does not guarantee robustness at large N.

Abstract: Multiple-choice and candidate-selection evaluations are widely used to assess LLM reasoning and decision-making, yet most benchmarks contain relatively small candidate sets. It remains unclear whether conclusions drawn from these settings remain valid as the candidate space scales. We systematically evaluate LLMs as the number of competing candidates increases and find substantial accuracy degradation across tasks, prompting strategies, and model scales. Controlled analyses show that standard long-context retrieval explanations cannot fully account for this degradation. Instead, we identify two systematic failure patterns. First, gold-margin collapse: the score gap between the correct answer and the strongest distractor progressively shrinks, driven primarily by weakening confidence in the correct answer. Second, earlier candidate preferences become increasingly difficult to overturn, with later candidates exerting progressively weaker influence on the final prediction. Motivated by these findings, we evaluate hierarchical partitioning and permutation-based inference, which improve accuracy by roughly 20 percentage points at $N=160$ on both HotpotQA and MIMIC. Overall, our results identify candidate-set scale as an important evaluation-protocol variable and show that strong small-option performance does not necessarily imply robust large-scale candidate comparison.

</details>


### [158] [Right Answer, Wrong Reason: Accuracy, Consistency, and Consensus Are Misleading Indicators of LLM Faithfulness in Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32817)
*Bharath Kumar Bolla,Bharath Kumar Bolla,Vishnu Surya Reddy Nandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 准确率高并不等于解释可信：通过ESI、CFS、PSS三项行为指标发现临床LLM常引用非必要概念，准确性/一致性/共识不足以保证解释的因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对临床LLM的评估主要关注答案正确性，但缺乏对模型“为何做出该决策”这一因果/忠实性的量化评估；需要轻量、可直接解释的指标来评估解释与预测之间的信任差距。

Method: 提出并实测三种可解释性/信度行为度量：解释稳定性指数(ESI)、因果信度分数(CFS，基于概念消融进行反事实检验)与扰动稳定性分数(PSS，基于语义等价改写)。在150道MedQA-USMLE题目上对6个LLM进行了实验，共900个模型-题目观察。

Result: 仅23.3%的被引用临床概念在因果上是必要的。正确答案的CFS (0.212) 低于错误答案 (0.398)。答案一致性与CFS负相关（Spearman r = -0.466）。模型对答案达成一致时，所共享的推理概念仅为8.8%。这些证据为行为层面的因果敏感性测试，而非内部机制解析。

Conclusion: 临床大语言模型在医学考试准确率高并不意味着其解释所指示的临床概念真实驱动了预测，准确率、答案一致性与共识都不足以作为可靠的安全证明。

Abstract: Clinical Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong medical-exam accuracy; however, a correct answer does not guarantee that the explanation names the concepts that actually drove the decision. We introduce three lightweight, directly interpretable metrics for this faithfulness gap: the Explanation Stability Index (ESI), which measures reasoning consistency across repeated queries; the Causal Faithfulness Score (CFS), which tests whether cited concepts drive predictions via concept ablation; and the Perturbation Stability Score (PSS), which measures robustness to semantic-preserving paraphrases. By evaluating six LLMs on 150 MedQA-USMLE questions (900 model-question observations), we found that only 23.3% of the cited clinical concepts were causally necessary. Correct answers had lower CFS than incorrect answers (0.212 vs. 0.398), answer consistency negatively predicted CFS (Spearman r = -0.466), and model pairs could agree on answers while sharing only 8.8% of cited reasoning concepts. These results show that accuracy, consistency, and consensus are incomplete safety signals for clinical decision-making support. The evidence is behavioral rather than mechanistic: concept ablation tests counterfactual sensitivity of outputs, not internal circuits.

</details>


### [159] [When Can First-Order Models of Fine-Tuning Bound Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32818)
*Jianchang Su,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过有限差分估计的一阶响应模型可高度精确地预测margin变化；将线性替代margin与Freedman/Azuma首通界相结合，可在响应系数变化量R小于margin到零边界距离a时，为遗忘概率提供有效证书；当R>=a时，一阶模型会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调可能使模型忘记应保留的事实。研究是否能在微调开始时通过测量对每个受保护事实的遗忘概率进行界定，以便在训练中提供置信度或防护措施。

Method: 在0.6B到14B参数的语言模型上用LoRA与SGD进行微调；用有限差分探针估计一阶响应系数以构建线性（第一阶）响应模型，预测每个事实的margin变化，并导出基于线性替代margin的Freedman和Azuma首通（first-passage）界；引入R量度响应系数随训练的变化，并通过112个及后续43个预注册条件的实验证明和检验这些界的有效性。

Result: 一阶响应模型对per-fact margin变化的预测与实测相关系数为0.974–0.998；但对遗忘的直接预测失败，因为遗忘涉及参数远离验证区域的变化。简化Freedman界（R=0）在112个条件中大多成立，但在14个条件违反，且所有被违反的事实都有R>=a；完整Freedman界仅认证R<a的事实并在所有条件下成立。被违反事实的margin跨运行方差中位数约为模型预测的14.6倍。两项预注册验证（43个新条件）表明：完整界在所有条件下成立，简化界仅在3个事实上失败，均满足R>=a。

Conclusion: 一阶响应模型可在一定条件下为微调导致的遗忘给出概率性界定：当响应系数在训练过程中变化量R小于该事实边界距离a时，线性替代模型的Freedman界（完全形式）在所有测试条件下成立；当R>=a时，一阶模型会失效，导致简化界（假设R=0）被违反。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on new data can make it forget facts that it should keep. We ask whether measurements taken at the start of a fine-tuning run can bound, for each protected fact, the probability that the run makes the model forget it. In LoRA fine-tuning with stochastic gradient descent on models from 0.6B to 14B parameters, a first-order response model estimated by finite-difference probes predicts changes of per-fact margins with correlation 0.974-0.998. Predictions of forgetting built on this model nevertheless failed, because forgetting requires parameter changes far outside the region in which the model was validated. The probes can, however, bound the probability that a margin first falls below a boundary near zero: we derive Freedman and Azuma first-passage bounds for a linear surrogate of the margin and test on new runs whether they hold for the model. The bounds contain a term R that measures how much the response coefficients change during the run. The simplified Freedman bound, which sets R = 0, certified most facts but was violated in 14 of 112 conditions, and every fact on which it was violated had R >= a, where a is the distance of the fact's margin to the boundary. The complete Freedman bound certifies only facts with R < a, and it held in every condition. On the violated facts, the spread of the margin across test runs was a median of 14.6 times the prediction of the response model, so the failures are breakdowns of the model, and in our data they occurred only where R >= a. We found this pattern post hoc and tested it in two preregistered confirmatory studies with 43 new conditions: the complete bound held in all of them, and the simplified bound failed there on only 3 facts, each with R >= a. First-order models of fine-tuning can thus bound forgetting on the facts whose response coefficients change by less than their distance to the boundary.

</details>


### [160] [Rank Collapse Is Recoverable, Growing $|Q|$ Is Not: Out-of-Sample Early Warning for Value Divergence in High-UTD Soft Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32819)
*Tianqi Bu,YuXuan Peng,Junteng Tu,Henghui Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在高 UTD 下，critic 的价值幅度快速增长是训练失败的早期可预测信号（优于神经元“休眠”），可用于早停以节省计算；LayerNorm 抑制该增长但可能损害回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解为何在高 UTD 情况下离策略 critic 会失效（训练不稳定或发散），并找到能早期预测/缓解失败的可操作信号，以节省大量离线/离策略 RL 的计算资源，同时区分不同失效机理以指导修正措施。

Method: 在 Soft Actor-Critic 框架下用放大 critic（宽度 2048、无需归一化）分离两种故障模式；通过监控单次运行中 critic 的单位激活（判断“休眠/崩塌”）和 Q 的对数幅度 log10|Q|，并使用训练守卫（当 loss 或 |Q| 爆炸时停止）标记失败运行。用早期（step 15k）log10|Q| 的上升率做生存/排序预测，评估 Harrell's C 和离样本 AUC，并衡量基于该速率的提前中止对保存计算量及在线收益的影响；对比 LayerNorm 的效果。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 在 UTD 比高时，表征崩塌（大量单元休眠）并不必然导致失败——有运行在崩塌下仍能继续学习；2) critic 的 log10|Q| 在早期的上升率能有效排序并预测哪个运行将被训练守卫终止（在 Walker2d、Ant 上分别达到离样本 AUC 0.78/0.98）；3) 在 Walker2d 上以该速率提前中止可节省约 10% 的持出计算，并在在线阶段保持净收益；4) 使用 LayerNorm 能降低该上升率并在 Walker2d（UTD=4）消除被终止情况，但同时降低了回报。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：提高更新-数据比（UTD）会通过两种互不等价的机制破坏离线/离策略 critic（称为“可塑性丧失”）：表征崩塌（神经元休眠）和价值幅度增长（|Q| 增大）；其中价值幅度的早期增长率比表征崩塌更能预测训练失败（被训练守卫终止），并可用于早期停止以节省计算。LayerNorm 可以抑制 |Q| 的增长并减少被终止的运行，但同时可能降低最终回报。

Abstract: Raising the update-to-data (UTD) ratio breaks off-policy critics in two ways grouped as "plasticity loss": collapsing representations and growing value magnitude $|Q|$. We separate them in Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with scaled critics (width 2048, no normalization). Collapse is survivable: at UTD ratio 16, HalfCheetah critics with most units dormant keep learning, and the training guard, which stops runs whose loss or $|Q|$ explodes, never flags them. Within one high-UTD SAC configuration, runs start close together, and how far a critic's $\log_{10}|Q|$ has climbed by step 15k, its early growth, ranks the runs by how soon the guard flags them. At 15k, a flagged run's $|Q|$ sits a median of over a hundredfold below its flag level, yet the climb's rate already orders the flags (Harrell's C and out-of-sample AUC 0.78 on Walker2d, 0.98 on Ant, at UTD ratio 4). Dormancy does not. Aborting on this rate saves about a tenth of held-out Walker2d compute and stays net-positive live. A LayerNorm critic lowers the rate, removes the flag on Walker2d at UTD ratio 4 and lowers the return.

</details>


### [161] [Routing Drift Alone Does Not Diagnose Failure in Merged MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32821)
*Yuanyi Wang,Yanggan Gu,Su Lu,Guanghao Zhu,Pengkai Wang,Yifan Yang,Congkai Xie,Zhaoyi Yan,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 路由漂移不是路由失败的充分证据：必须通过在固定非路由参数下的任务级干预效果来判断是否需要修复。工具包与 SRR 代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并在无联合再训练下高效整合专长，但在 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 中常伴随专家路由改变（路由漂移）。社区常把漂移视为失败，但缺乏定量证据与可操作的判据去确定何时应修复。

Method: 提出并使用路由分析工具包：受控反事实干预（交叉源/合并器输入与参数）、逐 token 级别分析、以及定义可操作的路由失败检测（在固定非路由参数条件下评估通过路由干预恢复的任务损失）。作为案例研究，设计并评估了选择性路由修复（SRR）。

Result: 在 DeepSeekMoE、OLMoE、Qwen3-MoE 上的实验证明：大部分专家重分配由输入变化而非同层参数改变引起；源路由与恢复源路由并不能可靠预测或带来下一步 token 概率提升；不同专家选择可产生方向一致的混合输出；在设计的路由腐败测试中可检测到可恢复损失，但在真实合并模型中源路由恢复通常不会带来一致的任务收益；SRR 未能用源专家的 token 概率优势稳定定位有益修复。

Conclusion: 合并 MoE 模型后出现的路由漂移并不必然表明路由失败；必须以任务层面、在仅更改路由而固定其他参数的干预是否能恢复任务损失来判定是否需要修复。

Abstract: Model merging efficiently combines specialized large language models (LLMs) without joint retraining, but can substantially alter expert routing in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Such \emph{routing drift} is often interpreted as routing failure, raising a fundamental question that remains unclear: \emph{does routing drift after MoE merging actually indicate routing failure, and what evidence should justify repair?} We investigate these questions across DeepSeekMoE, OLMoE, and Qwen3-MoE proposing a routing analysis toolkit for controlled counterfactual interventions and token-level analysis. By crossing source and merged router inputs and parameters, we attribute most expert reassignments to input shifts rather than parameter changes at the same layer. However, source-relative routing differences poorly predict next-token likelihood gains from source-route restoration, and different expert selections can produce directionally similar mixture outputs. We therefore operationalize routing failure as \textit{task loss recoverable under a specified routing intervention, with non-routing parameters fixed.} These tests detect recoverable loss under deliberate router corruption, whereas source-route restoration does not establish reliable task benefits in the evaluated merged models. Motivated by these, we propose \emph{Selective Router Repair (SRR)} as a case study, and find that source-specialist token-likelihood advantages do not reliably identify beneficial local corrections. Together, these findings show that \textbf{routing drift alone is insufficient evidence of routing failure}: source-informed corrections must be judged by their task-level intervention effects. The analysis toolkit and SRR code are released.

</details>


### [162] [Improving LLM Collaboration via Multi-Agent Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32827)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Tianle Chen,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAPL，将偏好学习应用于多智能体协作，并以MARLHF和MADPO实现；实验证明能提升协作表现，MARLHF总体更优但受多种因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM协作的多智能体强化学习中，构建可靠的、完整的奖励函数困难且难以聚合，偏好学习提供了从比较反馈（人类或AI）中学习的替代方案，但其在多智能体系统中的扩展尚未充分研究。

Method: 从去中心化与中心化协作角度形式化偏好驱动的多智能体问题，构建通用的多智能体偏好学习框架（MAPL）。MAPL通过与由不同智能体生成的去/中心化解进行比较，迭代更新当前解。具体实现包括基于学习到的奖励模型的MARLHF（MARL from human feedback）和多智能体直接偏好优化（MADPO）。

Result: 在协同写作、编码、工具使用和旅行规划等任务上的实验证明：1) MAPL能改善协作质量与效率，接近使用固定良好定义奖励的MARL；2) 在MAPL内，MARLHF在大多数任务上优于MADPO，但对数据覆盖、智能体与比较器模型以及底层MARL算法较为敏感。

Conclusion: 提出了MAPL框架，将偏好学习推广到多智能体系统（MAS），能在去中心化和中心化协作场景下通过迭代比较改进多智能体协作策略；实验表明MAPL能提升协作质量与效率，并接近具有固定明确定义奖励的MARL表现。

Abstract: Several works have explored multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in LLM collaboration. However, constructing reliable rewards is difficult in practice, as complete and accurate metrics are often unavailable and hard to aggregate. Preference learning provides an alternative by learning from comparative human or AI feedback. Yet, its extension to multi-agent systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we formulate preference-based multi-agent systems (MAS) from decentralized and centralized collaboration perspectives. We also introduce a general multi-agent preference learning framework (MAPL) to solve these problems. MAPL allows iterative updates by comparing the current solution with decentralized or centralized solutions generated by various agents. We instantiate MAPL using MARL from human feedback (MARLHF) with a learned reward model and multi-agent direct preference optimization (MADPO). Experiments on collaborative writing, coding, tool use, and travel planning show that MAPL can improve collaboration quality and efficiency while approaching the performance of MARL with fixed, well-defined rewards. Within MAPL, MARLHF generally outperforms MADPO on most tasks but remains sensitive to data coverage, agent and comparator models, and the underlying MARL algorithms.

</details>


### [163] [FinancialAuditBench: Benchmark Construction under Differential Privacy Using Real-World Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32835)
*Jerry Huang,Sarvesh Babu,Matt Van Buren,Alexander Wang,Pranav Pillai,Arush Jain,James P. Burton,Julia Hockenmaier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在差分隐私约束下结合专业审计知识生成了一个面向审计工作的合成基准，评估表明大型模型具备部分自动化审计能力但需人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限于对专有和隐私敏感审计数据的访问，现有基准多依赖公开数据或人工/LLM生成任务，难以反映真实审计场景；因此需要在保护隐私的前提下，系统化生成更现实的合成审计任务用于模型测评。

Method: 利用差分隐私保护的历史审计汇总统计数据作为约束，结合超过1100小时的审计专业知识参与，构建了一个包含六个合成审计参与、共90项任务（平均每个参与约179个文件）的任务生成框架与基准；在该基准上对11个前沿模型进行了定量评估。

Result: 构建并公开了 FinancialAuditBench 与生成框架；实验显示模型在完成文档和审阅任务上有实质性能力，但仍存在程序不当、文档错误等风险；此外该方法为其他隐私敏感领域生成合成评估任务提供了可复用思路。

Conclusion: 本文提出了 FinancialAuditBench，通过系统化生成合成审计任务并评估代理人在财务报表审计中的表现，发现前沿模型能完成大量员工级别的审计任务，但仍会执行不当程序或输出错误文档，提示需要人工复核。

Abstract: As AI agents are becoming widely adopted in the financial services industry, careful measurement is essential to understand where they can be reliably deployed and where oversight and professional review remain necessary. Such measurement, however, is constrained by limited access to proprietary or privacy-sensitive data. Existing benchmarks therefore often rely on publicly available data, human- and/or LLM-authored tasks, or simplified settings. We introduce FinancialAuditBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on financial statement audit tasks, along with a framework for systematically generating synthetic engagements. Our task generation framework leverages differentially private aggregate statistics from historical audits along with audit expertise contributed through over 1,100 hours of benchmark development and review. FinancialAuditBench consists of 90 tasks spanning workpaper completion and review across six synthetic audit engagements, each containing an average of 179 files. Evaluation on eleven frontier models shows that while agents complete substantial portions of staff-level audit tasks well, they sometimes perform inappropriate procedures or produce incorrect documentation. Beyond financial auditing, our framework offers an approach for systematically generating synthetic tasks for model evaluation and training in privacy-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [164] [Counterfactual Self-Evolving Agents for Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32870)
*Xing Han,Yuxin Wang,Chen Chen,Wei Dai,Gautham Krishna Gudur,Shijun Li,Hsing-Huan Chung,Gregory D. Hager,Joydeep Ghosh,Paul Pu Liang,Suchi Saria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用专家标注的反事实指令微调Proposer，结合Solver/Verifier反馈设计奖励，并把被接受的反事实存入记忆以供冻结求解器在上下文中适应，从而在证据可识别任务上显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于依赖特定证据与领域知识以得出可检验答案的任务，传统自我对弈难以自动生成既合理又可验证的案例。需要一种机制自动产生高质量、可核查的反事实证据以用于训练与修正模型推理。

Method: 训练一个可调教的Proposer生成分类化的证据编辑并给出因果性解释；通过专家人工构造的反事实指令微调数据集进行instruction-tuning；基于Solver和Verifier的反馈设计细化的微调奖励；将被接受的反事实累积到记忆中作为Frozen Solver的上下文，使Solver通过演化的上下文而非参数更新来适应新证据。

Result: 在临床推理、事实验证与业务推理等多种领域上，随着反事实记忆的逐轮增长，该框架在多种前沿模型上均取得了优越性，并能向更难案例转移能力。

Conclusion: 提出的“反事实自我演化（counterfactual self-evolution）”框架有效地扩展了自我对弈提议者—求解者范式，使其能在证据可识别的任务中生成可验证的、针对性的反事实证据，从而修正错误或增强对正确决策的置信。

Abstract: Self-play proposer--solver methods improve reasoning by generating tasks and learning from verified solutions. However, for evidence-identifiable tasks, where case-specific evidence and domain knowledge determine a checkable answer, self-play requires generating plausible cases whose answers can be independently verified. We introduce counterfactual self-evolution, which generates counterfactual context for reconsidering the original case. A trainable Proposer constructs targeted evidence edits and describes potential outcome changes with causal explanations. We handcraft an expert-verified counterfactual instruction-tuning dataset to teach the Proposer to generate high-quality counterfactuals across a broad range of action--outcome scenarios. Each counterfactual instruction-tuning example specifies an edit within a defined category and explains its hypothesized causal effect on the decision, teaching the Proposer to reason systematically about what changes and why. We instruction-tune the Proposer on these examples, then formulate a fine-tuning reward that integrates feedback from the Solver and Verifier. Across diverse counterfactual scenarios, this reward favors high-quality counterfactuals and warranted revisions, while penalizing changes that overturn correct decisions. The counterfactual context aims to correct errors and strengthen confidence in correct decisions. Accepted counterfactuals accumulate in memory that supplies in-context evidence to the frozen Solver; the Solver adapts through evolving context rather than weight updates. We apply the framework to clinical reasoning, fact verification, and business reasoning. Our evaluation tracks performance over successive rounds as counterfactual memory grows, including transfer to harder cases. Our method achieves superior results across diverse frontier models.

</details>


### [165] [Can LLMs Predict the Future? A Brier Score Analysis of Prediction Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32885)
*Yuanbo Li,Zekun Li,Xiaoyan cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在零样本问题-仅评估下，模型升级并未带来稳健的概率估计改进，域内差异比版本差异更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验模型升级是否能带来更准确的概率输出，并强调在解释预测性能时应同时考虑简单基准与问题构成。

Method: 构建Resolved Market Forecasting (RMF) 基准：3,000个已决二元问题、九个域；在问题-仅、零样本设置下评估6个Claude和Qwen模型变体；以Brier分数相对经验基线评估，做Murphy分解、配对差异比较，并按事件类别和训练截止时间分层报告结果。

Result: 在后截止（post-cutoff）分层，四个Claude模型的Brier为0.183–0.192，比基线低0.024–0.033；Qwen 32B未显著优于基线；Claude版本/层级升级未显示显著提升；同一模型在不同事件类别间的差异大于不同Claude变体之间的差异。

Conclusion: 在该评估协议下（问题-仅、零样本、与经验基准比较），新版或更高层级的模型并不持续改善概率估计的准确性。

Abstract: We study whether model upgrades improve probability estimates for prediction-market questions. Our Resolved Market Forecasting (RMF) benchmark contains 3,000 resolved binary questions across nine domains, on which we evaluate six Claude and Qwen model variants using a question-only, zero-shot protocol. We assess Brier scores relative to an empirical base-rate predictor, examine their Murphy decomposition, and compare models through paired differences, with results stratified by event category and timing relative to training cutoffs. In the reported post-cutoff stratum, the four Claude models achieve Brier scores of 0.183-0.192, improving on the base-rate reference by 0.024-0.033. Qwen 32B does not significantly outperform that reference, although its paired Brier is 0.024 lower than that of the 7B checkpoint. The evaluated Claude version and tier upgrades yield no significant improvement. Within-model differences across event categories exceed the observed differences among Claude variants. These results show why forecasting scores should be interpreted alongside simple probability baselines and question composition: under this protocol, newer versions or higher model tiers do not consistently produce more accurate probabilities.

</details>


### [166] [StraTune: Adaptive Selection of Revision Operators for Self-Evolving LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32886)
*Zeping Liu,Yan Li,Ni Lao,Gil Wolff,Gengchen Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StraTune用冻结优化器LLM基于历史与当前反馈自适应选取修订算子，通过候选筛选和结果回写实现更稳健高效的技能修订，实验表明相较固定/随机/调度/bandit策略显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能修订方法依赖单一或预设的修订算子与搜索策略，但没有任何单一算子在所有任务上始终最优，反复应用不合适的算子会阻碍进一步提升，因此需要一种能在运行时自适应选择修订算子的框架。

Method: 定义优化状态（包含当前执行反馈和以往算子/形式的记录）；每轮由冻结的优化器LLM从候选修订算子中选择一个；每个算子产生候选技能并经过两阶段评估（小样本筛选再大样本验证）；评估结果写回优化状态以指导后续选择。

Result: 在四个基准和两种LLM设定下，StraTune在大多数设置中超过五个基线方法；消融实验证明收益主要来自自适应算子选择；使用小型号目标LLM学到的技能能提升更强模型；代码与技能在公开仓库可得。

Conclusion: StraTune通过让冻结的优化器LLM基于优化状态自适应选择修订算子，从而显著优于固定或简单策略的修订方法，改进学习到的文本技能并具备跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can learn reusable textual skills from execution feedback without updating their parameters, but effectively deciding how to revise these skills remains a key challenge. Existing methods typically rely on a fixed revision operator, a search strategy and the revision forms applied under it. However, we observe that no single revision operator consistently performs best across tasks, and repeatedly applying an unsuitable operator can limit further improvement. We propose StraTune (strategy-guided skill tuning), which lets a frozen optimizer LLM choose the revision operator at every round from the optimization state, which is defined as the current execution feedback together with the recorded outcomes of earlier strategies and forms. Candidate skills from every revision operator pass one candidate evaluation, which screens for gains and regressions on a small sample set and validates them on a larger one, and every outcome is written back to the optimization state for later choices. Across four benchmarks and two LLM settings, StraTune outperforms all five baselines in most settings. Ablations attribute the gains to the adaptive choice of the revision operator, since fixed, random, scheduled, and bandit strategy choices all score lower, and skills learned with a small target LLM also improve a stronger one. Code and learned skills are available at https://github.com/seai-lab/StraTune.

</details>


### [167] [Constraints Are Graphs, Not Chains: Exact Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32900)
*Jianchang Su,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了基于因子图与变量消元的训练免费约束解码器 FactorDLM，使得对有限域关系的约束解码复杂度由因子圖的诱导宽度主导，避免了顺序化自动机的指数状态爆炸，并在多项基准上以低开销实现完备约束满足且效率优于通用求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的精确约束解码惯常把约束编码成顺序语言（自动机或文法），但对许多关系型约束（如同序拷贝）会导致状态数或文法规模呈指数增长；而从关系的因子化表达看，这些约束可以用线性大小的因子图紧凑表示。作者旨在用更直接的因子化表示和精确条件化来降低解码时的计算与表示开销。

Method: 在不改变模型权重的前提下，FactorDLM 将关系约束表示为因子图，并在每个去噪步骤上通过变量消元（精确的均场条件化）来调整模型的均值预测，解码复杂度受约束图的诱导宽度支配。该方法可与链形自动机联合编译以同时强制语法与非局部引用约束。

Result: 在九个关系基准与三种 backbone 模型上，FactorDLM 在 0.4–6.9% 的投影开销下使每个输出满足所有约束（相比无约束解码的 0–79% 合法率），且已编译的投影比八线程 CP-SAT 快 13.6 倍。作者还构造了具有已知随机基线与模板下限的新基准，验证在这些场景中基于精确约束样本的选择优于贪心投影。

Conclusion: 作者提出的 FactorDLM 用因子图代替顺序化的自动机或文法来表示有限域关系，从而在约束解码中避免指数级的状态爆炸，实验证明在多项关系基准上能以低投影开销保证生成输出满足所有声明的约束，并显著快于通用 CP-SAT 求解器。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) predict masked positions in arbitrary order, but their exact constrained decoders still encode constraints as sequential languages, whose state must track every unresolved dependency between positions. For relational constraints this encoding grows exponentially: for same-order copy, every finite automaton needs $4^k$ states, deterministic or nondeterministic, and every context-free grammar has size $2^{Ω(k)}$, while the factor graph of the same relation has size $O(k)$ and a 16-entry peak table. We introduce FactorDLM, a training-free decoder that represents finite-domain relations as a factor graph and, at each denoising step, conditions the model's mean-field prediction on that graph exactly by variable elimination. Decoding cost then grows exponentially with the induced width of the constraint graph, which replaces automaton size as the governing parameter. Because a finite automaton is a chain-shaped factor graph, one compiler enforces syntax and nonlocal relations together: on JSON records with cross-field references, a schema automaton alone leaves references dangling, relational factors alone produce malformed JSON, and the combined plan is valid on both counts, including on records of variable length. Across nine relational benchmarks and three backbones, every output satisfies every declared constraint at 0.4-6.9% projection overhead, where unconstrained decoding is 0-79% valid, and compiled projection answers repeated queries 13.6x faster than CP-SAT with eight parallel workers. Because model-free rules solve three of five standard benchmarks, we construct benchmarks with exact chance and fixed-template floors, on which selecting among exact constrained samples beats greedy projection. Which encoding is cheaper, sequential state or direct factors, depends on the constraint and is computable before decoding begins.

</details>


### [168] [Logical subspace in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32907)
*Hope Kean,Enric Boix-Adsera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MVS方法并在两款模型上找到支持逻辑推理的低秩、可局部化子空间，表明语言模型中可能存在类似人脑的逻辑专用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否像人脑一样，语言模型中也存在一套专门支持抽象形式推理的可局部化机制；从解释性与认知比较的角度判断模型能力是否模块化。

Method: 提出了最小可行子空间（MVS）方法：在某一层搜索能在切除该子空间外所有激活后仍保持任务性能的最低秩激活子空间。通过对Gemma和Qwen模型的层激活进行投影/消融，评估不同秩子空间对逻辑推理及其他任务的影响。

Result: 在Gemma和Qwen上发现低秩子空间可支持逻辑推理；这些子空间与模型在事实知识、工作记忆、认知控制、算术等能力上的表现在功能上可分离。保留“逻辑”子空间能保全推理同时削弱其它能力，反之消融导致推理精度回落到随机但大体保留其它能力。

Conclusion: 本文认为大型语言模型中存在可局部化的、低秩的‘逻辑’激活子空间，类似于人脑中负责抽象推理的网络。保留这一子空间可以维持逻辑推理能力而损害其他能力；消除它则使逻辑推理降至随机水平而大体不影响其它能力。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a human brain network specialized for abstract formal reasoning (Kean et al., 2025). Does the same hold true in language models? To answer this question, we introduce the minimal viable subspace (MVS) method, which searches for the lowest-rank activation subspace at a layer that preserves task performance when everything outside that subspace is ablated. Using MVS, we demonstrate low-rank subspaces supporting logical inference on Gemma and Qwen models. Furthermore, these subspaces exhibit a clear dissociation from model capacities on other tasks, such that retaining these late logic subspaces preserves inference while impairing factual knowledge, working memory, cognitive control, and arithmetic. Conversely, ablating them reduces logical inference accuracy to chance while largely sparing these other capacities. Our results suggest a functionally localizable core machinery for logic akin to that in the human brain.

</details>


### [169] [Planner-as-Router: Joint Plan-Time Model Routing for Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32917)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam,Gautam Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaR把模型层级选择集成到规划中，在链式工作流上实现显著成本降低且保持接近最佳准确率，基于54个真实执行任务的基准验证并开源代码与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境中运行大型语言模型代理开销极大，尤其当工作流串联多个调用时，单次按调用决策（如级联路由）无法看到全局依赖和成本累积，因而需要一种在规划阶段进行成本感知的路由策略以降低费用同时保持可接受的准确率。

Method: 提出Planner-as-Router（PaR）：在将查询分解为子任务的同时为每个子任务分配模型层级（小/中/前沿），使整个工作流的依赖关系在调用前可见，从而无需独立的路由模型或训练数据。用新构建的基准EntBench（54个企业任务，真实执行SQL/Mongo查询）在1,157次评估、8种路由器、3个随机种子下比较方案表现。

Result: PaR在实验中位于观测到的成本-准确率前沿：与仅终端使用前沿模型的策略在准确率上持平且成本相当；比FrugalGPT级联在成本上更节约；相较于全部使用前沿模型节省约44%成本、平均损失2.9个百分点准确率。若干准确率差异落在±6个百分点的置信区间内，作者将PaR的优势描述为前沿位置优势而非绝对准确率提升，并提出廉价路由可能在组合性工作流上产生隐性累积惩罚的假设。

Conclusion: PaR通过把模型层级分配嵌入到规划阶段，在工作流级别上进行成本-能力折衷，能够在成本-准确率的观测前沿上运行：与只在终端节点用前沿模型的策略在准确率上相当、在成本上优于多数逐调用路由器，并在与全部使用前沿模型相比时节省了约44%的成本，同时平均牺牲约2.9个百分点的准确率。

Abstract: Running large language model (LLM) agents in production gets expensive fast. A frontier model (the largest, most capable tier) is accurate but can cost 25 times what a small model costs per token, and the gap compounds once a workflow chains several calls together. Planner-as-Router (PaR) attacks this from a different angle. Instead of leaving model-tier selection to some component downstream, it folds the choice into planning itself. As the planner breaks a query into subtasks, it also assigns each one a model size tier (small, mid, or frontier, ordered by capability and price), so the dependencies between subtasks are visible before any specialist runs. Unlike per-call routers such as cascade routing, which look at one node at a time, PaR sees the whole workflow up front and needs no separate router model or training data. We evaluate PaR with EntBench, a benchmark of 54 enterprise agentic tasks across seven classes, graded by actually running the generated Structured Query Language (SQL) and MongoDB queries against live databases. Over 1,157 evaluations spanning eight routers and three seeds, PaR stays on the observed cost-accuracy frontier. It matches a sink-frontier heuristic (frontier model on terminal nodes only) in accuracy at comparable cost and a faithful FrugalGPT cascade at lower cost, and cuts cost 44% against all-frontier routing while giving up 2.9 points of accuracy. Several accuracy gaps fall inside the plus-or-minus six-point confidence interval of a 54-task study, so we frame PaR's advantage as frontier position rather than a clean accuracy win. We also report a preliminary observation, not a validated result: a small pilot hints that cheap routing may carry a hidden compounding penalty on compositional workflows, which we frame as a hypothesis for future measurement. PaR, EntBench, and all evaluation code are open source.

</details>


### [170] [Precision As You Need: Stochastic Computing Is a Dense Adaptive Quantizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32922)
*Haoran Jin,Kangqi Zhang,Jirong Yang,Barry Lyu,Qiuyi Ding,Ruijie Gao,Nathan Bleier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在 GPU 上仿真并验证了随机计算（以比特流长度控制精度）作为一种稠密可变精度方案，可在视觉 Transformer 上实现无需重训练的按行混合精度推理，效果可与传统 INT 量化相比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉 Transformer 的推理成本被矩阵乘法主导；现有高效化（后训练量化、混合精度）受限于传统加速器支持的固定位宽格式（INT4/8、BF16、FP16），缺少细粒度、可变的精度控制机制。

Method: 将随机计算重新视为按比特流长度 L 控制精度的自适应量化器；在 GPU 上实现大规模 SC 矩阵乘法仿真库，暴露流长为内核参数；不需重训练，设计动态按行混合精度策略（每 token/组分配流长）并在相同硬件下复用。对图像分类、目标检测、实例分割、条件图像生成和视觉世界模型规划进行端到端评估。

Result: 在多项视觉任务上，SC 在匹配平均比特预算时与固定格式 INT 量化表现相当；引入的按行混合精度在更低平均流长下可以保持模型精度；展示了软件层面可行性，暗示 SC 可作为细粒度混合精度推理的底层基石。

Conclusion: 基于软件级评估，随机计算（SC）可作为一种稠密可变精度子层，用于对视觉Transformer进行细粒度混合精度推理：在相同平均比特预算下，SC 与定点（INT）量化在准确率上具有可比性；而按行/按 token 的动态混合精度策略能在更低平均流长下保持性能。

Abstract: Matrix multiplications dominate the inference cost of modern transformer-based vision models, yet existing efficiency techniques such as post-training quantization and mixed-precision inference are largely limited to the small set of fixed-width formats (INT4, INT8, BF16, and FP16) supported by conventional accelerators. We revisit stochastic computing (SC) as a way to lift this constraint: viewed as a dense adaptive quantizer, SC controls precision by bit-stream length L rather than a fixed datapath, while each multiplication reduces to a single AND/XNOR gate. We build a GPU library that emulates SC matrix multiplication at scale, exposes stream lengths as first-class kernel arguments, and evaluates SC end-to-end on image classification, object detection and instance segmentation, class-conditional image generation, and visual world-model planning. On top of this substrate, we develop a dynamic per-row mixed-precision policy that assigns stream length per token or group at matched average budget, requires no retraining, and uses the same SC hardware across schedules. Across tasks, SC remains competitive with fixed-format INT quantization at matched bit budgets, while per-row mixed precision helps maintain accuracy at lower average stream lengths. These results provide software-level feasibility evidence that SC can serve as a dense-precision substrate for fine-grained mixed-precision inference on modern vision transformers.

</details>


### [171] [TRACE: Learning to Self-Calibrate Wireless Digital Twins from ISAC Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32923)
*Saad Masrur,Saeed R. Khosravirad,Ismail Guvenc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE通过对齐实测与孪生渲染射频的残差并用多视角学习预测建筑六参数修正，实现了高精度的数字孪生自校准，并在合成到真实数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真传播引擎对环境模型高度敏感，而现实中用于初始化数字孪生的三维地图常含位置、高度、朝向和外形误差。研究旨在让已部署的无线网络利用自身射频测量数据自动修复这些孪生模型，提高传播仿真与基于孪生的网络决策的准确性。

Method: 提出基于物理的学习自校准框架TRACE：对当前DT用射线跟踪渲染，分别对实测与仿真射频进行相干逆投影到统一世界栅格，围绕每栋建筑提取相同局部视图；通过一个多视角校正器融合来自多个感测节点和邻近建筑的证据，预测每栋建筑的门控六参数修正（支持迭代重渲染）。方法在输入上不依赖全局布局或传感器顺序。

Result: 在5400个未见模拟样本（28 GHz）上，TRACE将建筑三维位置RMSE从2.202 m降至0.302 m，偏航RMSE从4.978°降至0.894°，性能优于ViT和U-Net基线，并在布局、建筑数量、感测节点数量和SNR变化下保持鲁棒。在NIST户外庭院的28 GHz实测数据上（训练仅用合成数据），将平面墙体位置平均误差从1.00 m降至7.8 cm，且未用实测微调或几何标签。

Conclusion: TRACE证明了通过将测量的射频与孪生渲染的射频进行残差对齐，可以作为有效的学习信号来修复和校准三维数字孪生（DT）。所提出的方法在模拟和实测28 GHz数据上均显著降低了平面位置、三维位置和偏航误差，且无需实测数据微调或几何标签即可实现从合成到真实数据的泛化。

Abstract: Wireless digital twins (DTs) rely on 3D environment models to predict radio propagation and support wireless-network decisions, yet these models are often initialized from imperfect 3D maps. Errors in building position, height, footprint, and orientation can therefore cause a high-fidelity propagation engine to simulate the wrong physical environment. In this paper, we study how a deployed wireless network can repair an existing DT using its own radio frequency (RF) measurements. In particular, we introduce Twin Residual Alignment and Calibration Engine (TRACE), a physics-grounded learning-based self-calibration framework that treats twin maintenance as residual alignment between the physical world and the current DT. Using the same sensing configuration as the physical measurements, TRACE ray-traces the current DT, coherently backprojects the measured and simulated RF onto a common world grid, and extracts the same local region around each building's current DT position. A multi-view corrector then fuses evidence across sensing nodes and neighboring buildings to predict a gated six-parameter correction per building, without relying on absolute layout or sensor ordering, and supports iterative correction through re-rendering. On 5,400 held-out samples from unseen simulated scenes at 28 GHz, TRACE reduces 3D position RMSE from 2.202 m to 0.302 m and yaw RMSE from 4.978° to 0.894°, outperforming ViT and U-Net baselines under changes in layout, building count, sensing-node count, and SNR. On measured 28 GHz RF data from the NIST outdoor courtyard, a model trained only on synthetic RF reduces mean planar wall-position error from 1.00 m to 7.8 cm, without measured-data fine-tuning or geometric labels. These results show that the discrepancy between measured and twin-rendered RF can serve as a learning signal for repairing a wireless DT.

</details>


### [172] [Diagnosing Sampled LLM Reasoning in Formal Geometry: Coverage, Realization, and Validity Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32924)
*Xiao Yue,Guangzhi Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRV把覆盖、读出、推导支持三者分离并分别量化；实验显示多数被采样出正确答案并不伴随可靠的读出或推导支持，建议在LLM推理评估中分别报告这三类证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样可暴露正确数值但不保证系统输出可靠或推导有支持，现有评估（如pass@k）混淆了候选可用性、读出可靠性与推导有效性，迫切需要解耦这些维度以更精确评估LLM推理能力。

Method: 在形式几何状态下对采样的LLM推理应用CRV协议：先冻结每题的候选答案池，再比较不同读出策略的表现；通过分析为何覆盖失败（正确答案数量、题内区分度）和对已覆盖的样本用盲标注批评者评估推导支持度。基准包含HardShift441（441题，参考解算器未解406题）与对195道覆盖题的人工审核。

Result: 在HardShift441上，LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-7B生成器单样本平均准确率24.2%，pass@16为68.9%，而基于验证器加权自洽（WSC）达到38.0%。当池中正确答案只有1~2个时，readout准确率显著下降。在195道已覆盖题的审计中，批评者认为仅12个正确答案代表有推导支持，181个被判为不支持，2个不确定。

Conclusion: CRV定义了三种互不等同的证据维度：coverage（候选池中含有正确答案的概率）、realization（从冻结候选池中读出正确答案的准确率）、validity evidence（经盲标注的衍生支持度而非形式证明）。实验表明这些量应分别报告：覆盖率高并不意味着readout或证明支持高。

Abstract: Repeated sampling can reveal a correct numerical answer without yielding either a reliable system output or a supported derivation. We present Coverage, Realization, and Validity Evidence (CRV), an evaluation protocol for sampled large language model (LLM) reasoning over formal geometry states. Coverage is answer availability, realization is readout accuracy on the frozen candidate pool, and validity evidence is a label-blinded critic judgment of derivational support rather than a proof certificate. CRV freezes each candidate pool before comparing readouts and analyzes covered failures by correct-answer multiplicity and within-problem discrimination. On HardShift441, a 441-problem set for which a reference solver leaves 406 problems unsolved, a LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5-7B generator obtains 24.2% average single-sample accuracy and 68.9% pass@16, whereas verifier-weighted self-consistency (WSC) reaches 38.0%. Readout accuracy is particularly low when the correct answer occurs only once or twice in the pool. In a separate constructed audit of 195 covered problems, the critic labels 12 correct-answer representatives as supported, 181 as refuted, and two as uncertain. These results show that coverage, realization, and validity evidence from the critic are distinct quantities and should be reported separately.

</details>


### [173] [The Commit-Abstain Circuit: Why Language Models Hallucinate Instead of Abstaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32964)
*Vy Nguyen,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan,Estrid He,Feng Xia,Renqiang Luo,Erik Cambria,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Using causal gating, the authors find a small, causally-localised Commit-Abstain Circuit in LMs that causes overcommitment through early accumulation and late undercorrection; exploiting its activations with a lightweight policy substantially improves abstention decisions and generalises across models and benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMs often hallucinate by giving confident but unsupported answers instead of abstaining when information is insufficient; prior work focused on detection/abstention mechanisms but not on how models internally decide to commit or abstain. The aim is to mechanistically identify the internal circuitry that produces overcommitment and leverage it to improve abstention.

Method: Mechanistic causal analysis using causal gating to locate specific attention heads and MLP sublayers (the CAC) across 10 models (3B–14B) and three benchmarks; layerwise intervention and activation analysis to reveal an accumulate-yet-undercorrect dynamic; training a lightweight classifier on CAC activations to improve commit/abstain decisions, validating transfer to unseen benchmarks and larger models (27B–35B).

Result: Discovery of a recurrent CAC comprising a sparse set of attention heads and MLPs; empirical finding of early-layer accumulation of commitment and late-layer corrective but insufficient abstention signals; a lightweight policy trained on CAC activations yields +12.2 percentage points decision accuracy, 2.5x fewer false abstentions, generalises to unseen benchmarks, and scales to larger models.

Conclusion: The paper identifies a sparse, causally-localised Commit-Abstain Circuit (CAC) that drives LM decisions to commit or abstain, showing commitment accumulates in early layers while late abstention signals undercorrect, and demonstrates a lightweight CAC-based policy that meaningfully improves abstention accuracy and reduces false abstentions across models and benchmarks.

Abstract: Language models (LMs) often hallucinate by committing to confident answers rather than abstaining, even when they do not have enough information to answer reliably. A large body of existing work mitigates hallucination through detection or abstention mechanisms, but leaves open how models internally arrive at the decision to commit or abstain in the first place. We study this decision through mechanistic analysis, framing hallucination as unsupported commitment: the model commits despite exhibiting signals of unanswerability. Using causal gating, we identify a Commit-Abstain Circuit (CAC), a sparse, causally localised subset of attention heads and MLP sublayers underlying this decision. Across ten LMs (3B-14B) from five families and three benchmarks, the CAC exhibits a recurring accumulate-yet-undercorrect pattern: commitment-promoting components build up commitment in earlier layers, while abstention-promoting components act later as corrective signals that are often insufficient to overturn the accumulated commitment. Building on this finding, a lightweight policy trained on CAC activations improves decision accuracy by 12.2 points over the model's intrinsic commit-abstain margin, reduces false abstentions by 2.5 times, transfers to unseen benchmarks, and extends to larger models (27B-35B). The CAC is both diagnostic, clarifying how models overcommit, and practical, enabling improved abstention decisions.

</details>


### [174] [Certified Long-Horizon Code Agent Evolution via Validation-Gated Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32990)
*Yifan Wang,Hao Cheng,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Dongwon Jung,Hao Tang,Keru Wang,Chenliang Zhou,Qianhui Wu,Wenlin Yao,Ananth Grama,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Baolin Peng,Jaron Lanier,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VALVE 是一个带验证门控的上下文技能演化框架，理论证明确保收敛与可控回撤，实验证明在1000+软件工程任务的长期部署中显著稳定提升代理表现并大幅压缩技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向长期部署的 agent，需要在不更新权重的情形下积累、压缩并复用可迁移技能，软件仓库级别的软件工程任务流是考察长时程适应与经验持久化的理想基准；现有非参数文本化技能优化方法在长期部署下常出现不稳定更新、性能回撤与代理崩溃。

Method: 提出“验证门控”的演化流程：在不改变模型参数的前提下，通过文本化的技能（skill bank）产生候选技能、用独立的验证集筛选并仅在通过门控时合并入技能库，同时通过评估集合衡量未来任务收益与回撤。理论上给出有限收敛性证明、未来任务收益与回撤的保证，并推导出满足容差所需的验证/评估保持集规模的主阶标度。实验在跨1000+软件工程任务序列上，对 GPT-5.5、Claude-4.6、MiniMax-M2.7 三个大模型进行长期演化验证与消融。

Result: 理论上：证明了有限收敛性并提供对未来任务增益和回撤的界与保持集规模的标度结论。实证上：在>1000 个 SWE 任务演化中获得稳定自提升，平均最终/峰值增益分别为 14.9 和 16.5 点（跨三种前沿模型）；验证门控将平均回撤降低约75%，并使技能库比无门控演化更紧凑，规模小约11倍；同时给出大量消融实验以确定关键设计要素。

Conclusion: VALVE 提供了一种验证门控的、基于上下文的长期技能优化框架，使得不需要模型权重更新的部署代理在长时程软件工程任务流中实现稳定自我提升，并在理论与实证上证明了收敛性与可控的性能回撤。

Abstract: Long horizon agent self-evolution without model weight updates is essential for enabling deployed agents to accumulate reusable skills and improve over time. Prior self-evolution work has focused primarily on short-horizon tasks, while repository-level software engineering remains unexplored despite being an ideal testbed for long-horizon adaptation. In this setting, agents are required to solve streams of sequential tasks, navigate complex dependencies with evolving repositories and persistently store and reuse experience. Text-based skill optimization offers an efficient, non-parametric approach for such adaptation. However, existing methods often suffer from unstable updates, performance drawdown, and agent collapse over extended deployments. In this paper, we formalize the concept of in-context self-evolution and introduce VALVE, a validated-gated framework for long-horizon skill optimization. We establish finite convergence, provide theoretical guarantees for future-task gain and drawdown, and derive the validation and evaluation holdout sizes required for a prescribed tolerance, with leading-order scaling
  Empirically, our pipeline, VALVE achieves stable self-improvement over evolution horizon spanning more than 1,000 SWE tasks, with average final and peak gains of $14.9$ and $16.5$ points across three frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude-4.6 and MiniMax-M2.7). The validation gate reduces average drawdown by 75% and produces an 11x more compact skill bank than ungated evolution. We further present extensive ablations identifying the design choices most critical to long-horizon skill evolution.

</details>


### [175] [X-Tree: Tokenizing Reusable Experience for Efficient Agent Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32993)
*Sitao Cheng,Xunjian Yin,Zhiyuan Sun,Yuxuan Li,Ruiwen Zhou,Xiangru Jian,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自动从动作流中挖掘并训练层级技能（X-Tree），无需LLM调用，即可提高离线/在线/自蒸馏训练下的多步代理表现与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的平铺（token级）训练忽视跨任务反复出现的子过程与分层结构，导致轨迹利用不充分；现有用LLM写技能的方法只在上下文中有效，未将技能注入模型权重，因而泛化受限。

Method: 基于可重用性对动作片段打分并对动作进行规范化合并，构建可重用的eXperience树（X-Tree）。将X-Tree融入三种训练方案：离线RL（将每个节点作为训练实例）、在线RLVR（引入自适应技能奖励）和在策略内的自蒸馏（将X-Tree作为有特权上下文的自教师）。

Result: 在WebArena、ScienceWorld、WebShop三个基准和三种模型尺度上，X-Tree在相同数据和计算预算下分别带来最高约+4.5%、+5.8%和+4.1%的成功率提升。配对分析将改进归因于X-Tree结构及三种集成方式。

Conclusion: X-Tree从训练数据自动构建动作层级，将频繁且成功的技能及其子技能组织成可重用树状结构，替代仅在上下文中调用的LLM技能，从而以更高效的数据利用率和无额外LLM调用的前提下提升多步任务代理的泛化与性能。

Abstract: Multi-step agents are trained on flat action streams: SFT and RLVR weight every token uniformly and ignore the sub-procedures that recur across tasks, the hierarchy that lets humans plan top-down from reusable routines. This structure sits unused, and flat training uses each scarce trajectory less fully than its content allows. Recent agents do use that structure, but only as LLM-written skills in context, never in the weights, so their gains do not generalize beyond retrieval. We instead recover this hierarchy from the data itself and train on it, with no LLM calls. Following text tokenizers, which build a vocabulary by counting alone, we score action spans by reusability and merge canonicalized actions into a reusable eXperience tree (X-Tree). Each X-Tree node captures how a frequent and success-bearing skill is composed from sub-skills, guiding efficient generalization. We integrate X-Tree into three training settings: offline RL, with each node as a training instance; online RLVR, with an adaptive skill bonus; and on-policy self-distillation, with X-Tree as the self-teacher's privileged context. Across WebArena, ScienceWorld, and WebShop at three model scales, X-Tree improves over standard recipes at matched data and budget by up to 4.5% SR on WebArena, 5.8% SR on ScienceWorld and 4.1% success on WebShop. Matched analyses attribute the gains to the X-Tree structure and the three integrations.

</details>


### [176] [Model-Aware Data Selection from In-and-Out Information Interplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33010)
*Yifan Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Dongwon Jung,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Ananth Grama,Varun Chandrasekaran,Yu Hu,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现层级上激活与权重秩的互补模式，提出基于早晚层表征差异的模型感知数据选择CAP，显著提高少量数据时的微调效果并具备跨模态与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的LLM已吸收大量知识，但需要后训练筛选合适数据使模型可靠“知道自己知道什么”。如何在数据选择上利用模型内部表征，识别出对当前模型真正有增益的样本，是提高高效微调/后训练的数据利用率的核心问题。

Method: 通过在所有层计算隐藏状态（数据流）和权重的秩分布，观测到激活U型、权重倒U型；提出CAP（Counterfactual Assimilation Profile）：用模型生成响应与参考响应在早／晚层表示的发散差值作为可获取信息度量，基于该分数对数据进行优先选择并用于后续训练。

Result: 在数学、代码和科学任务上，CAP在不同选择预算下对基线平均提升35.4%；仅用10%数据即在数学和科学任务上达到或超过全数据训练效果；同时CAP对多模态选择、响应长度和噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文发现大模型内部存在“权重秩”和“激活秩”之间的平衡：激活（数据流）在层次上呈U型，而权重秩呈倒U型，表明中间层激活只需保留权重无法提供的信息；基于此提出CAP方法，通过比较早/晚层模型生成响应与参考响应的表示差异来判断数据候选是否对当前模型有可获取信息，显著提升数据选择效果。

Abstract: LLMs are effective representations that assimilate vast amounts of knowledge during pretraining, but post-training is necessary for models to reliably access this knowledge and "know what they know." We observe an interesting rank equilibrium between knowledge stored in the weights and the data stream passing through the model. Across all model layers, we find that the hidden states (data stream) follow a U-shaped pattern, showing substantial compression in early layers and a steep rise during the late-layer decoding phase. In contrast, the weight rank follows an inverted U-shaped pattern, with very low rank in the early and late layers and high rank in the middle. We interpret this as an in-and-out information interplay: intermediate activations do not need to carry content that the weights can supply later, so they primarily preserve what the weights cannot provide.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose a model-aware data selection method, CAP (Counterfactual Assimilation Profile), which can determine whether a data candidate contains information accessible to the current model by utilizing the divergence gap in early- and late-layer representations between model-generated and reference responses. Across math, code, and science domains, CAP delivers 35.4% greater average improvement over the base model than the strongest baseline under different selection budgets. With only 10% of the data pool, CAP surpasses or matches full-pool training on math and science. We further show that CAP transfers to multimodal data selection and is robust to response horizon and noise.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Hypothesis-awkward: Property-Based Testing Strategies for Awkward Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31820)
*Tai Sakuma,Ianna Osborne,Peter Elmer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个为 Awkward Array 定制的 Hypothesis 策略库，能够生成广泛的数组变体并在 CI 中自动发现缺陷，已实际找到多处 bug，证明了方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Awkward Array 表示复杂的嵌套、可变长度和混合类型数据，现有单元测试依赖预定义样例覆盖面有限；借助 Hypothesis 的属性化测试可以自动探索大量边界和组合情况，暴露潜在缺陷。

Method: 使用 Hypothesis 的策略系统定义生成器，参数化控制布局、数据类型、缺失值、掩码和其他数组属性，生成几乎所有可能的 Awkward Array 变种，并将这些策略用于持续集成中的属性测试。

Result: 在将策略应用到 Awkward Array 的持续集成后，已发现 37 个 Awkward Array 的 bug 和 1 个 Hypothesis 本身的 bug；测试开始覆盖项目的一小部分并增强了测试可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并实现了 Hypothesis-awkward，一个为 Awkward Array 提供 Hypothesis 策略的库，能大规模生成几乎所有可能的 Awkward Array 实例，从而发现了多个实际 bug，提高了相关工具的测试覆盖和可靠性。

Abstract: Hypothesis-awkward is a collection of Hypothesis strategies for Awkward Array. Awkward Array can represent a wide variety of nested, variable-length, mixed-type data. Many tools that process Awkward Arrays are widely used and actively developed. The unit test cases of many of these tools list predefined input samples. In practice, such samples can cover only a small portion of the vast combinatorial space of Awkward Array instances. Hypothesis, a Python property-based testing library, generates test data that can make test cases fail and automatically explores edge cases. The main strategy of Hypothesis-awkward generates nearly all possible Awkward Arrays, with options to control the layout, data types, missing values, masks, and other array attributes. Property-based tests written with these strategies have begun to run in the continuous integration of Awkward Array; to date, they cover a small part of Awkward Array and have found 37 bugs in it, as well as one in Hypothesis itself. These strategies make the tests of Awkward Array and of the tools that use it more reliable.

</details>


### [178] [Verification as an Architectural Layer for LLM Agents: A V-Model Design, and a Pilot Study of Its Deterministic Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31937)
*Ali Afoud,Jie JW Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将V-Model引入LLM agent，通过分层验证器（确定性门+可选LLM审判）与确定性控制器把错误局部化并让agent以弃答终止。小规模试点在MuSiQue四跳上显示验证显著改善终止性与低成本修正，但仍需扩展到集成层与更大规模评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于ReAct的LLM agent把策略选择、动作生成、格式化与结果判定四项职责揉在同一个生成循环里，且外部无法拒绝其输出，导致无法进展时持续运行直至预算耗尽。动机是把验证作为架构层来分离职责，使错误可定位、使agent能主动拒绝并有确定性控制以保障资源与行为可控。

Method: 将V-Model移植到LLM agent：把从需求到单步的规格按层下降，每层配一对验证器（一个零成本确定性“门” + 一个可选的LLM“审判”）；一个确定性控制器执行所有验证结论，且只有验证通过的输出写入记忆。进行试点实现：只实现接受层和单元层的验证器；在相同执行器、工具集和评分器下比较五种仅在验证策略上不同的配置；在MuSiQue四跳任务上用8B参数骨干模型，47次执行、10个问题，记录终止与答题情况，并分析修正来源与成本。

Result: 试验表明：两种未经验证的配置在10个问题上均未给出答案，每次运行都以步骤上限或提供商令牌限制终止；带验证但无规划器的配置回答了8题并对其余题目选择弃答；确定性门在观测到的9次修正中贡献了8次且无额外成本；当启用验证后，规划行为反而使表现下降。作者强调这些结果只表明终止行为而非大规模精度，并计划进一步完成与评估完整架构（含集成层验证）。

Conclusion: 作者提出用软件工程的V-Model在LLM agent架构中引入分层验证器：每个规格层配对专门的验证器，验证结果受确定性控制器强制执行，且只有验证结果能写入记忆，从而把错误局部化并使agent可通过“拒绝”而非资源耗尽终止。试点实现了接受层和单元层的验证器对，结果显示验证能显著改进终止行为，确定性门在零边际成本下完成大部分修正；同时，规划模块在有验证时反而降低了表现。试验规模小且只考察终止行为，作者计划在十二个月内完成完整架构并补上集成层验证器。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents built on the ReAct pattern concentrate four responsibilities in one model: selecting a strategy, choosing each action, formatting it, and judging whether the result is adequate. Nothing outside the generative loop can reject its output, so an agent that cannot make progress does not report failure; it runs until an external budget stops it. We propose treating verification as an architectural layer by adapting the V-model from software engineering: specification levels descend from requirements to individual steps, each level is paired with a dedicated verifier, a deterministic controller enforces every verdict, and only verification outcomes write to memory, so a rejection localizes the level that introduced the fault and an agent halts by declining rather than by exhaustion. Each verifier separates a zero-cost deterministic \emph{gate} from an optional LLM \emph{judge}, so the contribution and cost of each can be measured independently. We report a pilot implementing the acceptance- and unit-level verifier pairs, comparing five configurations that share one executor, tool set, and scorer and differ only in verification, on the four-hop stratum of MuSiQue with an 8B-parameter backbone. Across 47 executions, the two unverified configurations answered none of ten questions, every run ending at a step cap or provider token limit; the verified configuration without a planner answered eight and abstained on the rest. Deterministic gates produced eight of the nine observed corrections at zero marginal cost, and planning degraded performance once verification was present. These results characterize termination behavior, not accuracy at scale; we outline a twelve-month plan to complete and evaluate the full architecture, including the integration-level pair the pilot omits.

</details>


### [179] [Methodological Harness in Agentic Software Engineering: An Empirical Study on Mining Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32014)
*Jessica Díaz,Jorge Pérez,Sergio Gil-Borrás*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Framework mechanisms appear in practice, but mainly individually; integrated harnesses are uncommon.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To empirically validate, at repository scale, a proposed eight-mechanism framework for coordinating agentic software engineering.

Method: From AIDev dataset (116,211 repos) the authors stratified by visibility (stars) and analyzed 5,435 repos. For RQ1 they detected and dated artifacts representing seven observable mechanisms. For RQ2 they qualitatively coded rule files from 150 repos.

Result: 21.7% of the studied population had at least one mechanism; 65.3% prevalence among the most visible repos. Multi-mechanism configurations were rare. Rule files usually guide agents and state norms; other mechanisms (persistent shared knowledge, executable specs, structured consultation, graduated autonomy) were less common.

Conclusion: The proposed methodological harness is empirically observable in GitHub repositories, but typically as isolated mechanisms rather than as an integrated system.

Abstract: Context. Agentic software engineering requires mechanisms to coordinate and govern agents' work. The framework motivating this study proposes a methodological harness with eight mechanisms: context engineering, persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, N-version mindset and parallel agents, normative specifications, structured consultation, evidence-based acceptance, and graduated autonomy. These are realized through artifacts such as rule or context files, specifications, and architectural decision records, but the framework had not been empirically validated at repository scale. Objective. We analyze to what extent and how this harness is observable in repositories with agentic activity. RQ1 characterizes adoption through artifact prevalence, breadth, co-occurrence, and temporal evolution; RQ2 examines rule files - a key observable artifact and persistent source of agent instructions - to assess how their content reflects the proposed mechanisms. Method. From the AIDev dataset of 116,211 GitHub repositories, we analyzed 5,435 using a design stratified by visibility, measured through stars as an indicator of popularity and/or reputation. For RQ1, we detected and quantified artifacts and introduction dates for the seven observable mechanisms. For RQ2, we qualitatively coded rule files from 150 repositories. Results. Population prevalence of at least one mechanism is 21.7%, versus 65.3% among the most visible repositories. Multi-mechanism configurations are rare. Where rule files exist, they almost always guide the agent and state norms, while persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, structured consultation, and graduated autonomy appear only in a minority. Conclusions. The harness is empirically observable, but mainly through isolated mechanisms rather than the integrated system proposed by the framework.

</details>


### [180] [CG-Diff: Organizing Code Changes Around Call Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32057)
*Bimal Raj Gyawali,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Nadia Polikarpova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将PR按调用图子图组织（CG-Diffs）可以在许多变更场景下比按文件展示更利于理解与导航，但需解决节点重复和非函数更改可见性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统按文件展示的代码审查视图在跨文件调用关系密集的变更场景下难以帮助审查者理解控制流与影响范围，尤其是审查不熟悉的代码或LLM生成的补丁时。

Method: 对GitHub真实PR做经验性分析以量化变更函数之间的调用连接性；基于调用图将变更分解成子图（CG-Diffs）；实现一个基于Web的PR可视化界面；通过组内被试实验将该界面与GitHub默认PR视图进行比较。

Result: 研究发现约40%的PR中超过半数被改动的函数在调用图上是连通的；CG-Diffs在用户研究中提升了参与者的定向感与更有意义的导航结构；但也暴露出函数节点在多个CG-Diff中重复导致的迷惑，以及全局变量/导入等非函数性更改不够显眼的问题。

Conclusion: 基于调用图的CG-Diffs能有效将PR中的相关代码更有意义地组织起来，改进审查者的定向与导航，但在节点重复和非函数性更改的可见性方面存在局限。

Abstract: Tools for code review present code changes file-by-file. We argue that, oftentimes, changes can be better organized around a call graph of the changed code. From an empirical study of GitHub pull requests (PRs) in-the-wild, we find that (1) in around 40% of the PRs, more than half of the changed functions (and methods) are connected by call graphs, and (2) necessary callees are often located in different files from their callers. Based on these findings, we develop the notion of CG-Diffs, subgraphs derived by decomposing a call graph of the changed code into smaller and more navigable directed graphs. We then implement a web-based interface for viewing PRs that restructures the PR around these CG-Diffs. Through a within-subject study comparing our interface against GitHub's PR view, we find that CG-Diffs help orient participants and provide them with more meaningful structures to navigate the PR. We also found several limitations: function nodes repeated across multiple CG-Diffs can be disorienting, and changes not contained in a function (e.g. globals, imports) are not as immediately apparent. Our study shows promise in using call graphs to help contextualize and navigate unfamiliar codebases, which may benefit new contributors to open source, and reviewers of unfamiliar LLM-generated PRs.

</details>


### [181] [IChart2Code: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Interactive Chart Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32413)
*Xu Zhang,Hongzhang Zheng,Zhili Huang,Yaoyi Wang,Ling Xu,Sheng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了面向交互式图表代码生成的基准IChart2Code和基于浏览器的评估协议，并用轨迹引导的双代理TRAIL显著提升生成与修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表到代码基准只关注静态输出，缺乏对交互规范、浏览器执行与交互后验证的任务表示和评估协议，因而无法衡量模型在交互式场景下的实际可用性与正确性。

Method: 构建IChart2Code数据集（377任务、20种图表形式、13类数据、1209项交互需求），为每个任务提供参考截图、任务本地数据、自然语言交互要求和可执行HTML/JS目标代码；设计基于浏览器的评估协议（可执行性门槛 + 数据保真、静态视觉正确性、交互正确性三维评分），并训练/使用一个基于Rubric的多模态LLM裁判来对浏览器观测结果进行评分；提出TRAIL双代理框架：Inspector生成检查项并在浏览器执行以产生轨迹，Diagnoser根据轨迹诊断失败并生成结构化修复反馈供代码生成模型迭代。

Result: IChart2Code包含377个任务与1209项交互需求；Rubric-guided MLLM裁判在任务级别上对人类裁判的F1为0.8844；TRAIL在四个评估维度上相对于直接提示平均提升了7.89、4.98、3.45和4.47个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出了一个用于交互式图表代码生成的基准和评估流程，并设计了一个轨迹引导的双代理修复框架，展示了在四个大模型上的实证改进。

Abstract: Interactive chart code generation requires models to reproduce a reference chart's appearance and underlying data and correctly implement the state changes triggered by specified user interactions. Existing chart-to-code benchmarks focus on static outputs and lack task representations or evaluation protocols for interaction specification, browser execution, and post-interaction verification. We introduce IChart2Code, a benchmark comprising 377 tasks across 20 chart forms and 13 data families, with 1209 interaction requirements in six families. Each task provides a reference screenshot, task-local data, and natural-language interaction requirements, with executable HTML/JavaScript code as the target output. We further develop a browser-based evaluation protocol with an Executability gate and three rubric-guided dimensions: Data Fidelity, Static Visual Correctness, and Interaction Correctness. The protocol tests runtime viability, consistency with task-local data, fidelity of the initial rendering to the reference screenshot, and interaction-induced state changes in a sandboxed browser. A rubric-guided MLLM judge evaluates task-specific items for the three scored dimensions using the collected browser observations and achieves an overall item-level F1 score of 0.8844 against adjudicated human labels. We also propose TRAIL, a trajectory-guided dual-agent framework for interactive chart code generation. An Inspector derives task-specific inspection checks, executes them in the browser, and uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures and produce structured repair feedback. Averaged across four MLLMs, TRAIL improves the four evaluation dimensions over direct prompting by 7.89, 4.98, 3.45, and 4.47 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [182] [How AI Changes DevOps Performance: A Mechanism-Based Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32414)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将 AI 区分为生成代码和代理式两类，发现二者对 DORA 指标影响截然不同且受团队能力与管线容量调节，建议基于队列/审查容量与审计策略来测量与治理 AI 影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实证关于 AI 对软件交付（DORA 指标）影响的结论混杂、横截面居多，且常将 AI 生成代码与代理式 AI 混为一谈；需明确两类 AI 的独立与联合效应及能力如何调节这些效应并提供可操作的测量与治理建议。

Method: 对现有证据进行审计，构建二构念（AGC vs AGT）与四个能力调节因子的形式化模型，并实现离散事件仿真管线。设计六个实验：因子设计、Shapley 分解、针对 600 个参数抽样的敏感性分析、固定需求的稳健性测试，以及包含 5,000 支模拟团队并带有反事实的滞后采用面板模拟。

Result: AGC 提高失败率、返工与恢复时间；当 AI 增加变更量且代码审查容量充足时，AGC 可将前置时间缩短约 13%，但审查饱和时则延长。低能力团队的部署频率提升完全来自未计划返工。AGT 提升部署频率（+31% 至 +34%）、缩短前置时间（-8% 至 -22%）与恢复时间（-29% 至 -38%），但其稳定性效应依赖于自动化 CI 修复掩盖缺陷，且在高能力团队中外逃缺陷增加。能力能降低 AGC 的绝对失败率惩罚（7.9 vs. 1.5 个百分点）但相对惩罚几乎不变（+34% vs. +84%）。两向固定效应低估采用效应 8–16%；检测稳定性效应约需 50 个团队，检测能力调节约需 400 个团队。

Conclusion: 在模型范围内，DORA 指标对 AI 的响应主要通过排队、分批和监督机制发生，而不只是代码质量本身。AI 生成代码（AGC）普遍提高失败率、返工率和恢复时间；代理式 AI（AGT）能显著提高部署频率、缩短交付前置时间和恢复时间，但会引入由自动化修复掩盖的稳定性风险且在高能力团队可能增加外逃缺陷。组织能力能够减轻 AGC 的绝对失败率惩罚，但对相对惩罚帮助有限。两向固定效应估计会低估采用效应，且检测稳定性与能力调节效应需要较大样本。研究提出了测量准则、代理化修复的防护措施和一套基于样本量的实地验证协议。

Abstract: Context. AI tools for software development have progressed from code completion to autonomous agents, yet evidence on their effects on DORA performance is mixed, mostly cross-sectional, and often conflates AI-generated code (AGC) with agentic AI (AGT). Objective. We examine how AGC and AGT, separately and jointly, affect deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, recovery time, and deployment rework rate. Method. We audit the evidence, formalise a two-construct model with four capability moderators, and implement a discrete-event simulation of a delivery pipeline. Six experiments include a factorial design, Shapley decompositions, sensitivity analysis over 600 parameter draws, a fixed-demand robustness test, and a staggered-adoption panel of 5,000 simulated teams with counterfactuals. Results. AGC increased failure rate, rework, and recovery time in both capability profiles. When AI increased change volume, AGC shortened lead time only where review capacity was spare (-13%) and lengthened it once review saturated; in the low-capability team, its deployment-frequency gain came entirely from unplanned rework. AGT improved deployment frequency (+31% to +34%), lead time (-8% to -22%), and recovery time (-29% to -38%), but its stability effect depended on autonomous CI repair masking defects, and escaped defects increased in the high-capability team. Capability reduced AGC's absolute failure-rate penalty (7.9 vs. 1.5 points) but not its relative penalty (+34% vs. +84%). Two-way fixed effects underestimated adoption effects by 8-16%; detecting the modelled stability effect required about 50 teams, and its capability moderation about 400. Conclusions. Within the model, DORA metrics respond to AI through queueing, batching, and oversight, not only code quality. We derive measurement rules, guardrails for agentic remediation, and a sample-size-informed field-validation protocol.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond the Model: Demystifying Harness Effects in Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32459)
*Haichuan Hu,Quanjun Zhang,Shengcheng Yu,Zhifei Chen,Tianyu Luo,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: harness设计是影响SE-agent性能的一流因素；通过模块化轻量harness并选择合适组件，可以在效率与效果间取得良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM基础模型重要，但harness——即代理如何与代码仓库交互、执行动作与验证解法——在SE任务中同样关键，而其设计影响在不同模型、任务与组件层面尚未被充分理解。

Method: 系统性实证研究：对比评估了两种代表性harness（mini-SWE-agent与OpenCode），在三套基准（SWE-bench Pro、ProgramBench、GitTaskBench）上使用来自Qwen与DeepSeek两大开源模型家族的10个模型进行测试；基于mini-SWE-agent构建轻量模块化NanoHarness，并对五个关键组件（工具注册表、上下文压缩、显式规划、子代理与延迟技能）进行消融与分析。

Result: 实验显示：1）harness效果依赖于模型能力与任务复杂度；2）对于SWE风格的问题修复，复杂harness对高能力模型边际收益递减；3）在复杂、开放的仓库级任务上，强模型仍可从复杂harness受益；4）结构化工具使用与任务专属子代理带来最稳定的提升，而上下文压缩与通用子代理在仓库生成任务上可能有害；5）NanoHarness相较mini-SWE-agent在Qwen3.7-Max与DeepSeek-V4-Pro上分别提升约7.37与6.21百分点，接近产品级harness的性能。

Conclusion: Harness设计显著影响SE-agent的表现；其有效性由模型能力与任务类型共同决定；组件级选择（如结构化工具使用与任务专属子代理）会带来稳定提升；轻量化的NanoHarness能在大部分情况下恢复产品级harness的大部分收益。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, yet their performance is not determined by the base model alone. The agent harness substantially shapes how SE agents interact with repositories, execute actions, and validate solutions. However, the role of harness design remains insufficiently understood, especially across different models, tasks, and harness components. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of harness effects in SE agents. We first evaluate two representative harnesses, mini-SWE-agent and OpenCode, with ten models from two prominent open-weight model families, Qwen and DeepSeek, on three benchmarks: SWE-bench Pro, ProgramBench, and GitTaskBench. We then construct NanoHarness, a lightweight modular harness built on top of mini-SWE-agent, and use it to analyze five representative harness components: tool registry, context compression, explicit planning, subagents, and lazy skills. Experimental results show that harness effectiveness depends jointly on model capability and task type. Complex harnesses provide diminishing marginal gains on SWE-style issue repair as model capability improves, but can benefit stronger models on more complex and open-ended repository-level tasks. Component-level analysis on ProgramBench further shows that structured tool use and task-specific subagents provide the most stable improvements, while context compression and general subagents can hurt repository-generation performance. When combined, NanoHarness improves over mini-SWE-agent by 7.37 and 6.21 percentage points on Qwen3.7-Max and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, respectively, recovering most of the gains of product-level harnesses. These findings highlight harness design as a first-class factor in SE-agent performance and provide insights for building more effective and efficient coding agents.

</details>


### [184] [Evolution of Deprecated APIs and Their Replacements in Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32618)
*Gangqiang He,Guanping Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究揭示了废弃 API→替代 API 关系随粒度、版本与生命周期多维变化的规律，为演化感知的替代推荐与自动化迁移提供实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对废弃、替代识别与客户端迁移多为分立研究，缺乏对废弃 API→替代 API 在多个版本与生命周期维度上如何变化的系统性理解。

Method: 从 33 个 Python 库中收集 830 个由维护者指定的废弃→替代映射（类/函数/方法），比较定义差异、在两种迁移场景下基于相似度的候选排名随版本变化、并通过源码检查与逐版本执行识别生命周期事件。

Result: 同模块替代占比：类 39.1%、函数 55.6%、方法 79.9%；大多数映射参数不变，但函数参数变化更频繁且多样；不同版本与代码表示下排名波动明显，仅 ~60% 情况总体趋势一致；关键版本中 49.0% 存在显著的其他候选影响；废弃后 56.4% 保留源定义，68.6% 保留可执行原始调用；753 个有终结事件的映射中 25.2% 为先源或先调用而非同版本结束，且其中 77.4% 为先源定义结束。

Conclusion: 废弃 API 与替代 API 的关系受 API 粒度、发布上下文、代码表示、候选竞争和生命周期状态影响，理解这些因素可改善面向演化的替代 API 推荐与自动迁移。

Abstract: Context: API deprecation is common in library evolution, but migration requires finding replacements and adapting calls. Prior studies examine deprecation, replacement identification, and client migration separately, leaving deprecated API-replacement API relationships across releases and lifecycle states insufficiently understood. Objective: We examine replacement locality, parameter-interface differences, changes in replacement rankings across versions, and post-deprecation lifecycles from source-definition and original-invocation perspectives. Method: We construct 830 maintainer-specified mappings from 33 Python libraries, covering classes, functions, and methods. We compare definitions, track similarity-based rankings under two migration scenarios, and identify lifecycle events through source inspection and release-by-release execution. Results: Same-module replacements account for 39.1%, 55.6%, and 79.9% of class, function, and method mappings, respectively. Most mappings have no parameter changes, but function mappings show more frequent and diverse changes. Rankings vary across releases and code representations, and the applicable methods agree on the overall trend in only about 60% of cases. Other-candidate effects are dominant or mixed in 49.0% of key versions. After deprecation, 56.4% of mappings retain the source definition and 68.6% retain an executable original invocation. Among 753 mappings with at least one observed ending event, 25.2% are source-first or invocation-first rather than same-release. Of these cases, 77.4% are source-first. Conclusions: Deprecated API-replacement API relationships depend on API granularity, release context, code representation, candidate competition, and lifecycle state. These results support evolution-aware replacement API recommendation and automated API migration.

</details>


### [185] [AsynCodeBench: Benchmarking Collaboration of Asynchronous Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32662)
*Kaituo Zhang,Zhen Xiong,Zhimeng Jiang,Mingyu Zhong,Zhouyuan Yuan,Zhecheng Li,Bowen Lin,Chia-Yuan Chang,Mingzhi Hu,Huazheng Wang,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AsynCodeBench，一个以依赖为中心的异步多智能体软件工程基准，包含显式依赖图与可执行检查器，并引入ADPR与DRS两个协作度量；实验显示编码性能提升未必带来协作提升，任务级指标可能掩盖协作缺陷，且协作常呈“跳跃窗口”集中爆发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multi-agent coding evaluations rely on task-level outcomes from single-agent settings, conflating individual coding ability with inter-agent coordination; a direct, dependency-focused measure of collaboration is needed.

Method: Constructed a dependency-centric benchmark of 19 real-world tasks with explicit dependency graphs and executable Dependency Checkers; introduced two metrics (Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate and Dependency Resolution Step); ran experiments across model families, scales, and generations and performed dependency-trajectory analysis.

Result: Found that improvements in coding accuracy do not reliably improve collaboration as measured by ADPR and DRS; task-level metrics can diverge significantly from dependency-level measures; identified a hopping window pattern where many dependencies resolve in short execution bursts.

Conclusion: AsynCodeBench demonstrates that task-level success masks cross-agent coordination failures: there is a measurable gap between coding performance and collaboration, and coordination often occurs in concentrated bursts (hopping windows).

Abstract: Multi-agent coding has emerged as an increasingly active direction in software engineering, where complex development tasks are decomposed across multiple specialized agents working on different parts of the problem. Despite the shift from individual problem solving to distributed collaboration, multi-agent systems still lack a direct measure of collaboration and are largely evaluated through task-level outcomes inherited from single-agent coding, conflating individual coding capability with cross-agent coordination. We introduce AsynCodeBench, a dependency-centric benchmark for asynchronous multi-agent software engineering that represents each task with an explicit dependency graph and executable Dependency Checkers. Through this dependency-tracking process, we propose two complementary measures: Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate (ADPR), which measures how many cross-agent dependencies are ultimately satisfied, and Dependency Resolution Step (DRS), which measures when each dependency first becomes satisfied during execution. AsynCodeBench comprises 19 tasks from real-world repositories, exposing 52 directed dependencies as explicit units for evaluating cross-agent collaboration. Experiments across model families, scales, and generations reveal a clear gap between coding and collaboration capability: improvements in coding performance do not necessarily translate into stronger collaboration, and task-level metrics can diverge substantially from dependency-level collaboration measures. Dependency-trajectory analysis further reveals that successful coordination often emerges not gradually, but through concentrated bursts in which many dependencies become resolved over a short portion of the execution trajectory, a pattern we term a hopping window.

</details>


### [186] [Social3Source: Connecting Social Provenance, Societal Qualities, and Social Purpose on an Open-Source Software Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32823)
*Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Social3Source——将社会价值纳入开源软件开发的概念框架与原则，强调从设计到发布全流程负责社会影响，当前为概念性和初步实施讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件产量空前增长，但很多软件未必对社会有益；论文以“软件应改善人类生活与社会”为出发点，呼吁超越仅仅开源代码的考量。

Method: 采用概念性阐述：定义概念、提出原则，并进行初步实现讨论与示例性说明，展示如何将社会性考量整合进开源软件流程。

Result: 给出一套概念框架与原则，并提供初步实现思路与示例性讨论，尚无系统的实证评估或量化结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出Social3Source概念，主张在开源软件开发过程中系统地纳入社会性考量，以确保软件对人类和社会产生积极影响。

Abstract: The speed at which software can be created and deployed today has increased dramatically. But what is the point of all this software if it does not contribute to society? The ultimate goal of building software should be bettering human lives and society. And this goes beyond publishing the software as open source; it requires considering how it is developed, the societal qualities it embodies, and the purposes it serves. This is what I call Social3Source. This paper presents the concept, principles, and initial discussion for the implementation of Social3Source, illustrating how it integrates social considerations into open-source software development. We have never generated as much software as we do today. Let's make sure it counts.

</details>


### [187] [What Does a Skill Actually Do? Estimands and Evaluation Validity for Tool and Skill Use in LLM Agents: A Critical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33153)
*Shuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过估计量视角将LLM代理的工具/技能评估划分为六个要素，指出常见指标的误读风险，提供分类、反例与报告清单来提升结论的明确性与可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于当前关于检索器、路由器或技能库“改善”代理性能的报告存在多重含义，容易混淆不同比较目标与假设；需要一个统一、明确的评估语言来理解不同设计在问什么、在假设什么。

Method: 采用文献批判性综述与估计量（estimand）框架：将工具/技能设计沿六个维度（处理对比、目标人群、结局、预算约束、汇总量、识别假设）展开；以13项核心实证研究为证据支点，辅以方法学文献、分析反例与数值玩具模型。无新实验，结果来自被引研究与理论推导。

Result: 提出并系统化了六轴分类、对比了多种常见研究设计（如策划技能 vs 检索替换、触发子集 vs 全任务结局、观察到的成本降低 vs 预算约束比较），给出配对条件化配对分解、分析反例和数值示例，最后给出一份报告核对表与示例以指导未来研究的可比性与透明度。

Conclusion: 论文的主要结论是：对LLM代理使用外部工具或技能库时，报告“改进”必须明确比较对象与评估估计量。不同设计（如配对触发任务、全部任务比较、预算约束下的效率评估等）回答不同问题，且某些常用指标（如配对增益、回归计数）并不直接识别调用效应或代表任务期望结果的恶化比例。作者提供了基于估计量的六轴分类并给出反例与示例以澄清误读。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly draw on external tools and reusable skills selected at run time from libraries that hold thousands of entries. Reports that a retriever, router, or skill library "improves" an agent may refer to retrieval recall, the success change from enabling a library, a paired contrast restricted to triggered tasks, or a gain under an approximate budget constraint. This critical review asks what each design compares and under which assumptions.
